# SPEC -- Allen dendrite-diameter re-measurement (`allen_diameter`)

Single source of truth for what the code must do. The code follows this file;
when the two disagree, fix whichever is wrong and record why in the decision
log (`TEEG_decisions_and_ideas_log.md`, project knowledge; entries D-018 to
D-024, D-030, D-031, D-052 to D-054, D-057 to D-065, D-067 to D-071, D-084 to D-090, D-092 to D-094 and D-096 bind this spec (D-095 is the noise-fit chat's) (D-066
withdrawn by D-067); ID note in section 4). The integration tester reads only the repository, so
every constraint the code must satisfy is stated here.

Notation rules: every symbol is defined at first use; a conditional quantity
keeps its conditioning every time it appears; quantifiers are stated.

Method documents (decision D-020; cited below as "handoff Eq. n", "procedure
Eq. (n) / s.n", "mathematics Eq. (n) / s.n", "impl-handoff (S n)"):
`Passive Features/Diameter Re-measurement/docs/handoff_diameter_remeasurement.md`,
`.../TEEG_diameter_bias_table_procedure_2026-10-04.md`,
`.../TEEG_diameter_bias_table_mathematics_2026-10-04.md`,
`.../TEEG_diameter_implementation_handoff_2026-10-06.md`.

## 0. Coverage

Paths this spec covers:

- `Passive Features/Diameter Re-measurement/src/`
- `Passive Features/Diameter Re-measurement/scripts/`
- `Passive Features/Diameter Re-measurement/tests/`

Everything else in the repository is not specified here and is out of scope
for the tester. The four flat modules `src/allen_image_*.py` and the two
suites `tests/smoke/smoke_allen_image.py`, `tests/smoke/robustness_registration.py`
are the 2026-09-23 image-access code imported byte-identical (Block 0); their
entries are `Confirmed: inferred` and their own suites are their tests.

## 1. Scientific goal

Run B of the passive fit gives L2/L3 membrane capacitance 2-3 times the
reference value; the working hypothesis is that the Allen SWC radii are too
thin. This code re-measures dendrite diameters from Allen's 63x brightfield
image stacks (specimen 529878215 first, D7) and writes corrected SWC files in
the archive's own format (D-013) for the Cm refit.

Deliverables, per cell: a per-node CSV (identity, registration, focus and
path, background, fit, correction, flags, final diameter; columns in
Block 8), a corrected SWC with radius = d_final / 2 on dendrite nodes (types
3, 4) and everything else byte-identical (D-013), and the dendritic
membrane-area ratio against Allen's radii. Per configuration: a bias table
(Block 6) with its configuration dictionary C. Consumers: the user; the I_h
fitter (`Passive Features/HPC script/Ih Fit/run_ih_fit.py --swc-dir`).

## 2. Mathematical formulation

### 2.1 Frames and objects

Global image frame: $(x, y)$ in um along pixel columns and rows
(pitch $p_{\rm x} = 0.1144$ um), $z$ along the optical axis in stage units
(plane spacing $\Delta z = 0.28$ um; plane index $k$; depth $z_k$). A dendrite
node $i$ has SWC position $p_i \in \mathbb{R}^3$ (um) and refined centre
$c_i \in \mathbb{R}^3$. The branch near node $i$ is a round straight tube of
diameter $d$ (um), direction $\hat t_i = (\cos\varphi_i\cos\theta_i,
\cos\varphi_i\sin\theta_i, \sin\varphi_i)$ with heading
$\theta_i = \operatorname{atan2}(t_y, t_x) \in (-\pi, \pi]$ and tilt
$\varphi_i = \arcsin|t_z| \in [0, \pi/2]$; measuring axis
$\hat y_i = (-\sin\theta_i, \cos\theta_i, 0)$ (handoff Eq. 5).

### 2.2 Per-node measurement (Blocks 3, 5; handoff Eqs. 1-5, 9; D-018; D-019)

Focus score (D-030, 2026-10-07; replaces handoff Eq. 1, kept as the labelled
comparison (1')). For node $j$ with radius $r_j$ (um; Allen's radius on a real
node, the true radius on a phantom) and plane $k$, let $\tilde I_{j,k}(v)$
(grey levels) be the raw profile along $\hat y_j$ through $p_{j,\parallel}$
at offsets $v$ (um) smoothed by Gaussian weights of $s$ = `focus_smooth_px`
pixels, and $B_{j,k}$ (grey levels) the median of the profile ends
$|v| > 1.5$ um (D-023, `focus_bg_rule = "profile_ends_median"`). The
gradient energy of the plane is

$$G_{j,k} = \frac{1}{B_{j,k}^2}\int_{|v| \le h_j}\Big(\frac{\partial\tilde I_{j,k}}{\partial v}(v)\Big)^2\,dv \quad (\mathrm{um}^{-1}), \qquad k^*_j = \operatorname*{arg\,max}_k G_{j,k}, \tag{1}$$

with $h_j = r_j + m_G$, $m_G$ = `focus_grad_margin_um` (`focus_grad_window =
"radius_margin"`, PROVISIONAL), or $h_j$ = the whole profile
(`"whole_profile"`). The comparison `focus_rule = "dip_depth"` is handoff
Eq. 1 as written,

$$F_{j,k} = -\ln\big(\min_v \tilde I_{j,k}(v) / B_{j,k}\big), \tag{1'}$$

and every node records it beside $G$ (`focus_depth`, `k_star_depth`). For a
noise-free Gaussian dip $\tilde I/B = 1 - D\,e^{-v^2/(2w^2)}$ with depth
$D \in (0, 1)$ and width $w > 0$ (um), (1) over the whole line equals
$\sqrt\pi\,D^2/(2w)$ and over $|v| \le h$ equals
$(D^2/w)\big[\tfrac{\sqrt\pi}{2}\operatorname{erf}(h/w) - (h/w)\,e^{-h^2/w^2}\big]$;
at a fixed dip area $A = \sqrt{2\pi}\,D\,w$ (um, conserved through focus
for a faint isolated process, D-019 (a)) it equals $A^2/(4\sqrt\pi\,w^3)$, so
depth and narrowness enter with no weight to choose. These are properties of
the continuous dip (analytic level); the code evaluates (1) on the sampled
profile (computed level, Block 5).

Sub-plane depth (handoff Eq. 2; on by default), with $S$ the configured score
($G$ by default, $F$ under `dip_depth`), $S_\mp = S_{j,k^*_j \mp 1}$,
$S_0 = S_{j,k^*_j}$: $z_j = z_{k^*_j} + \Delta z\,(S_- - S_+)/[2(S_- - 2S_0 + S_+)]$,
clipped to $z_{k^*_j} \pm \Delta z/2$; the middle of the plateau when $S$ is
flat-topped.

Centre: $c_j = (p_{j,\parallel} + \hat v_{0,j}(-\sin\theta_j, \cos\theta_j),\ z_j)$
with $\hat v_{0,j}$ from the fit (3) in plane $k^*_j$ (handoff Eq. 3).

Direction for node $i$: total-least-squares line through the centres
$c_j$ with $|s_j - s_i| \le L/2$ ($s$ = path length, $L$ = `line_fit_window_um`),
in um in the global frame (D6: never in pixel/plane units):
$\hat t_i$ = leading eigenvector of $\frac{1}{|W_i|}\sum_{j \in W_i}(c_j - \bar c_i)(c_j - \bar c_i)^\top$,
oriented away from the soma (handoff Eq. 4). The first pass uses the raw
SWC direction; one redraw with the fitted direction (`direction_redraws`).

Profile (handoff Eq. 9): $I_i(v_n) = I_{k^*_i}(c_{i,\parallel} + v_n \hat y_i)$,
$v_n \in [-3, 3]$ um in steps of $p_{\rm x}$, bilinear interpolation,
averaged over $\pm$`along_branch_avg_um` along $\hat e_u$ when enabled.

Background (D-018.1, D-023): $\bar B_i$ = median of the node's block in
plane $k^*_i$ with every pixel within (Allen radius + `bbar_mask_margin_um`)
of any SWC segment in the block masked out; phantoms mask the same way
around their known axis.

Fit (D-019.1; $\bar B_i$ fixed, $\sigma_{\rm fit}$ fixed, $\varphi_i$ from the
line fit), with $s_d(u) = \sqrt{1 - (2u/d)^2}$ for $|u| \le d/2$, else 0, and
$g_\sigma$ the 1-D Gaussian of standard deviation $\sigma$ (unit integral):

$$T_{d,\mu,v_0}(v \mid \varphi_i) = \exp\!\Big(-\frac{\mu\,d}{\cos\varphi_i}\,s_d(v - v_0)\Big), \qquad (\hat d_i, \hat\mu_i, \hat v_{0,i}) = \operatorname*{arg\,min}_{d, \mu, v_0} \sum_n \Big[I_i(v_n) - \bar B_i\,\big(T_{d,\mu,v_0}(\cdot \mid \varphi_i) * g_{\sigma_{\rm fit}}\big)(v_n)\Big]^2 \tag{3}$$

over the bounds of `MeasureConfig` (fit_d_bounds_um, fit_mu_bounds_per_um,
fit_v0_bounds_um), `scipy.optimize.least_squares`, multi-start at
$d_0 \times$ `fit_multistart_factors`. Derived: $\hat\alpha_i = \hat\mu_i \hat d_i / \cos\varphi_i$
(diagnostics only). With $\mu$ free per node (D-023) the fit has the same
minimiser in $(d, v_0)$ as the $\alpha$-parameterised D-018.2 (regression
guard, Block 3).

Selection $\mathcal S$ (procedure s.3.2; D-024), applied identically to
phantoms and real nodes: converged, no parameter at a bound, registration
verdict on the branch (real data only), not `faint`, no second dip
(`crossing`), not `stack_edge`, $\hat\alpha_i \le$ `alpha_dark_flag`, a sound
$\bar B_i$ (not `bbar_few`) and a complete profile (not `profile_nan`)
[the last two added 2026-10-06, Block 6, PROVISIONAL].
**Tilt is never a selection criterion** (D-024); `steep`
($\tan\varphi_i \ge$ `steep_tan_diagnostic`) and `vertical`
($\varphi_i >$ `phi_vertical_deg`) are reported columns.

Fit at a plane, D-052 (Block 12, 2026-10-18; it becomes the node's final fit
when Block 13 wires it into the chain, (3) staying the pilot's fit at
$k^*_j$). For node $j$ and plane $k$, with $I_j(v_n; k)$ the profile and
$\bar B_j(k)$ the background of that plane, $\varphi_j$ the pilot's tilt,
$\theta = (d, \mu, v_0, w, \beta, \xi, \zeta)$ and
$\alpha = \mu d/\cos\varphi_j$, for every $v$ (um) and every weight vector
$\omega = (\omega_1, \ldots, \omega_N)$:

$$I_{\rm model}(v \mid \theta;\, \varphi_j, \bar B_j(k)) = \bar B_j(k)\Big[1 - \frac{d}{2}\int_{-\pi/2}^{\pi/2}\big(1 - e^{-\alpha\cos\psi}\big)\cos\psi\; g_{w,\kappa}\big(v - v_0 - \tfrac{d}{2}\sin\psi\big)\,d\psi\Big], \qquad Q_j(\theta \mid k, \omega) = \frac12\sum_{n=1}^{N}\omega_n\Big[\frac{I_{\rm model}(v_n \mid \theta;\, \varphi_j, \bar B_j(k)) - I_j(v_n; k)}{\bar B_j(k)}\Big]^2, \tag{3''}$$

$g_{w,\kappa}$ the skewed generalized-$t$ kernel of FWHM $w$ (um) and shape
$\kappa = (\beta, \xi, \zeta)$ (Block 12, (H1)-(H3)). The unweighted fit is
$\hat\theta^{(0)}_j(k) = \arg\min_{\theta \in \Theta} Q_j(\theta \mid k, \mathbf 1)$;
then $\hat\theta^{(t+1)}_j(k) = \arg\min_{\theta \in \Theta} Q_j(\theta \mid k, \omega^{(t)})$
with $\omega^{(t)}_n = \exp(-|v_n - \hat v_0^{(t)}|/\lambda)$, $\hat v_0^{(t)}$
the centre of iterate $t$ and $\lambda$ = `sgt_lambda_um`, until $d$ and $v_0$
both move by at most `sgt_irls_tol_um`, after at most `sgt_irls_max_iter`
weighted solves. The node's fit at plane $k$ is the last iterate
$\hat\theta_j(k)$, with $\hat\alpha_j = \hat\mu_j\hat d_j/\cos\varphi_j$. The
box $\Theta$, the starts and the status rule (D-053 (ii): only $d$, $\mu$,
$v_0$ or $w$ at a bound, or an unconverged IRLS, keep a fit out of
$\mathcal S$) are in Block 12.

### 2.3 Phantom geometry and rendering (Blocks 2, 4; impl-handoff (S1)-(S5); procedure Eqs. 5-6)

For a tube of radius $r = d/2$, tilt $\varphi$, heading $\theta$, node point
$c$, and every $(x, y, z)$: $u = (x - c_x)\cos\theta + (y - c_y)\sin\theta$,
$v = -(x - c_x)\sin\theta + (y - c_y)\cos\theta$, $w = z - c_z$ (S1);
membership $v^2 + (w\cos\varphi - u\sin\varphi)^2 \le r^2$ (S2). The
vertical line through $(x, y)$ is inside the tube iff $|v| \le r$ (and
inside the end cut at half-length $U$ = `U_um`: $|u| \le U$ for
`end_cut = "vertical"`, the axial arc length $|u\cos\varphi + w\sin\varphi| \le U$
for `"axial"`; Block 2) and
$z \in [z_{\rm lo}, z_{\rm hi}]$, $z_{\rm lo/hi} = c_z + u\tan\varphi \mp \ell(v)/2$,
$\ell(v) = 2\sqrt{r^2 - v^2}/\cos\varphi$ (S3). These are the round-section
forms ($k$ = `cross_section_aspect` = 1); Block 2 states the squashed-section
generalisation $k \ne 1$. Slab $j$ spans
$[\zeta_j - \delta\zeta/2, \zeta_j + \delta\zeta/2]$; its absorbance along the
vertical ray and the light reaching it (slabs numbered in the light's
direction, `light_direction`):

$$a_j(x, y) = \mu\max\{0, \min(z_{\rm hi}, \zeta_j + \tfrac{\delta\zeta}{2}) - \max(z_{\rm lo}, \zeta_j - \tfrac{\delta\zeta}{2})\}, \qquad T_{<j}(x, y) = \exp\!\big(-\mu\max\{0, \min(z_{\rm hi}, \zeta_j - \tfrac{\delta\zeta}{2}) - z_{\rm lo}\}\big) \tag{S4}$$

(both 0 resp. 1 for $|v| > r$; $\sum_j a_j = \mu\ell(v)$ when the slabs tile
the column). Absorbed-light partition and one output plane $k$ with a
circular Gaussian kernel $G_{\sigma_{\rm r}(\delta)}$, $\delta = \zeta_j - z_k$:

$$\Delta A_j = T_{<j}\,(1 - e^{-a_j}), \qquad I_k(x, y) = B\Big[1 - \sum_j \big(\Delta A_j * G_{\sigma_{\rm r}(\zeta_j - z_k)}\big)(x, y)\Big], \tag{5-6}$$

computed as one forward FFT per slab and one inverse FFT per plane on an
array zero-padded to twice the grid (linear, not circular, convolution;
impl-handoff "FFT form"); the `direct` backend is the reference. Kernel
table $\sigma_{\rm r}(\delta)$: linear interpolation of
`kernel_table_(delta|sigma)_um` in $|\delta|$, continued beyond the last
tabulated offset by `kernel_continuation` (`linear`:
$\sigma_{\rm r}(\delta_{\max}) + \gamma(|\delta| - \delta_{\max})$ with
$\gamma$ = `kernel_continuation_slope`; `frozen`; `proportional`:
$\sigma_{\rm r}(\delta_{\max})|\delta|/\delta_{\max}$). Camera chain in this
order (procedure s.3.6 step 6): block-average to $p_{\rm x}$, black level
and gain, Gaussian noise `noise_sd_gl`, 8-bit rounding, JPEG (quality or
Allen's own tables). Alternative absorption treatments by name:
`linear` ($I_k = B[1 - \sum_j a_j * G]$, mathematics Eq. 11) and
`ray_world` (impl-handoff (S6), geometric optics; Block 9).

The hybrid (`hybrid`, Block 21; D-084, D-092; the renderers document of
2026-10-09, s.3.8, Eqs. 13-14, on `main`) [added 2026-10-27]. The
condenser's directions are the unit vectors $\hat s = (s_x, s_y, s_z)$ with
$s_z > 0$ and $(s_x, s_y)$ uniform on the disc of radius
$s_m$ = `ray_na` / `ray_n_oil` -- the measure $W$ of Block 9, by its
quadrature; the light travels along $+\hat s$ when `light_direction` = +1
and along $-\hat s$ when it is -1. For a point $p \in \mathbb R^3$ (um) and
a direction $\hat s$, let $[t_1, t_2]$ be the interval of the line
$p + t\hat s$ inside the tube (Block 2 `line_interval`; $t$ is arc length,
$|\hat s| = 1$; empty when the line misses) and

$$\ell_{\rm back}(p; \hat s) = \big|[t_1, t_2] \cap (-\infty, 0]\big| \ \text{(light +1)}, \qquad \ell_{\rm back}(p; \hat s) = \big|[t_1, t_2] \cap [0, \infty)\big| \ \text{(light -1)},$$

the length (um) inside the tube of the ray that reaches $p$, before it
reaches $p$ ($-t_1$, resp. $t_2$, for $p$ inside the tube). For each fixed
slab $j$ and column $(x, y)$, with $\lambda_j(x, y) = a_j(x, y)/\mu$ the
length of the column inside both the tube and the slab (S4) and
$\bar\zeta_j(x, y) = \tfrac12\big[\max(z_{\rm lo}, \zeta_j - \tfrac{\delta\zeta}{2}) + \min(z_{\rm hi}, \zeta_j + \tfrac{\delta\zeta}{2})\big]$
the midpoint of that part,

$$h(p) = \Big\langle \frac{e^{-\mu\,\ell_{\rm back}(p;\hat s)}}{s_z} \Big\rangle_W, \qquad \Delta A^{\rm hyb}_j(x, y) = a_j(x, y)\, h\big(x, y, \bar\zeta_j(x, y)\big) \tag{H}$$

($\Delta A^{\rm hyb}_j = 0$ where $a_j = 0$); the planes are (5-6) with
$\Delta A^{\rm hyb}_j$ in place of $\Delta A_j$. In words: each ray of the
cone brings to a slab point the light it has left, $e^{-\mu\ell_{\rm back}}$
(that ray's own history), and loses there $\mu$ per unit path, $1/s_z$ of
path per unit depth; the slab books the average over the cone at the column,
and the kernel blurs it as in (5-6) -- the kernel does not know a ray's
direction, so the average comes before the blur (renderers document s.3.8).
(H) is the document's Eq. 13 with the slab's inside part in place of the
whole slab: a midpoint rule in depth, whose relative error along one ray is
about $(\mu\lambda_j/s_z)^2/24$ (at most $10^{-3}$ at $\mu$ = 3/um,
$\delta\zeta$ = 0.02 um, $s_z \ge (1 - s_m^2)^{1/2}$ = 0.383). Faint limit:
$h \to \langle 1/s_z\rangle_W$ = 1.4470 (S10) as $\mu \to 0$, so
$\Delta A^{\rm hyb}_j = 1.447\,a_j + O(\mu^2)$, the `linear` renderer at
$1.447\mu$. Conserved: for each direction, the total booked over a tube
equals that direction's total shadow, $\iint (1 - e^{-\mu L})\,dx\,dy$
(renderers document s.3.9). Not conserved: a column's total
$\sum_j \Delta A^{\rm hyb}_j(x, y)$, which can exceed 1 (light that oblique
rays bring in through the side walls is booked at the column), so in dark
stain $I_k < 0$ is possible (Block 21's findings).

### 2.4 Bias table and correction (Blocks 6, 7, 14; procedure Eqs. 1-3, 7; mathematics Eqs. 19-21; D-024, D-057 to D-063, D-071)

[rewritten 2026-10-21 with Blocks 14.2-14.5 (S4); the Block 6 text it replaces is kept below as superseded]

Design (D-059, D-062; Block 14.2): $G$ independent draws $g$ of a cylinder's features $(d_g, \varphi_g, \theta_g, \mu_g)$ by the laws of D-024 (iii) -- $\log d \sim U[\log d_{\min}, \log d_{\max}]$, $\varphi \sim U[\varphi_{\rm lo}, \varphi_{\rm hi})$, $\theta \sim U[0, \pi)$, $\log\mu \sim U[\log\mu_{\min}, \log\mu_{\max}]$ -- each realized $n_{\rm real}$ times, realization $r$ with fresh nuisances (the sub-pixel offset, the axis depth, the node jitter, the camera noise, and the branch radius $r_{{\rm br},g,r} = d_g/2 + \epsilon$, $\epsilon \sim \mathcal N(0, \sigma_{\rm br}^2)$ redrawn until positive), from the keys `[seed, g]`, `[seed, g, r]`, `[seed, g, r, 1]`. A realization's fit (the deliverable of Block 13) gives $\hat D_{g,r}$, $\hat\mu_{g,r}$ and the ratio $y_{g,r} = \hat D_{g,r}/d_g$. The target (D-063.1): $b(x \mid \mathcal C, \mathcal S) = \mathbb E[\hat D/d \mid d, \varphi, \hat M\hat D = \hat\mu\hat d, \mathcal C, \mathcal S]$ at $x = (\ln d, \varphi, \ln(\hat\mu\hat d))$, and $m = d\,b$. Estimator (Block 14.4): local linear kernel regression of $y$ on $x$ over the rows in $\mathcal S$, evaluated on a regular grid and read by its tensor cubic interpolant, Gaussian weights of bandwidths $c\,(h_1, h_2, h_3)$ = $c$ x `tau_kernel_bandwidth`, the multiplier $c$ chosen by cross-validation with folds by draw (D-071.1); its SE the sandwich by draw (D-063.3, D-071.2); beside it $\hat\tau$, $N^{\rm loc}_{\rm eff}$ and the labelled independent-rows SE; over every counted row at its true $x^{\rm fail} = (\ln d_g, \varphi_g, \ln(\mu_g d_g))$ the kernel mass $M$, the failure rate $\hat p_{\rm fail}$ and $q_{\rm LOO}$ (D-062.5, D-063.4). Correction (Block 14.5): $\tilde d_i$ solves $\hat m(d, \varphi_i, \hat\mu_i\hat d_i \mid \mathcal C) = \hat d_i$ on $[d_{\min}, d_{\max}]$ by `brentq` when $\hat m(\cdot, \varphi_i, \hat\mu_i\hat d_i) - \hat d_i$ changes sign exactly once on the inversion grid (else `non_monotone`, or `out_of_domain` without a change); flags `large_correction` ($|\hat b - 1| >$ `bias_flag_threshold`, D5), `high_failure` ($\hat p_{\rm fail} >$ `max_failure_rate`), `off_support` ($M < q_{\rm LOO}$ or no support around the root), `phi_out_of_domain`, and the label `mu_d_out_of_domain` (the third coordinate clamped, the node inverted and not filled for it, D-071.4); the correction refuses a table whose estimator signature differs from the fit's (procedure s.3.11). Fill (D5): flagged nodes take the median of unflagged same-branch neighbours, else Allen's radius; then a running median of `median_window_nodes` along the branch.

Superseded (Block 6, 2026-10-06 to 2026-10-21; one realization per replicate, the spline in $(\ln d, \varphi)$):

> For a phantom of true $(d, \varphi)$ and nuisances $\xi$ (heading, sub-pixel
> offset, axis depth, noise, node jitter), the pipeline's fitted diameter
> before the draw is the random variable $\hat D$; the target is
> $m(d, \varphi \mid \mathcal C) = \mathbb E_\xi[\hat D \mid d, \varphi, \mathcal C, \mathcal S]$
> and $b = m/d$. Design (D-024): $N$ independent replicates with
> $\log d \sim U[\log d_{\min}, \log d_{\max}]$, $\varphi \sim U[0, 90°)$,
> $\theta \sim U[0, \pi)$, $\log\mu \sim U[\log\mu_{\min}, \log\mu_{\max}]$,
> replicate $n$ drawing from `numpy.random.default_rng([seed, n])` (reproducible
> whatever the chunking). Estimator: the smoothing thin-plate spline
> $\hat b(\ln d, \varphi)$ through the retained $(\ln d_n, \varphi_n, \hat d_n/d_n)$
> (`scipy.interpolate.RBFInterpolator`, `kernel="thin_plate_spline"`, inputs
> $(\ln d / \text{um}, \varphi / \text{rad})$, smoothing chosen by k-fold
> cross-validation over `spline_smoothing_grid`), and
> $\hat m(d, \varphi \mid \mathcal C) = d\,\hat b(\ln d, \varphi)$ -- the procedure's
> Eq. (7) works on the ratios $\hat d_n/d$ too, and their scatter is roughly
> constant in $d$ [corrected 2026-10-06: the spline was written on $\hat d_n$];
> $\hat\tau$ (kernel-weighted SD of the ratios about $\hat b$), the failure rate
> (kernel-weighted share outside $\mathcal S$ among the replicates that pass the screens named in `failure_rate_ignore` [corrected 2026-10-21, Block 14.1 (D-057.1): the default is now empty, so every replicate is counted; and `dark` is no criterion of $\mathcal S$ (D-030)], ~~default the dark flag~~: a real node applies those screens to itself [corrected 2026-10-06: all replicates; with the provisional $\mu$ range the dark share flagged every node with $d \ge 1$ um, gate 2]) and
> $\mathrm{SE} = \hat\tau/\sqrt{N_{\rm eff}}$ ($N_{\rm eff} = (\sum w)^2/\sum w^2$)
> from Gaussian weights with bandwidths `tau_kernel_bandwidth` ($\ln d$, degrees). Correction at a
> real node: $\tilde d_i$ solves $\hat m(d, \varphi_i \mid \mathcal C) = \hat d_i$
> by `brentq` on $d \in [d_{\min}, d_{\max}]$, requiring exactly one sign change of
> $\hat m(\cdot, \varphi_i) - \hat d_i$ on the inversion grid (else flag `non_monotone`; [corrected 2026-10-18:
> this sentence said "$\hat m(\cdot, \varphi_i)$ strictly increasing there", which is sufficient for a unique root
> but is not what Block 7 or `invert.invert_node` checks]) and
> $\hat d_i \in [\hat m(d_{\min}, \varphi_i), \hat m(d_{\max}, \varphi_i)]$ (else
> `out_of_domain`); flag `large_correction` when
> $|\hat b(\tilde d_i, \varphi_i) - 1| >$ `bias_flag_threshold` (D5),
> `high_failure` when the local failure rate $>$ `max_failure_rate`. The
> correction refuses a table whose estimator signature (`config.estimator_signature()`)
> differs from the fit's (procedure s.3.11). Fill (D5): flagged nodes take the
> median of unflagged same-branch neighbours, else Allen's radius; then a
> running median of `median_window_nodes` along the branch.

### Notation

| Symbol | Meaning | Type / shape | Units |
|---|---|---|---|
| $p_{\rm x}$, $\Delta z$ | pixel pitch, plane spacing | float | um |
| $i, j$ | SWC dendrite node indices | int | - |
| $k$, $z_k$, $k^*_i$ | plane index, its depth, sharpest plane of node $i$ | int, float, int | -, um, - |
| $G_{j,k}$, $F_{j,k}$, $h_j$ | gradient energy (focus rule, D-030), dip depth (comparison), half-width of $G$'s window | float >= 0, float, float > 0 | 1/um, -, um |
| $d, r, \mu$ | tube diameter, radius, absorption coefficient | float > 0, > 0, >= 0 | um, um, 1/um |
| $\varphi, \theta$ | tilt out of the image plane, heading | float in [0, pi/2], (-pi, pi] | rad |
| $\hat d_i, \hat\mu_i, \hat v_{0,i}, \hat\alpha_i$ | fitted diameter, coefficient, axis offset; derived centre-line absorbance | float | um, 1/um, um, - |
| $\bar B_i$, $B$ | background estimate (computed), rendered background (configured) | float | grey levels |
| $\sigma_{\rm fit}$, $\sigma_{\rm r}(\delta)$ | fit blur; rendering kernel width at defocus $\delta$ | float > 0 | um |
| $a_j, T_{<j}, \Delta A_j$ | slab absorbance, light reaching slab $j$, absorbed fraction | arrays (n_y, n_x) | -, -, - |
| $\hat s$, $W$, $s_m$ | a condenser direction (unit, $s_z > 0$); the cone's measure (Block 9's quadrature); its aperture radius `ray_na` / `ray_n_oil` | (3,); weights (N,) summing to 1; float | -, -, - |
| $\ell_{\rm back}(p;\hat s)$, $h(p)$ | length inside the tube of the ray that reaches $p$, before $p$; the cone's average of $e^{-\mu\ell_{\rm back}}/s_z$ (Block 21) | float >= 0 (inf inside an uncapped tube along its axis); float > 0 | um, - |
| $\lambda_j$, $\bar\zeta_j$, $\Delta A^{\rm hyb}_j$ | a column's length inside both the tube and slab $j$; its midpoint; the hybrid's absorbed fraction (Block 21) | arrays (n_y, n_x) | um, um, - |
| $I_k(x, y)$ | rendered or real plane $k$ | array (n_y, n_x) | grey levels |
| $m, b, \hat m, \hat b, \hat\tau$ | mean response, bias factor; their estimates; scatter of $\hat D/d$ | float | um, -, um, -, - |
| $\tilde d_i$, $d_{{\rm final},i}$ | corrected diameter; after flags and fill | float | um |
| $\mathcal C$, $\mathcal S$ | configuration dictionary; selection event | dict, predicate | - |

## 3. Data contracts

| Item | Source / format | Shape and axis order | dtype | Units | Conventions |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| SWC file | Allen `reconstruction.swc`; `swc_io.read_swc` | N nodes: ids, types, parent (N,), xyz (N, 3), radius (N,) | int, float64 | um | 7 fields per data line; `#` comments kept verbatim; types 3, 4 = dendrite |
| image block | `allen_image_io.fetch_zblock` (Colab) or the renderer | `block` (n_planes, height, width); `ks` plane indices; `valid` (n_planes,); `frame` CropFrame | uint8 (real) / float64 (synthetic before the camera chain) | grey levels | row = y, column = x; missing planes white (255) and `valid = False` |
| profile | Block 5 | $v_n$ (n_v,), $I_i(v_n)$ (n_v,) | float64 | um, grey levels | $v = 0$ at the node; step $p_{\rm x}$; bilinear |
| per-node CSV | Block 8 | one row per dendrite node | mixed | um, rad, grey levels, 1/um | columns listed in Block 8 |
| replicate table | Block 6 | one row per replicate: draws, outputs, status, flags | mixed | um, rad, 1/um | stored before any selection |
| bias table | Block 6 | `npz` (spline weights/centres, tau grid, failure grid) + JSON of `config.full_signature()` | float64 | - | file name carries `signature_hash("estimator")` |

Conventions: 0-based array indices; SWC node ids are the file's own (not
assumed 1-based); `plane_index = section_number - min(section_number)`
(handoff); z in stage units throughout; angles in radians inside the code,
degrees in config and CSV column names ending `_deg`.

## 4. Global conventions

- Units used internally: um, radians, grey levels, 1/um; converted at the loading boundary.
- Floating-point precision: float64 (uint8 only for real image blocks).
- Randomness: a `numpy.random.Generator` passed explicitly; the seed is `PhantomConfig.seed`, set once in the script.
- Parameters only in `src/allen_diameter/config.py` (D-021); every field carries a `# source:` tag (Block 1 asserts it).
- Code destined for the cluster is pure ASCII with LF line endings (`hpc-python-compat`); `.gitattributes` pins `*.py` to LF.
- Binding decisions:
  - D-013: corrected morphologies = one SWC per specimen, same format as the archive's; only radii change.
  - D-018: $\bar B_i$ fixed from a median (never fitted); $\sigma_{\rm fit}$ fixed.
  - D-019: the fitted darkness parameter is $\mu$; $\varphi_i$ from the line fit; $\alpha$ derived.
  - D-021: open choices are config fields with provisional defaults; alternatives selectable by name; comparisons labelled.
  - D-022: layout as in Coverage; branch `sci/diameter-pipeline`; production table on davinci.
  - D-023: `mu_mode = per_node`, `bbar_region = block_masked`, `focus_bg_rule = profile_ends_median`; $\sigma_{\rm fit}$ a study axis (`sigma_fit_study_um`), deliverable 0.099 um (provisional) [corrected 2026-10-21, S2.2: the study axis and `sigma_fit_study_um` are removed by D-057 (ii); 0.099 um is the one value].
  - D-030: `focus_rule = gradient_energy` (Eq. 1); the dip depth of handoff Eq. 1 is the labelled comparison `dip_depth`, recorded beside it on every node; its window (`focus_grad_window = radius_margin`, `focus_grad_margin_um` 0.5 um) is provisional. The registration check of Block 0 is not changed.
  - D-024: `absorption = partition_vertical`, `kernel_continuation = linear` (0.79); dark flag $\hat\alpha > 1.0$; random design, $U = 10$ um, $\varphi \in [0°, 90°)$, $d$ range temporary; `response_estimator = tps_spline`; no tilt exclusion; D5 (flag 0.2 + fill) and D7 confirmed; jitter 0 until cell 13.
  - D5/D6 (design handoff): correct by inversion of $\hat m$ (not the shortcut $\hat d/b(\hat d)$); compute directions in um in the global frame.
  - D-031: the table's third axis is the fitted optical depth $\hat\alpha$; a real node is corrected at its own $\hat\alpha_i$ (implemented in Block 14).
  - D-052: the measurement the table is built for: per node the Allen-guided plane (D-042 to D-044; Block 13) and at it the fit (3'') with the skewed generalized-$t$ kernel, $(d, \mu, v_0, w, \beta, \xi, \zeta)$ all fitted, centre weights $\lambda$ = 0.5 um re-centred by IRLS (Block 12); phantoms and real nodes through the same chain.
  - D-053: (i) the phantoms' radius is decided by a sensitivity check on rendered phantoms (before Block 14) [superseded by D-058, 2026-10-18]; (ii) a fit leaves $\mathcal S$ when $d$, $\mu$, $v_0$ or $w$ is at a bound or the IRLS did not converge, never for $\beta$, $\xi$, $\zeta$ at theirs (Block 12's status rule); (iii) the third axis is $\ln\hat\alpha$.
  - D-054: the spline's inputs divided by their design ranges, CV on the smoothing only; the third kernel bandwidth 0.3 in $\ln\hat\alpha$ (PROVISIONAL); `max_failure_rate` 0.2 PROVISIONAL until the davinci pilot; the SGT fit's per-solve budget `sgt_max_nfev` = 3000.
  - D-057: `failure_rate_ignore = ()`; the $\sigma_{\rm fit}$ study of D-023 (iv) dropped (one table at 0.099 um); phantoms measured by exactly the real-data pipeline, the plane rule and the profile extent included.
  - D-058: each phantom's starting radius is $r_n = d_n/2 + \epsilon_n$, $\epsilon_n \sim \mathcal N(0, \sigma_r^2)$ [user]; real nodes keep Allen's radius. Open (section 8): $\sigma_r$ (um); whether $r_n$ feeds only the iteration's start (round 1's line, half-length $3 r_n$) or all four places of the handoff's section 6; the rule for $r_n \le 0$; $\epsilon$ per draw or per realization. Supersedes D-053 (i). Block 14.
  - D-059: the laws of D-024 (iii) stay, with a check that the phantoms' fitted $\hat\alpha$ covers the real cells' [user]; $G$ independent cylinder draws $(d_g, \varphi_g, \theta_g, \mu_g)$, each realized $k$ times with fresh nuisances; per draw the mean ratio $\bar y_g = k_{\mathcal S,g}^{-1} \sum_{r:\,(g,r) \in \mathcal S} \hat d_{g,r}/d_g$ and the failure share $1 - k_{\mathcal S,g}/k$ [user]. $G$ and $k$ open (Q6). Block 14.
  - D-060: the spline is fitted through every realization in $\mathcal S$ at its own $(\ln d_g, \varphi_g, \ln\hat\alpha_{g,r})$ [user], so $\hat b(x \mid \mathcal C, \mathcal S)$ stays conditional on a single realization's fitted $\hat\alpha$ and a real node is inverted at its own $\ln\hat\alpha_i$; the per-draw means give the per-draw (nuisance-only) scatter and the checks. Cross-validation folds by draw: proposed, not decided (section 8). Block 14.
  - D-061 (2026-10-20): Block 13's implementation: one provider call per node for the pilot's block over the union of the pilot's plane ranges and $K_i = k_{{\rm SWC},i} \pm 6$, plus one call per restack; the pilot and the iteration with the final fit are separate functions of one chain, the same for phantoms and real nodes, the diagnostics calling the pilot; the flags of $\mathcal S$ computed on the plane fitted, $k^{\rm it}_i$, the pilot's kept as labelled columns [user]; Block 10 selects its calibration nodes by Allen's diameter (thresholds in D-067); the kernel table is calibrated in Colab and loaded into the davinci configuration, where the draws, the table and its tests run [user]. Block 13.
  - D-062 (2026-10-20): Block 14's values: the phantom's branch radius $r_{\rm br} = d/2 + \epsilon$, $\epsilon \sim \mathcal N(0, \sigma_{\rm br}^2)$, $\sigma_{\rm br}$ = 0.2 um (`radius_noise_sd_um`), redrawn until $r_{\rm br} > 0$, drawn per realization, used in all four places the radius enters; $G \approx 10^5$ draws x $n_{\rm real}$ = 10 realizations; cross-validation folds by draw; `spline_smoothing_grid` extended to $10^4$ with a flag at either end; every realization enters the failure count at its draw's true third coordinate [user]. Block 14.
  - D-063 (2026-10-20): the response estimator at $N \approx 10^6$ rows is local linear kernel regression on a grid, with the Gaussian weights of section 2.4 and the bandwidths of D-054 (ii) in raw coordinates, read through a tensor cubic interpolant (`response_estimator = "local_linear"`); the SE is the smoother's own variance with the by-draw (cluster) sandwich; `off_support` when the kernel mass at the root is below the rows' leave-one-out 5th percentile; the third axis is $\ln(\hat\mu\hat d)$ (amends D-053 (iii); D-054 (i)'s normalisation retired) [user]. Supersedes D-024 (v)'s spline and D-060.1's. Block 14.
  - D-064 (2026-10-20): the iteration's background $B_{j,k}$ masks the segments and the soma of a scene input -- the cell's traced segments and soma on real data, the node's own branch and no soma when the input is absent (a phantom); $\bar B_j(k)$ keeps its own-branch mask [user]. Block 13.
  - D-065 (2026-10-20): the phantom ranges stay; the coverage check of D-059 is a Colab survey of the real neurons' $\hat\alpha_i$ and $\hat\mu_i\hat d_i$ across cells per bin of $\hat d_i$; $d_{\max}$ from Allen's radii across cells; the node-jitter SDs from the registration survey; the pinned-kernel comparison (D-052 (g)) not run [user].
  - D-066 (2026-10-20): withdrawn by D-067, never implemented (the renderer's kernel as the SGT family).
  - D-067 (2026-10-20): the order Block 13 -> the Colab surveys and the kernel's calibration -> the final configuration -> Block 14 -> gate 2 -> the davinci pilot -> production; the renderer keeps its Gaussian kernel with the calibrated $\sigma_{\rm r}(\delta)$ table, the fit its SGT kernel; only `local_linear` is built (no P-spline comparison); Block 10's thresholds in Allen's diameter $2 r_{\rm A}$: thin $\le$ 0.6 um (near field), far field 0.6-0.8 um, PROVISIONAL, the other criteria from the pilot's columns [user].
  - D-068 (2026-10-21): an iteration that ends without converging keeps the fit of its last plane and stays in $\mathcal S$ when the fit's status and the flags of `SELECTION_FLAGS` allow (D-068.1); every ending but `converged` adds the label `iter_<ending>` to the flags, after Block 5's, not a criterion (D-068.2) [user: "Keep but flag"].
  - D-069 (2026-10-21): round 1's line has half-length $\max(m\,d^{(0)}/2,\ \tfrac{n_{\min}-1}{2}\Delta v)$, $n_{\min}$ = `iter_min_first_samples` = 3 and $\Delta v$ = `profile_step_um`, so it holds at least 3 samples; later rounds and the start radius unchanged [user: option (b) for the thin end of D-062.1].
  - D-070 (2026-10-21): the far-field calibration of D-026 is stage S2b; until it exists the continuation beyond 0.84 um stays D-024 (i)'s $\gamma$ = 0.79; S4 is written while S3 runs [user] [timing amended by D-071.5: S2b follows Block 14 directly].
  - D-071 (2026-10-21): Block 14's open choices -- the bandwidth multiplier $c$ chosen by cross-validation with folds by draw; the by-draw sandwich the one SE (beside the labelled independent-rows value); the grid's spacing at most half the chosen bandwidths; a real node whose $\hat\mu\hat d$ lies outside the table's third axis flagged `mu_d_out_of_domain`, a label only; after Block 14, the far-field calibration (S2b) next [user].
  - D-084 (2026-10-26): the hybrid absorption renderer (I-004) is the renderer actually used once built and tested; until then Block 4 keeps the partition of D-024 (i) [user]. Block 21.
  - D-092 (2026-10-27): "GO on with the hybrid renderer" [user]: Block 21 built as Block 4's absorption mode `hybrid`; the mode's name, the slab rule, Block 9's directions and the default left at `partition_vertical` are the assistant's, PROVISIONAL until the user confirms them.
  - D-093 (2026-10-27): the calibration nodes are not screened for straightness -- the calibration evaluates only the measuring line; flatness ($\varphi \le$ 5 deg, D-081.3) stays strict; if the test is restored, its tolerance is 2 (or 4) x Allen's radius [user]. Block 10.I (v), `CalibrationConfig.node_straight_check` = False, `node_straight_tol_radii` = 2. Supersedes D-081.3's straightness part.
  - D-094 (2026-10-28): "Remove both faint" [user] -- the calibration nodes are selected without the optical-depth criterion $\hat\alpha_j \le 0.5$ (`not_faint`) and without the flag `faint` (a dip under 6 gl); $\mathcal S$ itself, for the table and the deliverable, keeps the flag [the assistant's reading, stated to the user] [superseded 2026-10-28 by D-096: $\mathcal S$ drops the flag too]. Block 10.I (i) and (iv), `CalibrationConfig.node_alpha_check` = False, `node_faint_flag_check` = False (True restores either). Supersedes D-067.4's faint criterion and, for the calibration nodes only, its "in $\mathcal S$" as far as the flag `faint`.
  - D-096 (2026-10-28): "we have to correct ALL the nodes regardless the optical depth and the dip. So we need to drop entirely the faint selection criteria for the bias table" [user] -- the flag `faint` (a dip under `faint_min_dip_gl`) is no criterion of $\mathcal S$, for phantoms and real nodes: it stays a label, as `dark` since D-030, so the table is fitted on replicates of every dip depth and every real node whose fit is in $\mathcal S$ is inverted. Supersedes D-094.3 (the assistant's reading that $\mathcal S$ kept the flag). The flags of $\mathcal S$ enter the estimator signature (`"selection"`), so a table built under another $\mathcal S$ is refused [the assistant's choice, to confirm]. Blocks 1, 6, 7 (`correct_nodes`), 10 (`selection_flags`), 14.1.
  - ID note (2026-10-18): this spec cited the gradient-energy focus rule as "D-030" before this date; in the decision log that rule is **D-035**, and the log's D-030 is "every real node gets its own estimate whatever its optical depth" (the dark flag a label only). Text written from 2026-10-18 on cites the log's IDs.

### Block 2: geometry (S1)-(S6)

- Module: `src/allen_diameter/model/geometry.py`
- Public API: `Tube(c, r, phi, theta, aspect=1.0, half_length=None, end_cut="axial")` (frozen; properties `d`, `phi0`, `e_u`, `e_v`, `t_hat`, `t_hat0`, `half_chord_factor`); `axis_direction(phi, theta) -> (3,)`; `to_local(x, y, z, tube) -> (u, v, w)`; `inside_tube(x, y, z, tube) -> bool array`; `column_interval(x, y, tube) -> (z_lo, z_hi, inside)`; `depth_extent(tube) -> (z_min, z_max)`; `lateral_half_extent(tube) -> (along e_u, along e_v)`; `slab_centres(z_min, z_max, dzeta) -> zeta (J,)`; `slab_absorbance(z_lo, z_hi, zeta, dzeta, mu) -> a_j`; `transmitted_before(z_lo, z_hi, zeta, dzeta, mu, light_direction) -> T_<j`; `line_interval(P, s, tube) -> (t1, t2, hit)`
- Inputs: positions in um (x, y specimen-referred; z stage units), broadcasting arrays; `r` um > 0; `phi` rad in [0, pi/2) (observed tilt); `theta` rad; `aspect` k > 0; `half_length` U um > 0 or None; `mu` 1/um >= 0; `light_direction` +1 (light toward +z) or -1
- Outputs: float64 arrays of the broadcast input shape (absorbances dimensionless, lengths um); `z_lo = z_hi = c_z` and `inside = False` where a vertical line misses (never NaN); `line_interval` returns line parameters, arc length = (t2 - t1)|s|
- Generalisations of impl-handoff (S1)-(S6), both reducing to the handoff at k = 1:
  - squashed cross-section: the phantom is a round tube of radius r in unsquashed space mapped by S = diag(1, 1, k) about c (mounting shrinkage along global z, procedure s.3.5); unsquashed tilt phi0 = arctan(tan(phi)/k); the vertical-line half-chord is h(v) = sqrt(r^2 - v^2) sqrt(k^2 + tan^2 phi); the width along e_v stays d for every phi and k (handoff Eq. 7);
  - end cut `RendererConfig.end_cut`: `"vertical"` keeps |u| <= U (impl-handoff (S5)); `"axial"` keeps |s0| <= U with s0 = u cos(phi0) + (w/k) sin(phi0) the unsquashed arc length along the axis, so the depth span stays bounded for every phi < 90 deg. Depth extent: vertical c_z -+ (U tan phi + r sqrt(k^2 + tan^2 phi)); axial c_z -+ k (U sin phi0 + r cos phi0).
- Parameters: `RendererConfig.U_um`, `end_cut` (default `"axial"`, PROVISIONAL, to be confirmed), `cross_section_aspect`, `dzeta_um`; `AcquisitionConfig.light_direction`
- Library calls relied on: numpy
- Custom code: all (the closed forms of the spec; no library computes tube chords)
- Test oracles (`tests/smoke/test_smoke_geometry.py`): width along e_v = d to 1e-12 for random phi <= 85 deg, k in [0.4, 1.6], all cuts; handoff Eq. 8 oblique cut to 1e-10 relative and its 1.265 d example; vertical line through c = 2 r sqrt(k^2 + tan^2 phi) (or the cap-limited length) to 1e-10; a line along the axis with axial caps has length 2U/|S^-1 t_hat| to 1e-9; column intervals vs an independent (S2) membership and vs handoff Eq. 6: 0 mismatches away from 1e-9 of the ends; slab chords vs 20001-sample brute force to 2 sampling steps; line chords vs 2e-4 um sampling to 4 steps; identical to `checks/stack_geometry_check.py` (`ray_interval`, `inside_3d`) to 1e-12 at k = 1; sum_j a_j = mu (z_hi - z_lo) to 1e-12; T_<j = cumulative product of exp(-a) for both light directions to 1e-12; sum_j T_<j (1 - e^{-a_j}) = 1 - e^{-sum a_j} (C1) to 1e-12; the depth extent bounds every column and is attained at its extreme point (to 1e-9 (1 + tan phi)); rotation by pi with theta -> theta + pi is a symmetry (S3); tangent and on-surface lines miss; invalid tubes raise ValueError
- Data flow: feeds Block 4 (renderer) and Block 9 (ray world)
- Confirmed: yes (S1)-(S6) as written in the impl-handoff; the squash and the axial cut are the assistant's generalisations, the axial default PROVISIONAL (2026-10-06)
- Status: smoke-tested 2026-10-06 (5 pass, 2 skip)

### Block 3: forward model and fitter (handoff Eqs. 10-11; D-018.2; D-019.1)

- Module: `src/allen_diameter/model/tube_model.py` (forward model), `src/allen_diameter/analysis/fit.py` (fitter)
- Forward model: for each fixed $d > 0$ (um), $\alpha \ge 0$, $v_0$ (um), $\sigma > 0$ (um), $\bar B > 0$ (grey levels) and every $v$ (um), handoff Eq. 11 with $B$ fixed (D-018.2) is $I_{\rm model}(v) = \bar B\,(T_{d,\alpha,v_0} * g_\sigma)(v)$ with $T_{d,\alpha,v_0}(v) = e^{-\alpha s_d(v - v_0)}$. Since $1 - T$ vanishes for $|v - v_0| > d/2$ and $g_\sigma$ has unit integral, the substitution $u = (d/2)\sin\psi$ gives

  $$I_{\rm model}(v \mid d, \alpha, v_0, \sigma, \bar B) = \bar B\Big[1 - \frac{d}{2}\int_{-\pi/2}^{\pi/2}\big(1 - e^{-\alpha\cos\psi}\big)\cos\psi\; g_\sigma\big(v - v_0 - \tfrac{d}{2}\sin\psi\big)\,d\psi\Big] \tag{B3.1}$$

  whose integrand is analytic in $\psi$ (no endpoint square root), evaluated by $N$-node Gauss-Legendre quadrature in $\psi$ (`numpy.polynomial.legendre.leggauss`, nodes cached per $N$). In a fit, $N = \max(\texttt{fit\_quad\_min\_nodes}, \lceil \texttt{fit\_quad\_nodes\_per\_sigma}\cdot d_{\rm hi}/\sigma_{\rm fit}\rceil)$ with $d_{\rm hi}$ = `fit_d_bounds_um[1]`, fixed for the whole fit so the model is smooth in the parameters. The $\mu$ form (D-019.1): $\alpha = \mu d/\cos\varphi_i$ for $\varphi_i \in [0, \pi/2)$.
- Exact dip area (oracle, for every $\alpha \ge 0$): $\int_{\mathbb R}\big(\bar B - I_{\rm model}(v)\big)\,dv = \bar B\,\frac{\pi d}{2}\big(I_1(\alpha) - \mathbf L_1(\alpha)\big)$ ($I_1$ modified Bessel, $\mathbf L_1$ modified Struve), with limits $\bar B\mu\pi d^2/(4\cos\varphi_i)$ as $\alpha \to 0$ and $\bar B d$ as $\alpha \to \infty$
- Fit (deliverable, D-019.1): parameters $(d, \mu, v_0)$ in the box `fit_d_bounds_um` x `fit_mu_bounds_per_um` x `fit_v0_bounds_um`; residuals $r_n = (I_{\rm model}(v_n) - I_i(v_n))/\bar B_i$ (same minimiser as (3), $\bar B_i > 0$ a constant); `scipy.optimize.least_squares(method="trf", jac="2-point", x_scale="jac", ftol = xtol = gtol = fit_tol, max_nfev = fit_max_nfev)`, one run per start; the reported run is the converged run (scipy status > 0, finite) of least residual sum of squares, the first in start order on ties
- Starts (`fit_start_rule`, PROVISIONAL): $n^*$ = index of the smallest $I_n$ among samples with $v_n$ inside the $v_0$ bounds; $v_{0,{\rm start}} = v_{n^*}$. `"profile"`: $w$ = width of the dip at half depth, level $(\bar B + I_{n^*})/2$, crossings found walking out from $n^*$ and placed by linear interpolation between samples; $d_0 = \sqrt{\max(w^2 - 8\ln 2\,\sigma_{\rm fit}^2, 0)}$ (the blur's FWHM removed in quadrature), or `fit_d0_um` when $I_{n^*} \ge \bar B$ or a crossing is missing on either side. `"fixed"`: $d_0$ = `fit_d0_um`. Start $k$: $d = d_0 f_k$ ($f_k$ = `fit_multistart_factors`) and $\mu = \alpha_0\cos\varphi_i/d$ with $\alpha_0 = -\ln\min(1, \max(I_{n^*}/\bar B, \text{smallest positive float}))$ (the start's centre-line absorbance equals the observed depth), each clipped into its bounds
- Status: `"failed"` when no start converged (the finite run of least residual sum of squares is then reported); `"at_bound"` when a reported parameter lies within `fit_at_bound_rel_tol` x (its bound range) of a bound, the bounds named in `at_bound` (`d_lo`, `d_hi`, `mu_lo`, `mu_hi`, `v0_lo`, `v0_hi`; `alpha_lo`, `alpha_hi` in the alpha form); otherwise `"converged"`. For a dark tube (alpha above about 5) the profile hardly depends on $\mu$ and the fit may stop at `mu_hi` with a sound $\hat d$ (seen on the `mu_tie.py` case $\mu = 3$, $d = 2$ um, $\hat d/d = 0.977$); such nodes are excluded by $\mathcal S$ twice over (bound, dark flag)
- alpha form (D-018.2; labelled comparison only, never the deliverable): the same fit over $(d, \alpha, v_0)$ with $\alpha$ in the box $[\mu_{\rm lo} d_{\rm lo}/\cos\varphi_i,\ \mu_{\rm hi} d_{\rm hi}/\cos\varphi_i]$ that contains the image of the $\mu$ box; same starts ($\alpha = \alpha_0$); $\hat\mu$ reported as $\hat\alpha\cos\varphi_i/\hat d$. The two forms have the same minimiser in $(d, v_0)$ whenever no bound is active (bijection, D-019)
- Public API: `tube_model.transmittance(v, d, alpha, v0) -> T`; `tube_model.model_profile(v, d, alpha, v0, sigma, B_bar, n_nodes) -> I_model`; `tube_model.alpha_from_mu(mu, d, phi) -> alpha`; `tube_model.quadrature_rule(n_nodes) -> (sin_psi, cos_psi, weights)`; `tube_model.n_quadrature_nodes(d_hi, sigma, min_nodes, nodes_per_sigma) -> int`; `fit.half_depth_width(v, I, B_bar, n_star) -> float or None`; `fit.start_points(v, I, phi, B_bar, cfg, form) -> (n_starts, 3) array`; `fit.fit_profile(v, I, phi, B_bar, cfg) -> FitResult`; `fit.fit_profile_alpha(v, I, phi, B_bar, cfg) -> FitResult`
- `FitResult` (frozen): `d_hat_um, mu_hat_per_um, v0_hat_um, alpha_hat` (= $\hat\mu\hat d/\cos\varphi_i$), `phi_rad`, `form` (`"mu"` or `"alpha"`), `status`, `at_bound` (tuple of bound names), `rss_gl2` ($\sum_n (I_n - I_{\rm model}(v_n))^2$, grey levels squared), `n_samples`, `n_starts`, `best_start`, `n_converged`, `nfev` (summed over starts), `n_nodes`, `message`
- Inputs: `v` (n,) strictly increasing, um; `I` (n,) finite, grey levels; `phi` rad in [0, pi/2); `B_bar` > 0 finite, grey levels; `cfg` a `MeasureConfig` ($\sigma_{\rm fit}$ = `sigma_fit_um`); n >= 4; at least one sample inside the $v_0$ bounds. Anything else raises ValueError (Block 5 flags a profile that leaves the image before calling the fit). `mu_mode` other than `per_node` raises NotImplementedError (a shared $\mu$ is a planned labelled comparison, D-023)
- Parameters: `sigma_fit_um`, `fit_d_bounds_um`, `fit_mu_bounds_per_um`, `fit_v0_bounds_um`, `fit_multistart_factors`, `fit_start_rule`, `fit_d0_um`, `fit_tol`, `fit_max_nfev`, `fit_at_bound_rel_tol`, `fit_quad_min_nodes`, `fit_quad_nodes_per_sigma`
- Library calls relied on: `numpy.polynomial.legendre.leggauss`, `scipy.optimize.least_squares`; in the test `scipy.integrate.quad`, `scipy.special.iv`, `scipy.special.modstruve`
- Custom code: the substitution (B3.1) (no library convolves a Beer-Lambert dome with a Gaussian) and the start rule
- Test oracles (`tests/smoke/test_smoke_fit.py`): (B3.1) against `scipy.integrate.quad` of the unsubstituted integral at $d \in \{0.05, 0.5, 2, 6\}$ um, $\alpha \in \{0.3, 3, 30\}$, $\sigma \in \{0.08, 0.125\}$: $|\Delta| \le 10^{-12}\bar B$ at the fit's $N$; the exact dip area to $10^{-9}$ relative at $\alpha \in \{10^{-3}, 0.3, 3, 10\}$; faint-limit centroid $v_0$ and variance $\sigma^2 + d^2/16$ (handoff Eq. 12) to $10^{-6}$ relative at $\alpha = 10^{-6}$; quadrature error decreasing in $N$ and $\le 10^{-12}\bar B$ at the configured $N$ for the worst case $d = d_{\rm hi}$, $\sigma = \min$ `sigma_fit_study_um`; `alpha_from_mu` equals $\mu d/\cos\varphi$ to 1 ulp and is the only route from $\mu$ to the model; agreement with `checks/mu_tie.py`'s grid model within $10^{-3}$ (its grid and 4-sigma truncation); noise-free recovery of $(d, \mu, v_0)$ to $10^{-6}$ relative at $\alpha \in \{0.3, 3\}$, $d \in \{0.5, 1, 2\}$ um, $\varphi \in \{0°, 30°, 60°\}$ (handoff "Numbers checked" asks $10^{-3}$); on the `mu_tie.py` profiles ($\sigma_{\rm true} = 0.10$, $\sigma_{\rm fit} = 0.125$, $\varphi = 15°$, $\mu \in \{0.6, 3\}$, $d \in \{0.3, 0.5, 1, 2\}$) the $\mu$ and $\alpha$ forms return the same $\hat d$ to $2\cdot10^{-3} d$ wherever neither is at a bound, and both report `at_bound` where one is; the same runs reproduce the logged D-019 (a) values $\hat d/d$ = 0.88, 0.98, 1.00 ($\mu = 0.6$) and 0.89, 0.98, 0.98 ($\mu = 3$) at $d$ = 0.5, 1, 2 um to $\pm 0.006$ (two-decimal rounding plus the grid model's error); the half-depth width of a sampled triangular dip is exact; a flat profile returns `at_bound` with `mu_lo`, an 8 um dip `d_hi`; determinism (bit-identical repeat); invalid inputs raise ValueError
- Data flow: consumes Block 5's profile, $\varphi_i$ and $\bar B_i$; feeds Blocks 5 (centres, handoff Eq. 3), 6, 10; `model_profile` is the C2 oracle of Block 4
- Confirmed: yes for the model and the fit (D-018, D-019, D-023); the quadrature, the start rule and the tolerances are the assistant's, PROVISIONAL (2026-10-06)
- Status: smoke-tested 2026-10-06 (7 pass): model vs quad 4e-14 B over 216 points; vs `mu_tie.py` 7e-4 (its grid); quadrature error 4e-15 B at N = 450 (d = 6 um, sigma = 0.08 um), 4e-4 B at N/4; noise-free recovery 1e-14; D-019 (a) reproduced (0.879, 0.982, 0.996; 0.886, 0.975, 0.977)

### Block 4: renderer (procedure Eqs. 5-6; impl-handoff (S4), FFT form; procedure s.3.6 step 6)

- Module: `src/allen_diameter/model/kernel.py`, `src/allen_diameter/model/render.py`, `src/allen_diameter/model/camera.py`, `src/allen_diameter/loading/jpeg_tables.py`
- Kernel (`kernel_family = "gaussian_table"`): for every defocus $\delta$ (um), $\sigma_{\rm r}(\delta)$ = linear interpolation of (`kernel_table_delta_um`, `kernel_table_sigma_um`) at $|\delta| \le \delta_{\max}$ (the last knot); beyond, $\sigma_{\rm r}(\delta_{\max}) + \gamma(|\delta| - \delta_{\max})$ with $\gamma$ = `kernel_continuation_slope` (`"linear"`), $\gamma = 0$ (`"frozen"`), $\gamma = \sigma_{\rm r}(\delta_{\max})/\delta_{\max}$ (`"proportional"`, the rule of `checks/stack_geometry_check.py`). `sigma_r0_um` is the table's value at 0 (one number under two names; `validate()` keeps them equal). `"empirical"` raises NotImplementedError until Block 10
- Fine grid: a `FineGrid(x0, y0, h, nx, ny)` of sample centres $x_n = x_0 + n h$, $y_m = y_0 + m h$ (um); the object is drawn on every sample of the grid and nowhere else (the caller chooses the grid: Block 6 sizes it from the block, `pad_um` and the tube's footprint). For a block of pixels (`left`, `top`, `width`, `height`, full-resolution pixel coordinates whose integers are pixel centres; um = coordinate x $p_{\rm x}$), `block_fine_grid` subdivides every pixel into $f \times f$ samples, $f = p_{\rm x}/h_{\rm g}$ an integer ($h_{\rm g}$ = `h_g_um_thin` for $d \le$ `h_g_switch_d_um`, else `h_g_um_thick`; both $p_{\rm x}/8$ since 2026-10-06: on noise-free replicates through the whole chain, $\hat d/d$ agrees to 1e-5 at $p_{\rm x}/8$, /16, /32 for $d$ = 0.3 um, $\varphi$ = 5 deg and $d$ = 0.5 um, 20 deg, to 1.5e-4 at $p_{\rm x}/4$ vs /8 for $d$ = 2 um, 30 deg, and to 0.7 % for $d$ = 0.3 um, 45 deg, where $b$ = 1.87 is flagged by D5 anyway; the impl-handoff's $p_{\rm x}/16$ came from fine-grid profiles before pixel integration [corrected 2026-10-06], PROVISIONAL), and adds `pad_px` whole pixels on each side
- Slabs: centres $\zeta_j$ from Block 2 `slab_centres` over $[\min z_{\rm lo}, \max z_{\rm hi}]$ of the tube's columns on the grid, thickness `dzeta_um`. Per slab, on the grid: $a_j$ and $T_{<j}$ by Block 2 (`slab_absorbance`, `transmitted_before` with `AcquisitionConfig.light_direction`); the absorbed fraction is $\Delta A_j = T_{<j}(1 - e^{-a_j})$ for `absorption = "partition_vertical"` (procedure Eq. 5) and $\Delta A_j = a_j$ for `"linear"` (mathematics Eq. 11); $\Delta A^{\rm hyb}_j = a_j\,h(x, y, \bar\zeta_j)$ for `"hybrid"` (section 2.3 (H), Block 21: each slab's computed once per `render_transmittance` call and served to every plane and both FFT paths) [added 2026-10-27]; `"ray_world"`: `render_transmittance` hands the whole job to Block 9 (no slabs, no kernel; `RenderResult.backend = "ray_world"`) and `absorbed_fractions` refuses it with ValueError, since the ray world has no absorbed fractions [corrected 2026-10-06: it raised NotImplementedError until Block 9]
- Planes: transmittance $\tau_k = I_k/B = 1 - \sum_j (\Delta A_j * G_{\sigma_{\rm r}(\zeta_j - z_k)})$ on the grid (procedure Eq. 6 divided by $B$), at plane depths $z_k = k\,\Delta z$ (plane index $k$; $z$ in stage units, increasing in the light's direction when `light_direction = +1`). Backend `"fft"` (deliverable), split by kernel width: (slab, plane) pairs with $\sigma_{\rm r} \le$ `fft_split_sigma_um` go through one `scipy.fft.rfft2` per slab, zero-padded on each axis by $p \ge$ `fft_wrap_sigmas` $\cdot\,\sigma/h$ samples ($\sigma$ the widest such kernel), the Gaussian transfer $e^{-2\pi^2\sigma^2(f_x^2 + f_y^2)}$ as an outer product per pair, one `irfft2` per plane (a source's periodic images stay at least $p\,h$ from every output sample: wrap error below $e^{-p^2h^2/(2\sigma^2)}$ of a slab's peak contribution); planes are processed in depth-ordered chunks whose spectral accumulators fit `max_fft_accumulator_mb` (a slab's FFT is redone only when its near planes straddle two chunks); wider pairs (far defocus: steep, long or thick tubes) are summed exactly from the sampled object against the continuous Gaussian onto coarse output grids, one per octave of $\sigma$ above the split (spacing $\le$ the octave's smallest $\sigma$ / `far_grid_per_sigma`), then bicubic-spline interpolated (`scipy.interpolate.RectBivariateSpline`) to the fine grid. An optional `reduce` function is applied to every finished plane (`synthetic_block` crops and pixel-integrates there), so only reduced planes are held: a two-worker table run was OOM-killed at 3.5 GB per worker before this (2026-10-06); peak RSS is now about 0.7 GB per replicate. Without the split the padding grows with the widest kernel (9 um and a 4840-sample FFT for a 60-degree tube with $U$ = 10 um). The grid must resolve the narrowest kernel, $h \le \sigma_{\min}/2$ (ValueError otherwise; spectral truncation $< 3\cdot10^{-9}$). Backend `"direct"` (reference): `scipy.ndimage.gaussian_filter(sigma = sigma_r / h, mode="constant", cval=0, truncate=direct_truncate)` per (slab, plane), as procedure s.3.6 step 4 writes it
- Camera chain (procedure s.3.6 step 6, in this order): pixel integration (mean of the $f \times f$ samples of each pixel), grey mapping $s = $ `black_level_gl` $+$ `gain` $\cdot$ `background_B_gl` $\cdot\,\tau_{\rm px}$, additive Gaussian noise of SD `noise_sd_gl` from the passed `numpy.random.Generator`, rounding to the nearest integer and clipping to $[0, 2^{\rm bit\_depth} - 1]$, JPEG round trip when `jpeg` is true: with Allen's quantization tables when the `qtables` argument is given, else with `renderer.jpeg_qtables` when that is non-empty, else at `quality = jpeg_quality` (Pillow). Tables are 64 integers each in natural (row-major) order, which is Pillow's convention for both `Image.open(f).quantization` and `save(qtables=...)` from 8.3.0 on (8.2.0's `JpegImagePlugin.DQT` stored the file's zigzag order; 8.3.0 de-zigzags, read from both wheels' source); `jpeg_roundtrip` refuses tables under Pillow < 8.3 with RuntimeError, since tables read by one convention and written by the other would be permuted. The synthetic planes are greyscale, one JPEG component, so Pillow writes and uses table 0 only, the luminance table (checked 2026-10-07: with two tables an `L` image carries table 0 alone, an RGB image both); if Allen's crops carry a second (chroma) table, chroma quantisation is not modelled, the real crops being converted to grey after decoding (`allen_image_io`) [corrected 2026-10-07: the tables entered only through the `qtables` argument, which no table build passed, and `renderer.jpeg_qtables_file` was read by nothing, so every phantom was encoded at `jpeg_quality`]. No bilinear interpolation (Block 5 applies it when reading profiles)
- Public API: `kernel.sigma_r(delta, rcfg) -> ndarray`; `render.FineGrid`; `render.block_fine_grid(left, top, width, height, p_x, factor, pad_px) -> (FineGrid, (row slice, col slice))`; `render.fine_factor(d, rcfg, p_x) -> int`; `render.slab_grid(tube, grid, dzeta) -> zeta (J,)`; `render.absorbed_fractions(tube, mu, grid, zeta, dzeta, light_direction, absorption) -> (J, ny, nx)` (tests and small cases); `render.render_transmittance(tube, mu, z_planes, grid, rcfg, light_direction, backend=None, reduce=None) -> RenderResult(tau (n_planes, ...), zeta, n_slabs_used, sigma_max_um, fft_shape, backend, n_near_pairs, n_far_pairs, far_shape)`; `render.pixel_integrate(fine, factor) -> array`; `render.synthetic_block(tube, mu, ks, left, top, width, height, cfg, rng, pad_px, qtables=None, backend=None) -> (block uint8 (n_planes, height, width), ks, valid, frame)` in the `fetch_zblock` contract (Section 3; `valid` all true; `frame = CropFrame(left, top, 0, p_x)`); `camera.camera_chain(tau_px, rcfg, rng, qtables=None) -> uint8` (`qtables=None`: `rcfg.jpeg_qtables` when non-empty, else `jpeg_quality`); `camera.jpeg_roundtrip(img8, quality=None, qtables=None) -> uint8`; `jpeg_tables.load_qtables(path) -> list of 64-integer lists`; `jpeg_tables.collect_qtables(paths) -> list of (tables or None, count, first path)` (Block 11); `jpeg_tables.check_pillow_table_order() -> None` (RuntimeError under Pillow < 8.3; called by `qtables_from_jpeg` and by `jpeg_roundtrip` when tables are used)
- Inputs: a Block 2 `Tube` (um, rad), `mu` (1/um, >= 0), plane depths (um), a `FineGrid`, `RendererConfig`, `light_direction` (+1 or -1), a `numpy.random.Generator` for the camera noise
- Parameters: all of `RendererConfig`; `AcquisitionConfig.res0_um`, `dz_um`, `light_direction`
- Library calls relied on: `scipy.fft` (`rfft2`, `irfft2`, `rfftfreq`, `fftfreq`, `next_fast_len`), `scipy.interpolate.RectBivariateSpline` (far path), `scipy.ndimage.gaussian_filter` (direct backend), Pillow (`Image.save(format="JPEG", quality | qtables)`, `Image.open(f).quantization`)
- Custom code: the slab loop with the partition (Eqs. 5-6), the per-(slab, plane) transfer functions and the far-field separable sum (no library renders a depth-dependent blur)
- Test oracles (`tests/smoke/test_smoke_render.py`): $\sigma_{\rm r}$ exact at the knots, linear between, the three continuations, even in $\delta$, equal to `stack_geometry_check.sigma_r` for `"proportional"`; C1: $\sum_j \Delta A_j = 1 - e^{-\sum_j a_j}$ per column to 1e-12; C2: one slab holding the whole tube with a constant kernel $\sigma$ and $\varphi = 0$ equals the discrete convolution of the sampled $1 - T$ with $g_\sigma$ to 1e-12, and converges to Block 3's `model_profile` as $h \to 0$ (error falling monotonically over $h = p_{\rm x}/8, /16, /32$, at most 1e-4 at $/32$; the decrease is irregular because point samples meet the tube's edges at varying sub-sample positions); the node dip moves by at most 1e-4 when $h_{\rm g}$ halves and 5e-4 when `dzeta_um` halves from 0.04 um; `fft` without the split vs `direct` to 1e-10, with the default split to 1e-6; agreement with `checks/stack_geometry_check.render_planes` (vertical cut, proportional continuation) to 1e-10; mass: $\iint (1 - \tau_k) = \sum_j \iint \Delta A_j$ for every plane to 1e-10 relative when the grid holds the blurred tube; exact symmetries: a 90-degree rotation of the tube rotates the image, and $\tau(z_k; +1, \theta) = \tau(2c_z - z_k; -1, \theta + \pi)$ (light reversed = depth mirrored); faint limit: partition and linear agree to $O(\mu d)$; $\mu = 0$ gives $\tau = 1$; camera: pixel integration is the block mean, the background of a flat field has mean `black + gain B` and SD `noise_sd_gl` within 2 % before JPEG, values are integers in range, JPEG with explicit tables round-trips the tables (`Image.open(f).quantization`); determinism under a fixed seed; invalid inputs raise. Added 2026-10-07 (`tests/smoke/test_smoke_camera_tables.py`): a configuration carrying tables gives, through `camera_chain` and through `synthetic_block` (the table build's path), the same uint8 block as passing the same tables explicitly, bit for bit at a fixed seed, and a different block from the configuration without tables; the encoded JPEG carries exactly the configured tables; a Pillow version below 8.3 makes `jpeg_roundtrip` with tables and `qtables_from_jpeg` raise
- Data flow: consumes Block 2 (geometry) and Block 3 (C2 oracle); feeds Blocks 5 (synthetic blocks), 6, 10
- Confirmed: yes for the model (procedure s.3.6, D-024); the FFT padding rule, the reference backend's truncation and the grey mapping formula are the assistant's, PROVISIONAL (2026-10-06)
- Status: smoke-tested 2026-10-06 (7 pass; re-run 2026-10-07, 7 pass, the no-table path unchanged): fft vs direct 9e-16, with the split 5e-8; vs `stack_geometry_check.render_planes` 4e-16; C2 exact 4e-16 and vs Eq. 11 2.2e-3 / 5.8e-4 / 6.0e-5 at $p_{\rm x}$/8, /16, /32 (point sampling of the object: 0.4 % of a 0.6 dip at the production $p_{\rm x}/8$ for this flat $d = 0.8$ um tube). Timing (sandbox, one core, block of 87 pixels + 17 padding): 60-degree $d = 1$ um tube, $U = 10$ um, 21 planes: 28 s at `fft_split_sigma_um` 0.5, 48 s at 1.0; flat $d = 0.5$ um at $p_{\rm x}/16$, 7 planes: 11 s / 15 s

### Block 5: the shared per-node chain (handoff Eqs. 1-5, 9; D-018.1; D-023; D-024)

- Modules: `src/allen_diameter/analysis/profiles.py` (Eq. 9 sampling), `analysis/focus.py` (Eqs. 1, 1', 2; D-030), `analysis/path.py` (Eqs. 4-5), `analysis/background.py` (D-018.1), `analysis/node_pipeline.py` (orchestration); each under about 150 lines
- Branch: a `Branch(ids, types, xyz_um (n, 3), radius_um (n,), s_um (n,))` ordered proximal to distal (away from the soma), positions in the global image frame (x, y um = full-resolution pixel coordinate x $p_{\rm x}$; z stage um, plane $k$ at $z = k\,\Delta z$), `s_um` the cumulative 3-D path length. Real branches come from the SWC through the alignment transform (Blocks 8, 11); phantom branches from Block 6 (nodes on the axis every `phantom_node_step_um`, radius = the true $d/2$, D-018.1's "same mask around the known axis")
- Block provider: a callable `provider(left, top, width, height, k_lo, k_hi) -> (block, ks, valid, frame)` in the `fetch_zblock` contract (real: `fetch_zblock`; synthetic: Block 4 `synthetic_block` for one phantom). One call per measured node, covering the blocks and plane ranges of every node the measurement needs [2026-10-20, Block 13.3b: the one request is `node_request`'s, this block over these planes and Block 13's $K_i = k_{{\rm SWC},i} \pm$ `iter_planes_half`; the pilot reads only these planes of it, so its result is unchanged on a provider serving the same pixels for any request]
- Profile (handoff Eq. 9): for an origin $o$ (um, xy), a unit measuring axis $\hat y$ and the branch axis $\hat e_u$ (both in the image plane), $I(v_n) = \frac{1}{2A+1}\sum_{a=-A}^{A} I_k(o + v_n\hat y + a\,\delta\,\hat e_u)$ with $v_n = n\,$`profile_step_um`, $|n| \le$ round(`profile_half_um`/`profile_step_um`), $\delta$ = `profile_step_um`, $A$ = round(`along_branch_avg_um`/$\delta$) (0 = no averaging); each sample bilinear (`scipy.ndimage.map_coordinates`, order 1) at array coordinates (col, row) = $(x/p_{\rm x} - \text{left},\ y/p_{\rm x} - \text{top})$; a sample outside the block is NaN (`mode="constant"`, `cval=nan`), never clamped. The focus score uses $A = 0$ (Eq. 1 as written), the fitted profile uses $A$ from the config
- Focus (Eq. 1, D-030; Eq. 1' = handoff Eq. 1): per valid plane $k$, $\tilde I$ = the profile smoothed with `scipy.ndimage.gaussian_filter1d(sigma = focus_smooth_px` samples, `mode="nearest")` (unsmoothed when 0), $B_{j,k}$ = median of the raw profile at $|v| >$ `focus_bg_ends_um` (`focus_bg_rule = "profile_ends_median"`, D-023) or $\bar B_j$ (`"same_as_bbar"`). Gradient energy: $G_{j,k}$ = `scipy.integrate.trapezoid` of $g^2$ over the samples with $|v_n| \le h_j$ (+1e-9 um), divided by $B_{j,k}^2$, with $g$ = `numpy.gradient`$(\tilde I, v)$ (central differences inside, one-sided at the two ends, taken over the whole profile before the window is cut); $h_j$ = `branch.radius_um[j]` + `focus_grad_margin_um` (`"radius_margin"`; a missing, non-finite or negative radius raises ValueError) or $\max_n |v_n|$ (`"whole_profile"`); `focus_grad_margin_um` $\ge$ `profile_step_um` keeps at least three samples in the window. Dip depth: $F_{j,k} = -\ln(\max(\tilde I_{\min}, \text{smallest positive float}) / B_{j,k})$, $\tilde I_{\min}$ over the whole profile. Both NaN for a missing plane, a profile with a non-finite sample or $B_{j,k} \le 0$ (so they are finite on the same planes). $k^*_j$ = argmax over the node's planes $[k_j - P, k_j + P]$ of the score named by `focus_rule` (`gradient_energy` default, `dip_depth` the comparison), $k_j$ = round($z_j/\Delta z$), $P$ = `planes_half` + (`planes_widen_for_tilt` ? $\lceil (L/2)\sin\varphi_{\rm raw}/\Delta z\rceil$ : 0); the other score is computed on the same planes and recorded
- Sub-plane depth (handoff Eq. 2, `subplane_depth`), on the configured score $S$: the plateau is the run of consecutive valid planes around $k^*$ with $S \ge (1 -$ `focus_plateau_rel`$) S_{k^*}$; a run of 3 or more planes gives $z$ = the mean depth of the run (handoff: "middle of the plateau"); otherwise the parabola vertex $z_{k^*} + \Delta z (S_- - S_+)/[2(S_- - 2S_0 + S_+)]$ clipped to $\pm\Delta z/2$, or $z_{k^*}$ when a neighbour is missing or $S_- - 2S_0 + S_+ \ge 0$. `stack_edge` when $k^*$ lies within `edge_margin_planes` of the first or last usable plane of the node's range (the end of the searched range, or of the stack where planes are missing)
- Centre (handoff Eq. 3): $c_j = (o_j + \hat v_{0,j}\hat y_j,\ z_j)$ from the fit (Block 3) of the Eq. 9 profile through $o_j$ in plane $k^*_j$
- Direction (handoff Eqs. 4-5, D6): $\hat t$ = leading eigenvector (`numpy.linalg.eigh`) of the covariance of the points in the window $|s_j - s_i| \le L/2$ ($L$ = `line_fit_window_um`), in um, oriented so that $\hat t \cdot (x_{\rm last} - x_{\rm first}) \ge 0$ over the window (distal); $\theta = \operatorname{atan2}(t_y, t_x)$, $\varphi = \arcsin|t_z|$, $\hat y = (-\sin\theta, \cos\theta)$, $\hat e_u = (\cos\theta, \sin\theta)$. A window of one node keeps the direction it had
- Passes (`direction_redraws` = $R$): pass 0 uses the raw direction (the line through the raw SWC positions in each node's window) on nodes within $(R + 1) L/2$ of $i$; pass $p$ uses the line through the pass $p - 1$ centres on nodes within $(R + 1 - p) L/2$; the final direction of node $i$ is the line through the pass $R$ centres in its window. `vertical` ($\varphi_i >$ `phi_vertical_deg`): the heading is the circular mean of the window neighbours' headings with $\varphi \le$ `phi_vertical_deg` (unchanged if there are none); $\varphi_i$ is kept
- Measurement: the Eq. 9 profile through $c_{i,\parallel}$ along the final $\hat y_i$ in plane $k^*_i$ of node $i$'s last pass, fitted (Block 3) with $\varphi_i$ and $\bar B_i$
- Background (D-018.1, D-023): $\bar B$ = median of the node's block (`block_half_um` around $o$) in plane $k^*$ over pixels whose centre lies farther than (radius + `bbar_mask_margin_um`) from every segment of the branch (2-D distance in the image plane, each segment with the larger radius of its ends) (`bbar_region = "block_masked"`; `"block"` = no mask; `"plane_downsampled"` raises NotImplementedError); flag `bbar_few` when the unmasked fraction is below `bbar_min_unmasked_frac`
- Flags (strings, `;`-joined in the CSV): `stack_edge`; `faint` (fitted-profile dip $\bar B_i - \tilde I_{\min} <$ `faint_min_dip_gl`); `crossing` (a local minimum of the smoothed fitted profile at least `second_dip_min_sep_um` from the main one with depth $\ge$ `second_dip_rel` x the main depth); `bbar_few`; `profile_nan` (the fitted profile leaves the block: no fit); `steep` ($\tan\varphi_i \ge$ `steep_tan_diagnostic`, a column, never a selection rule, D-024); `vertical`; `dark` ($\hat\alpha_i >$ `alpha_dark_flag`); the fit status (`at_bound`, `failed`)
- Public API: `profiles.profile_offsets(cfg) -> v (n,)`; `profiles.sample_profile(plane, frame, origin_xy, y_hat, e_u, v, n_avg, step) -> I (n,)`; `focus.gradient_energy(I_raw, v, cfg, B_override=None, radius_um=None) -> (G, B)` (ValueError for a `v` that is not 1-D, increasing, of the profile's length and at least 3 samples); `focus.dip_depth(I_raw, v, cfg, B_override=None) -> (F, I_min, B)` [renamed 2026-10-07 from `focus_score`]; `focus.plane_scores(I_raw, v, cfg, B_override=None, radius_um=None) -> {rule: score}` (keys = `config.FOCUS_RULES`); `focus.plane_background(I_raw, v, cfg, B_override=None) -> B`; `focus.grad_half_width(v, cfg, radius_um=None) -> h`; `focus.best_plane(S, z, cfg, dz) -> (index, z_sub, plateau, at_edge)`; `path.tls_direction(points) -> t (3,)`; `path.orient(t, a, b) -> t`; `path.angles(t) -> (theta, phi, y_hat, e_u)`; `path.window(s, i, half) -> indices`; `background.segment_distance(px, py, a, b) -> distance`; `background.mask_near_branch(shape, left, top, res_um, xyz_um, radius_um, margin_um) -> bool (H, W)`; `background.masked_median(plane, mask, min_frac) -> (B_bar, frac, ok)`; `node_pipeline.Branch`; `node_pipeline.node_background(plane, frame, origin_xy, branch, measure_cfg) -> (B_bar, ok)` (the masked-median rule in one plane; public since Block 10); `node_pipeline.measure_node(branch, i, provider, cfg, reg=None) -> NodeResult` [since Block 13.3b (2026-10-20) this chain is `node_pipeline.measure_pilot`, with the same arguments, and `measure_node` is Block 13's: the pilot followed by the plane iteration and Block 12's fit] (`reg`: the dict of `allen_image_align.registration_check`; `verdict`, `lateral_offset_um`, `z_offset_um` are read, a null or missing value as NaN [corrected 2026-10-06: a null s* of a NOT ON node raised TypeError])
- `NodeResult`: the per-node CSV columns of Block 8 before the correction (`node_id, type, x_um, y_um, z_um, path_um, reg_verdict, s_star_um, dz_star_um, k_star, z_sub_um, cx_um, cy_um, cz_um, theta_rad, phi_rad, steep, vertical, B_bar, B_bar_region, d_hat_um, mu_hat_per_um, v0_hat_um, alpha_hat, fit_status, flags`) plus `fit` (the Block 3 `FitResult`) and, from the final pass (D-030; `focus_F` until 2026-10-07): `focus_rule` (str), `focus_planes` (int array, the node's planes), `focus_score` (the configured rule's score on them; `k_star` is its argmax), `focus_depth` (the dip depth (1') on the same planes) and `k_star_depth` (int, its argmax plane, -1 when none; a diagnostic, never used by the chain); `k_star = -1` when no plane was found, which is unambiguous on real stacks (plane indices >= 0) but not on phantom stacks, whose planes are numbered about 0: there `fit_status = "none"` and a NaN `z_sub_um` say it
- Parameters: `MeasureConfig` (all of it), `AcquisitionConfig.res0_um`, `dz_um`
- Library calls relied on: `scipy.ndimage.map_coordinates`, `scipy.ndimage.gaussian_filter1d`, `numpy.gradient`, `scipy.integrate.trapezoid`, `numpy.linalg.eigh`, `numpy.median`
- Custom code: the focus scores (1), (1') as compositions of those calls, the radius window, the plateau rule, the segment-distance mask and the orchestration
- Test oracles of the scores (`tests/smoke/test_smoke_focus.py`, D-030): on a sampled Gaussian dip with smoothing off, $G$ over the whole profile equals $\sqrt\pi D^2/(2w)$ and over $|v| \le h$ the erf form of section 2.2 within the central-difference error ($h_v^2/(2w^2)$ relative at step $h_v$, measured), $F = -\ln(1 - D)$ to roundoff; $G$ and $F$ against loops written out by hand (Gaussian weights, central differences, trapezoid, median) to 1e-12; observed order 2 of $G$ in the step; invariance of both to a gain on the whole profile, mirror symmetry, $G = 0$ on a flat profile, $G w^3$ constant at fixed area; NaN for a non-finite sample or $B \le 0$; refusal of a missing radius and of a bad `v`; on rendered planes (Block 4, default kernel, noise, JPEG; seeds 1 and 2), a dark isolated tube ($d$ 1.5 um, $\mu$ 1/um, centre in plane 0) is found in plane 0 by $G$ (both windows) while the dip depth moves off it, and a thin tube ($d$ 0.5 um) under a thick one ($d$ 2 um, 1.4 um deeper, crossing at 60 deg) is found within one plane by $G$ over the radius window while $G$ over the whole profile and the dip depth go 2 or more planes toward the thick one
- Test oracles (`tests/smoke/test_smoke_node_pipeline.py`): profiles of a synthetic linear ramp image are exact (bilinear is exact on linear functions); out-of-block samples are NaN; Eq. 2 vertex within $\pm\Delta z/2$ and exact on a sampled parabola; plateau middle on a flat-topped curve; the TLS line recovers a known 3-D direction from noisy points (angle error below 0.5 deg at noise 0.02 um over 4 um); $\hat y \perp \hat t_\parallel$ for any $\theta$; D6 guard: the same centres expressed in pixel/plane units give a different tilt (so the um computation is load-bearing); mask distances exact on a hand-made segment; on rendered phantoms with the default kernel (Block 4; $d$ = 1 um, $\varphi$ = 0 and 15 deg) the chain recovers $\theta$ within 2 deg and $\varphi$ within 4 deg (the tilt rests on sub-plane depths over a 4 um window, and $\varphi = \arcsin|t_z|$ is folded at 0), with $\hat d/d$ in [0.95, 1.25] (the thickness halo is the table's job, not an error here); the configured rule sets $k^*$ (D-030): on the dark flat tube of `test_smoke_focus.py`, `k_star` = 0 under `gradient_energy` and -1 under `dip_depth`, `k_star_depth` = -1 under both, and `k_star`, `k_star_depth` are the argmax planes of `focus_score`, `focus_depth` [amended 2026-10-20, Block 13.3b: these checks call `measure_pilot`, whose request spans $K_i$ as well, so the phantom's planes carry another noise draw and the dip depth's plane is -2 there; restated as `k_star` = 0 under `gradient_energy`, the dip depth's plane -1 or -2, equal under both rules and to `k_star` under `dip_depth`]; flags fire for empty tissue (`faint`), a second tube (`crossing`) and a node at the end of the plane range (`stack_edge`); **gate 1** (procedure s.3.10, first row): single-depth phantoms (one slab, constant kernel $\sigma_{\rm r}(0)$), $\varphi = 0$, $d \in \{0.5, 0.75, 1\}$ um, full camera chain without noise, $\sigma_{\rm fit} = \sqrt{\sigma_{\rm r}(0)^2 + p_{\rm x}^2/4}$: $\hat d/d$ within 2 % of 1
- Data flow: consumes Blocks 0 (real provider), 3 (fit), 4 (synthetic provider); feeds Blocks 6, 8, 10, 11
- Confirmed: yes for Eqs. 1-5, 9 and D-018/D-023/D-024; the focus rule by D-030 (user, 2026-10-07), its window rule and margin PROVISIONAL (assistant, 2026-10-07); the raw-direction rule, the pass radii, the plateau rule, the crossing rule, the vertical-node heading, the per-node plane range within one shared fetch, `bbar_min_unmasked_frac` and the phantom mask radius are the assistant's, PROVISIONAL (2026-10-06)
- Status: smoke-tested 2026-10-06 (6 pass, 1 skip). Gate 1 holds with margin: $\hat d/d$ = 1.003, 1.000, 1.001 at $d$ = 0.5, 0.75, 1 um (4 random headings and offsets each; $\hat\mu/\mu$ = 0.98-1.00): the budget $\sigma_{\rm fit}^2 = \sigma_{\rm r}(0)^2 + p_{\rm x}^2/4$ is consistent with the renderer, pixel integration, 8-bit rounding and the bilinear profile. With the default kernel (block 10 um, pad 2 um, full camera chain): $\hat d/d$ = 1.093 ($d$ = 1, flat), 1.108 ($d$ = 1, 15 deg), 1.043 ($d$ = 0.5, flat); $\theta$ within 0.1 deg; the sub-plane depth of the dark $d$ = 1 um tube sits 0.15 um toward the light (the partition's focus shift, impl-handoff Findings (2)). 11-34 s per node on one core. 2026-10-07 (D-030): `test_smoke_focus.py` 7 pass; `test_smoke_node_pipeline.py` 6 pass, 1 skip, gate 1 unchanged in verdict. Phantom check behind the window default (one noise draw per scene unless stated; profiles along the true line; truth plane 0): isolated flat tubes $d$ = 0.5, 1, 1.5, 2.5 um at $\mu$ = 0.5 and 1/um: $G$ picks 0, 0, 0, -1 under both windows, the dip depth 0, 0, -1, -2 or -3 (the partition's shift toward the light); a thin tube ($d$ 0.5 um) with a second tube 1.1-2 um above or below it: with a thicker one ($d$ 1.5-2 um; parallel, or crossing at 60 deg; 5 scenes) $G$ over $r + 0.5$ um picks 0 or +1, $G$ over the whole profile the window edge toward the thick tube ($\pm 3$) in all 5, the dip depth in 4 (+1 in the fifth); with one of similar size ($d$ 0.5 or 1 um, 0.84-1.12 um away) every rule picks +3, and with one 1.68 um away crossing at 40 deg every rule picks 0. Through `measure_node` (3 seeds): same picks on the isolated tubes; on the crossing scene `k_star` +1 (radius window), +4 (whole profile: the wrong centres tilt the line and widen the plane range), `k_star_depth` +2 or +3. At the right plane the fit still returns $\hat d$ = 2.1-2.2 um for the 0.5 um tube: the second tube's shadow is in the profile, and no flag fires (Section 8). A radius used for the window at half the true one sends $G$ back to the dip depth's plane on thick tubes ($d$ 1.5 and 2.5 um); at twice the true one the isolated picks are unchanged and, of four overlap scenes, one goes to the window edge and two move one plane

### Block 6: phantoms and the bias table (procedure s.3.5, 3.7, 3.8; D-024)

- Modules: `src/allen_diameter/analysis/phantoms.py` (draws, phantom branch, one replicate), `src/allen_diameter/analysis/table.py` (estimator), `src/allen_diameter/loading/table_io.py` (files), `scripts/build_table.py` (entry point: chunks for a PBS array or a local pool, then a merge) [2026-10-20, Block 13.3b: `run_replicate` measures with Block 13's `measure_node` (no scene: the phantom's own tube), and its row gains `k_iter`, `iter_status`, `n_rounds`, `n_restacks`, `w_hat_um`, `beta_hat`, `xi_hat`, `zeta_hat`, `irls_ok`, `at_bound`, `d_pilot_um`, `alpha_pilot`, `pilot_status`; the fitted columns are the deliverable's at $k^{\rm it}$]
- Draws, per replicate $n$, in this order from `numpy.random.default_rng([seed, n])`: $d$ (log-uniform on `d_range_um` if `d_log_uniform`, else uniform), $\varphi$ (uniform on `phi_range_deg`, upper end excluded), $\theta$ (uniform on `theta_range_rad`), $\mu$ (log-uniform on `mu_range_per_um` if `mu_log_uniform`), the lateral offset of the node from the pixel centre (uniform on $[-p_{\rm x}/2, p_{\rm x}/2)$ in $x$ and $y$ if `subpixel_offset`, else 0), the axis depth (uniform on $[-\Delta z/2, \Delta z/2)$ about plane 0 if `axis_depth_jitter`, else 0), the node jitter (normal, SD `jitter_xy_um` in $x, y$ and `jitter_z_um` in $z$, per node); the same generator then feeds the camera noise. `design = "grid"` (labelled comparison) cycles $(d, \varphi)$ over `grid_d_um` x `grid_phi_deg` and draws the rest. [Block 14, not implemented: D-059 [user] replaces one realization per draw by $G$ independent draws $(d_g, \varphi_g, \theta_g, \mu_g)$ by these laws, each realized $k$ times with fresh nuisances (offset, depth, jitter, camera noise); D-060 [user]: the spline is fitted through every realization in $\mathcal S$ at its own $(\ln d_g, \varphi_g, \ln\hat\alpha_{g,r})$, the per-draw mean ratios giving the per-draw scatter and the checks (section 4)]
- Phantom: Block 2 `Tube(c, d/2, phi, theta, cross_section_aspect, U_um, end_cut)` with $c$ = (offset, depth); its branch has `nodes_each_way` nodes on each side of $c$ every `phantom_node_step_um` along $\hat t$ (plus jitter), radius $d/2$ (the mask of D-018.1 around the known axis; [Block 14, not implemented: D-058 [user], $d/2 + \epsilon$ with $\epsilon \sim \mathcal N(0, \sigma_r^2)$, section 4]); the measured node is the middle one; provider = Block 4 `synthetic_block` with `pad_px` = ceil(`pad_um`/$p_{\rm x}$)
