- Replicate row: the draws, the `NodeResult` columns, `in_S` and `reject` (the failed criteria of $\mathcal S$, `;`-joined), the seed, the index and the wall time. $\mathcal S$: `fit_status == "converged"` (excludes bounds and failures), a registration category of ON when a verdict is given (Block 11 `registration_category`; [corrected 2026-10-06]: the verdict sentence was compared with "ON") and none of the flags `faint`, `crossing`, `stack_edge`, `dark`, `bbar_few`, `profile_nan` (tilt never, D-024) [corrected 2026-10-21, Block 14.1 (D-030): `dark` is no longer a criterion -- `SELECTION_FLAGS` = `faint`, `crossing`, `stack_edge`, `bbar_few`, `profile_nan`; `dark` stays a label] [amended 2026-10-28, Block 14.1 (D-096): `faint` is no longer a criterion either -- `SELECTION_FLAGS` = `crossing`, `stack_edge`, `bbar_few`, `profile_nan` (`config.SELECTION_FLAGS`, re-exported by `phantoms`); `faint` stays a label, as `dark`]
- Table (`response_estimator = "tps_spline"`, `table_statistic = "mean"`; other choices raise NotImplementedError): Section 2.4; `BiasTable` holds the retained inputs and ratios, the chosen smoothing, the inputs and $\mathcal S$ outcome of every replicate counted in the failure rate (`X_all`, `ok_all`: those passing the `failure_rate_ignore` screens; `n_rows` = all replicates) [corrected 2026-10-06: every replicate], the ranges, `config.full_signature()` and `signature_hash("estimator")`; it rebuilds the spline on load (deterministic). Methods: `b_hat(d, phi)`, `m_hat(d, phi)`, `tau_hat`, `se_hat`, `failure_rate`, `n_eff` (vectorised over arrays of $d$ um and $\varphi$ rad); `check_estimator(cfg)` raises ValueError when the fit's estimator signature differs (procedure s.3.11) [superseded 2026-10-21 by Block 14.4: the table is the local linear fit on a three-axis grid, `BiasTable(axes, arrays, supported, meta)` queried at $(d, \varphi, \mu d)$; `tps_spline` stays as a small-table path on the three coordinates]
- Files: replicate rows as CSV (one file per chunk, `rows_<hash>_<start>_<stop>.csv`; since 2026-10-07 every row carries `sim_hash` = `"sim-"` + `signature_hash("simulation")` of the configuration it was rendered under, the prefix keeping it a string through `table_io.read_rows`); the merge refuses (exit message `merge refused: ...`) when any row's `sim_hash` differs from that of the configuration it was given (`table.check_rows_config`), and counts rows without the column (older runs) as unchecked [added 2026-10-07: a merge without `--config-json` assumed the default configuration and, when the estimator hashes agreed, recorded the default ranges and signature in the table]; the table as `bias_table_<hash>.npz` (arrays) + `.json` (signature, ranges, smoothing, counts: `n_all` replicates, `n_failure_counted`, `n_kept`), `<hash>` = `signature_hash("estimator")`
- Parameters: `PhantomConfig`, `CorrectionConfig` (`response_estimator`, `table_statistic`, `spline_smoothing`, `spline_cv_folds`, `spline_smoothing_grid`, `tau_kernel_bandwidth`, `failure_rate_ignore` (default `("dark",)`, PROVISIONAL, 2026-10-06)), `RendererConfig.pad_um`
- Public API: `phantoms.draw_replicate(cfg, seed, index) -> (Draw, generator)`; `phantoms.phantom_tube(draw, cfg)`; `phantoms.phantom_branch(draw, cfg, rng) -> Branch`; `phantoms.reject_reasons(NodeResult) -> list`; `phantoms.run_replicate(cfg, seed, index, backend=None) -> dict`; `table.table_inputs(d, phi) -> (n, 2)`; `table.row_provenance(cfg) -> str` and `table.check_rows_config(rows, cfg) -> int` (rows not checked; ValueError on a mismatch) (2026-10-07); `table.choose_smoothing(X, y, grid, folds, seed) -> (smoothing, scores)`; `table.fit_table(rows, cfg) -> BiasTable`; `table_io.write_rows`, `read_rows`, `save_table(table, stem)`, `load_table(stem)`; `scripts/build_table.py run --start --stop --out-dir [--workers --seed --config-json --backend]`, `merge --out-dir [--config-json]` [corrected 2026-10-21, Blocks 14.2-14.4: `draw_replicate(cfg, seed, g, r)`, `run_replicate(cfg, seed, g, r, backend=None, d_override_um=None)`, `table_inputs(d, phi, mu_hat, d_hat) -> (n, 3)`, `choose_smoothing(X, y, draw, grid, folds) -> (smoothing, scores, at_edge)`, `run --draw-start --draw-stop`; the rest in Block 14]
- Library calls relied on: `numpy.random.default_rng`, `scipy.interpolate.RBFInterpolator`, `multiprocessing` (sandbox), `csv`, `json`, `numpy.savez`
- Custom code: the k-fold choice of the smoothing and the kernel-weighted $\hat\tau$, SE and failure rate (no library gives them under one weighting)
- Test oracles (`tests/smoke/test_smoke_table.py`): draws are reproducible per $(seed, n)$ and independent of chunking; their laws by Kolmogorov-Smirnov (`scipy.stats.kstest`) on 2000 draws ($p > 10^{-3}$); the phantom branch lies on the tube's axis at the configured spacing; on a fixture ($b = 1 + 0.06/d + 0.15\sin^2\varphi$, $\tau = 0.03$, rejection 50 % above 45 deg, 1500 draws) the spline recovers $b$ within 4 SE at every query point and within 2 SE at 85 % of them, the median $\hat\tau$ is within 10 % of $\tau$, the failure rate within 4 SD of its expectation given the draws; the SE halves when $N$ quadruples (within 25 %); $\hat m = d\,\hat b$; the file round trip reproduces $\hat b$ exactly; a changed estimator setting is refused; two real replicates (rendered) run end to end with the row contract and are bit-identical when repeated; `failure_rate_ignore`: a replicate failing the dark screen leaves the failure rate whatever else it failed, other rejections count, `()` counts all
- Cluster: `scripts/pbs/build_table.pbs` (PBS Pro array, `-J 0-99`, one core and 4 GB per task): task $i$ runs replicates $[i\,c, \min(N, (i+1)c))$, $c = \lceil N/T\rceil$ ($N$ = `DIAM_N_TOTAL`, $T$ = `DIAM_N_TASKS`, which must match `-J`); knobs prefixed `DIAM_` (the login shell exports `CODE` and `ENV_NAME`, which are reported and ignored); conda activation bracketed and checked by its outcome (davinci's `conda activate` returns 1 while activating); `DIAM_DRYRUN=1` prints the command; the merge runs on the login node [corrected 2026-10-21, Block 14.6: in an interactive job, of order 20 min at $10^6$ rows]. Its behavioural test (`tests/smoke/test_smoke_pbs.py`, stub conda and python, decoy HOME): chunk arithmetic, the argv handed to `build_table.py`, the header lines, the reserved-name notes and five refusals (exit codes 2-5)
- Data flow: consumes Blocks 2, 4, 5; feeds Block 7 and the production run (davinci, D-022)
- Confirmed: yes (D-024: random design, ranges, TPS); fitting the ratio, the input scaling (ln d, rad), the smoothing grid, `bbar_few` and `profile_nan` in $\mathcal S$ and the file layout are the assistant's, PROVISIONAL (2026-10-06)
- Status: smoke-tested 2026-10-06 (7 pass); re-run 2026-10-07 with the row-provenance checks (7 pass: the CLI's rows carry the run's `sim_hash`; a merge given a phantom-range change with the same estimator hash exits with `merge refused`; a merge given a smoothing change is accepted; legacy rows are counted, a mixed set refused, a numeric-looking hash survives the CSV). Fixture: CV picks smoothing 1 (CV error 0.00094 = $\tau^2$ 0.0009), max error/SE 2.4-2.6 over three seeds, median $\hat\tau$ 0.028-0.029. Two rendered replicates and the CLI (2 chunks + merge) run end to end. The production table (2000 replicates) has not been run: davinci (D-022)

### Block 7: inversion, flags, fill (procedure Eq. 1, s.3.9; mathematics Eqs. 20-21; D5)

- Modules: `src/allen_diameter/analysis/invert.py`, `src/allen_diameter/analysis/fill.py`
- Inversion (mathematics Eq. 20; procedure s.3.9): for a node with $\hat d_i$ and $\varphi_i$, $\tilde d_i$ solves $\hat m(d, \varphi_i \mid \mathcal C) = \hat d_i$ on $[d_{\min}, d_{\max}]$ (the table's `d_range`). $\hat m(\cdot, \varphi_i)$ is evaluated on `inversion_grid_points` log-spaced diameters; no sign change of $\hat m - \hat d_i$: flag `out_of_domain` (as procedure s.3.9 step 2: $\hat d_i \notin [\hat m(d_{\min}), \hat m(d_{\max})]$); more than one: flag `non_monotone`; exactly one: `scipy.optimize.brentq` in that grid bracket. $\varphi_i$ outside the table's tilt range: flag `phi_out_of_domain`. At the solution: `large_correction` when $|\hat b(\tilde d_i, \varphi_i) - 1| >$ `bias_flag_threshold` (D5), `high_failure` when the local failure rate $>$ `max_failure_rate`. A flagged inversion leaves $\tilde d_i$ = NaN. The table's axis is set by `table_phi_axis`: `"true"` (procedure Eq. 2: phantoms indexed by their true tilt, real nodes by the line-fit tilt) or `"measured"` (phantoms indexed by their line-fit tilt, mathematics s.3.7: the only way the table also corrects the tilt estimate's error)
- `correct_nodes(results, table, cfg)`: refuses a table whose estimator signature differs from `cfg`'s (ValueError); a node outside $\mathcal S$ (Block 6 `reject_reasons`, the same rule as the phantoms) is not inverted and carries its reasons as flags [2026-10-28, D-096: `faint` is a label, so a faint node is inverted]
- Fill (D5, `fill_policy`), along one unbranched stretch ordered proximal to distal: `"same_branch_then_allen"`: a node without $\tilde d$ takes the median of the nearest node with $\tilde d$ on each side (one or two values), else Allen's diameter $2r$; `"allen_only"`: Allen's diameter; `"none"`: NaN stays. Then a running median of `median_window_nodes` nodes (`scipy.ndimage.median_filter`, `mode="nearest"`) over the filled values gives $d_{{\rm final}, i}$; `filled_from` $\in$ {`self`, `neighbours`, `allen`, `none`}
- Public API [corrected 2026-10-21, Block 14.5: `invert_node(d_hat, phi, mu_hat, table, cfg)`, `shortcut(d_hat, phi, mu_hat, table)`, the flags `off_support` and `mu_d_out_of_domain` (a label)]: `invert.invert_node(d_hat, phi, table, cfg) -> Inversion(d_tilde_um, d_root_um, b_hat, flags)` (`d_root_um`: the root even when flagged, for diagnostics; `d_tilde_um` is NaN when flagged); `invert.correct_nodes(results, table, cfg) -> list of Inversion`; `invert.shortcut(d_hat, phi, table) -> d_hat / b_hat(d_hat, phi)` (the handoff's form, for comparison only); `fill.fill_stretch(d_tilde, allen_diameter, cfg) -> (d_final, filled_from)`
- Parameters: `CorrectionConfig` (`bias_flag_threshold`, `max_failure_rate`, `fill_policy`, `median_window_nodes`, `inversion_method`, `inversion_grid_points`, `table_phi_axis`)
- Library calls relied on: `scipy.optimize.brentq`, `scipy.ndimage.median_filter`
- Custom code: the root bracketing on the grid and the fill rule (orchestration)
- Test oracles (`tests/smoke/test_smoke_invert.py`): on a table whose spline is fitted to an exact $b = 1 + c_0/d$ ($c_0$ = 0.1 um, no scatter), $\tilde d$ recovers $d$ to 1e-3, the shortcut's relative error equals its closed form $c_0^2/(d(d + 2c_0))$ to 1e-3 and Eq. 21 to first order (within $2(b^\star - 1)^2$; Eq. 21 is a first-order expansion); $\hat m(\tilde d) = \hat d$ to 1e-9 on a smooth table; a non-monotone table and a $\hat d$ outside the range are flagged; `large_correction` fires above 0.2; the mismatched-$\mathcal C$ refusal; fill rules on hand-made stretches (interior gap, end gap, whole stretch flagged, each policy) and the median filter's spike removal; **gate 2** (procedure s.3.10, end-to-end, reduced): a table of rendered phantoms on $d \in [0.4, 3.5]$ um, $\varphi \le 20°$, then independent-seed phantoms at $d \in \{0.5, 1, 2, 3\}$ um corrected within 10 % -- run by `scripts/end_to_end.py` (hours on 2 cores; recorded, not part of the smoke suite)
- Data flow: consumes Blocks 5, 6; feeds Block 8
- Confirmed: yes (D5 confirmed by D-024; Eq. 20 rather than the shortcut, D5/D6); the grid bracketing, the neighbour-median fill and `table_phi_axis` are the assistant's, PROVISIONAL (2026-10-06)
- Status: smoke-tested 2026-10-06 (5 pass, 2 skip). **Gate 2 passed 2026-10-06** (sandbox): a table of 240 rendered replicates (`scripts/build_table.py run` / `merge` with `configs/gate2_reduced.json`: the default configuration with $d \in [0.4, 3.5]$ um, $\varphi \in [0°, 20°)$, $\mu \in [0.3, 3]$/um log-uniform; 126 in $\mathcal S$, CV smoothing 10, estimator hash 535847535e83cf7d), then `scripts/end_to_end.py phantoms --d 0.5,1,2,3 --reps 8 --seed 777 --alpha-range 0.15,0.9` (independent seed; $\mu d \in [0.15, 0.9]$ so that the test nodes pass the dark flag, as the nodes the table corrects) and `evaluate`: $\tilde d/d$ = 0.993 $\pm$ 0.024, 1.020 $\pm$ 0.013, 1.031 $\pm$ 0.015, 1.038 $\pm$ 0.009 at $d$ = 0.5, 1, 2, 3 um (8, 7, 8, 8 of 8 inverted), from $\hat d/d$ = 1.072, 1.114, 1.122, 1.126. With the failure rate over all replicates the same run failed: every node with $d \ge 1$ um was `high_failure` (the dark share of the provisional $\mu$ range), the unflagged roots already within 4 %; hence `failure_rate_ignore`. Same simulator for table and test: this tests the Monte Carlo, the estimator and the inversion, not the simulator (procedure s.3.10). The under-correction growing with $d$ (+3.8 % at 3 um) is a $\mu$ effect: the table's few retained replicates at $d \ge 2.3$ um have true $\mu d$ near 1 (1.0-1.2, just under the flag) and $\hat d/d$ 1.05-1.09, the faint test phantoms 1.13 (Section 8)

### Block 8: cell level -- SWC I/O, stretches, per-node CSV, membrane area (handoff step 7; D-013)

- Modules: `src/allen_diameter/loading/swc_io.py`, `src/allen_diameter/analysis/cell.py`, `scripts/run_cell.py` (real data, Colab), `scripts/allen_radius_distribution.py` [2026-10-20, Block 13.3b: `measure_cell` passes the cell's scene (`cell_scene`) to `measure_node`; `CSV_COLUMNS` ends with `ITER_COLUMNS` (`iter_columns(result)` writes them, tuples joined by ';'); `pilot_row_view(row)` reads a row as the pilot's]
- Stretches: maximal unbranched runs of dendrite nodes (`dendrite_swc_types`), proximal to distal; a stretch starts at a dendrite node whose parent is not a dendrite node or has two or more dendrite children. Each stretch, with its parent dendrite node prepended when it has one (a one-node stretch takes its parent whatever its type; a lone root node raises ValueError), is one Block 5 `Branch`; every node of the stretch is measured; the stretch is the unit of the fill (Block 7)
- Frame: SWC um to the global image frame um by `allen_image_align.swc_to_full_px` (its equations (1)-(2): shift, optional flip) times $p_{\rm x}$, and $z - z_0$ for the depth (plane $k$ at $k\,\Delta z$, `plane_table`'s index)
- Membrane area: lateral frustum areas $\pi(r_1 + r_2)\sqrt{h^2 + (r_1 - r_2)^2}$ between each dendrite node and its parent; a dendrite node whose parent is not a dendrite node (the soma) contributes a cylinder of its own radius; area ratio = area with $d_{\rm final}/2$ / area with Allen's radii
- Selection for real nodes: Block 6 `reject_reasons` (the phantoms' rule), which fails a node whose registration category (Block 11 `registration_category` of the `registration_check` sentence) is not ON when a verdict is given [corrected 2026-10-06: the sentence itself was compared with `ON`]
- Public API: `swc_io.read_swc(path) -> SWC`; `swc_io.write_swc(swc, path, radius=None)`; `swc_io.rewrite_radius_lines(swc, radius) -> lines`; `swc_io.segment_lengths_um(swc)`; `swc_io.frustum_areas_um2(swc, radius=None)`; `cell.stretches(swc, types) -> list of row arrays`; `cell.to_image_um(xyz, res0, shift_full_px, flip_y_full_h, z0_um) -> (n, 3)`; `cell.stretch_branch(swc, rows, xyz_img) -> (Branch, offset)`; `cell.dendrite_area_um2(swc, radius=None)`; `cell.area_ratio(swc, radius_new)`; `cell.node_row(result, inversion, d_final, filled_from, allen_radius, sigma_fit)` [`spread` removed 2026-10-21, S2.2]; `cell.measure_cell(swc, provider, table, cfg, transform, regs, only, log) -> (rows, radius_new, area_ratio)`; `run_cell.real_provider(fetcher, planes, res0)`; `run_cell.write_outputs(out_dir, specimen, swc, rows, radius_new, ratio, cfg) -> summary`
- Inputs: SWC path; radii (um, > 0, shape (N,)); a provider (real or synthetic); a bias table; the alignment transform
- Outputs: `SWC` (Section 3); written file byte-identical except the radius tokens, which keep the token's decimals (>= 4); `nodes_<specimen>.csv`, `specimen_<specimen>/reconstruction.swc` (radius = $d_{\rm final}/2$ on dendrite nodes, Allen's where nothing was measured), `summary_<specimen>.json` (area ratio, counts, estimator hash, $\sigma_{\rm fit}$)
- Per-node CSV columns (`cell.CSV_COLUMNS`): `node_id, type, x_um, y_um, z_um, path_um, reg_verdict, s_star_um, dz_star_um, k_star, z_sub_um, cx_um, cy_um, cz_um, theta_rad, phi_rad, steep, vertical, B_bar, B_bar_region, d_hat_um, mu_hat_per_um, v0_hat_um, alpha_hat, fit_status, b_hat, d_tilde_um, flags, filled_from, d_final_um, allen_radius_um, sigma_fit_um` [corrected 2026-10-21, S2.2: the column `d_tilde_sigma_spread_um`, NaN until the $\sigma_{\rm fit}$ study ran, is removed with the study, D-057 (ii)]
- Parameters: `AcquisitionConfig.dendrite_swc_types`, `res0_um`; `MeasureConfig.sigma_fit_um` [`sigma_fit_study_um` removed 2026-10-21, S2.2]
- Library calls relied on: numpy; `allen_image_io` (`fetch_swc`, `list_images`, `plane_table`, `HttpFetcher`, `fetch_zblock`; Colab only)
- Custom code: the separator-preserving tokeniser (`_split_keep`) -- `str.split` discards separators; the stretch walk
- Test oracles (`tests/smoke/test_smoke_swc_io.py`, `tests/smoke/test_smoke_cell.py`): hand-written file parses to known arrays; frustum areas of a cylinder ($2\pi r h$) and a cone exact to 1e-12; agreement with `allen_image_plot.read_swc`; write without new radii is byte-identical; with new radii only the 6th token of data lines changes; malformed lines and bad radii refused; the dendrite area of hand-made cylinders, cones and the soma link exact to 1e-12, doubling the radii of a cylinder chain doubles it; `to_image_um` equals `swc_to_full_px` x $p_{\rm x}$ (with and without the flip); the stretches of a hand-made tree by hand and a partition of the dendrite nodes; a synthetic cell (a straight $d$ = 0.8 um dendrite rendered into planes served through `fetch_zblock`) measured, corrected and filled end to end, rows with the CSV columns, the corrected SWC changed in the radius tokens only. `scripts/allen_radius_distribution.py`: pooled percentiles equal `numpy.percentile`
- Data flow: consumes Blocks 5, 6, 7; feeds the I_h fitter (`--swc-dir`, D-013) and Block 11
- Confirmed: yes (D-013; D-024 (iii) for the radius script); the soma-link cylinder, the stretch rule and the registration criterion are the assistant's, PROVISIONAL (2026-10-06)
- Status: smoke-tested 2026-10-06 (`test_smoke_cell.py` 5 pass, 2 skip; `test_smoke_swc_io.py` 6 pass, 1 skip); `run_cell.py` on real data not run (Colab, Phase II); re-run 2026-10-21 after S2.2 (the spread column removed): the same counts **[run, sandbox]**

### Block 9: ray-world generator (impl-handoff (S6), (S10); procedure s.3.10)

- Module: `src/allen_diameter/model/ray_world.py`; selected by `RendererConfig.absorption = "ray_world"` through `render.render_transmittance` (Block 4), hence available to `synthetic_block`, the phantoms (Block 6) and `scripts/end_to_end.py` through a config JSON
- Model (S6): for each fixed plane depth $z_k$ (um) and every lateral point $\mathbf x$ (um), $I^{\rm ray}_k(\mathbf x)/B = \langle e^{-\mu L(\mathbf x, z_k, \hat s)}\rangle_{\hat s}$, with $L(\mathbf x, z_k, \hat s)$ (um) the length inside the tube (Block 2 `line_interval`: squashed section, both end cuts) of the line through $(\mathbf x, z_k)$ with unit direction $\hat s$, and the average over directions whose transverse part $(s_x, s_y)$ is uniform on the disc of radius $s_m$ = `ray_na` / `ray_n_oil` (evenly filled condenser, sine condition, index-matched). Quadrature: `ray_n_rho` Gauss-Legendre nodes in $\rho^2 \in [0, s_m^2]$ times `ray_n_psi` azimuths $\psi_m = (m + 1/2)\,2\pi/n_\psi$, weights summing to 1. Afterwards each plane is blurred by a Gaussian of SD `ray_post_sigma_um` (`scipy.ndimage.gaussian_filter`, `mode="constant"`, truncate `direct_truncate`), the stand-in for the in-focus blur of `checks/optics_points_check.py`; then `reduce`. No diffraction: the ray world tests the treatment of absorption, never an absolute $b$ (impl-handoff (S6))
- Light direction: does not enter (absorption alone is reciprocal). A tube that is mirror-symmetric about $z = c_z$ ($\varphi = 0$) gives equal planes at $c_z \mp \delta$ when `ray_n_psi` is even (the quadrature set is invariant under $\psi \to \psi + \pi$)
- Skipped points: a grid point is set to 1 (no ray computed) when its lateral distance to the rectangle $|u| \le E_u$, $|v| \le E_v$ about $c$ (Block 2 `lateral_half_extent`, a superset of the footprint) exceeds $\max(|z_k - z_{\min}|, |z_{\max} - z_k|)\tan\vartheta_{\max}$, $\sin\vartheta_{\max} = s_m$ ($z_{\min}, z_{\max}$ from Block 2 `depth_extent`): no ray of the cone can reach the tube. The bound is analytic, so tube parts outside the grid still darken it. A tube without an end cut (infinite depth extent) raises ValueError
- Public API: `ray_world.directions(n_rho, n_psi, na, n_oil) -> (S (N, 3) unit vectors with s_z > 0, W (N,) summing to 1)`; `ray_world.mean_inverse_cos(S, W) -> float`; `ray_world.ray_transmittance(points (n, 3) um, tube, mu, S, W) -> (n,)`; `ray_world.render_planes(tube, mu, z_planes, grid, rcfg, reduce=None) -> (n_planes, ...)`
- Parameters: `RendererConfig.ray_na` (1.4) and `ray_n_oil` (1.515) (impl-handoff (S6)); `ray_n_rho` (16), `ray_n_psi` (32) PROVISIONAL (the check script used 48 x 96; doubling both changes a point's value by < 1e-3); `ray_post_sigma_um` (0.08, the check script's); `direct_truncate`
- Library calls relied on: `numpy.polynomial.legendre.leggauss`, `scipy.ndimage.gaussian_filter`; Block 2 `line_interval`, `depth_extent`, `lateral_half_extent`
- Custom code: the direction quadrature and the per-point average (no library averages chords over a condenser cone)
- Test oracles (`tests/smoke/test_smoke_ray_world.py`): the weights sum to 1 and $\langle 1/\cos\vartheta\rangle$ = `mean_inverse_cos` equals $(2/s_m^2)(1 - \sqrt{1 - s_m^2})$ = 1.4470 (S10) to 1e-10; an opaque ($\mu$ = 50/um) flat $d$ = 1 um tube is black (< 1e-3) at its centre in the node plane without the post blur; `ray_transmittance` equals `checks/optics_points_check.py`'s `ray_models` (its G column) to 1e-12 on three geometries (flat, 20 deg, 40 deg); doubling both quadrature sizes changes $\langle e^{-\mu L}\rangle$ by < 1e-3 at three points; faint limit (S10) against the analytic area: for every direction the integral over $v$ of the chord of a tube along $x$ is $\pi r^2/s_z$, so the ray world's dip area at any plane is $\langle 1/\cos\vartheta\rangle\mu\pi r^2$, and the partition renderer's is $\mu\pi r^2$ (normalised kernels), each within 0.5 % ($r$ = 0.5 um, $\mu$ = 1e-3/um, $h = p_{\rm x}/8$); a flat tube's planes at $\pm 3\Delta z$ are equal to 1e-12; grid independence: a tube whose footprint lies outside a small grid darkens it through oblique rays exactly as the same points of a grid that holds the tube (1e-13, no post blur); the `render_transmittance` contract (shape, backend name, $\tau \le 1$, the `reduce` path); a tube without an end cut is refused. Block 4's test: `absorbed_fractions(..., "ray_world")` raises ValueError
- Data flow: consumes Block 2; feeds Block 4's dispatch, hence Blocks 5, 6 and `scripts/end_to_end.py` when a config selects it; Block 21 takes its `directions` for the hybrid's condenser [added 2026-10-27]
- Not built: the matched comparison (S7)/(S8), which renders the partition with the kernel of the same rays ($K_\delta$ = law of $\delta\,\mathbf t_{\hat s}$) and matches $\mu$ through $\hat\mu$; it needs a cone kernel family in Block 4 (a radial, non-separable transfer function). `checks/optics_points_check.py matched` performs it at profile level, and this generator reproduces that script's G column (above). Section 8
- Confirmed: yes for (S6) and its use for checks only (D-024); the quadrature sizes, the analytic skip bound and the post blur's place in the chain are the assistant's, PROVISIONAL (2026-10-06)
- Status: smoke-tested 2026-10-06 (6 pass, 1 skip): ray / analytic area 1.44627 against $\langle 1/\cos\vartheta\rangle$ = 1.44700 (0.05 %, the square-root edges of the unblurred chord profile sampled on the grid), partition / analytic 0.99991 (a 1 um strip of the partition loses 1 % to the kernels' tails, so its strip is 6 um long); an off-grid tube ($r$ = 0.3 um, axis 1 um from the grid's centre line) darkens the plane at $z$ = 1 um to $\tau$ = 0.83 at the grid's near edge. Cost: points x directions chord evaluations (512 directions), so a full phantom block at $p_{\rm x}/8$ takes minutes per plane on one core

### Block 10: kernel calibration, first stage (procedure Eq. 4, s.3.4; mathematics Eq. 18, s.3.5)

- Modules: `src/allen_diameter/analysis/calibration.py`, `src/allen_diameter/analysis/calibration_scene.py`, `src/allen_diameter/analysis/calibration_report.py`, `src/allen_diameter/plotting/calibration_figures.py`, `src/allen_diameter/loading/calibration_io.py`, `scripts/calibrate_kernel.py` [2026-10-23: `calibration_scene.py` (the patch, the far pixels, the planar background, isolation, straightness; shared with Block 15), `calibration_report.py` and `plotting/calibration_figures.py` (10.F) added] [2026-10-20, Block 13.3b: `scan_node` measures the pilot (`measure_pilot`, D-061.2) and `calibration_reasons` reads `pilot_view(result)`; `scripts/calibrate_kernel.candidates_from_rows` reads the CSV through `cell.pilot_row_view`; the selection by Allen's diameter is D-067.4's, S2] [2026-10-21, S2.1 (implemented): the calibration nodes selected by Allen's diameter into a near-field and a far-field set (D-061.4, D-067.4); the far-field scan not built]
- Calibration node [rewritten 2026-10-21, S2.1 (D-061.4, D-067.4); until then: $\hat d_i \le$ `node_dhat_max_um` (0.3 um, "thin"), a field now removed]: node $j$ of a branch, with Allen's diameter $D_{{\rm A},j} = 2 r_{{\rm br},j}$ ($r_{{\rm br},j}$ = `branch.radius_um[i]`, the radius the branch carries at the node: Allen's $r_{{\rm A},j}$ on a real cell, the phantom's own on a phantom; D-061's notation), is a calibration node when `calibration_reasons(result, cfg, allen_diameter_um)` is empty. Every criterion but $D_{{\rm A},j}$ reads the pilot (`pilot_view`, D-061.2, D-067.4): (i) in $\mathcal S$ by the pilot's status and flags (Block 6 `reject_reasons`, registration included); (ii) $D_{{\rm A},j}$ in one of the two sets of D-067.4 (`calibration_set`, PROVISIONAL values) -- the near-field set `"near"`, $0 < D_{{\rm A},j} \le D_{\rm thin}$ with $D_{\rm thin}$ = `node_allen_diameter_max_um` = 0.6 um, for the plane offsets up to $P\,\Delta z$ = 0.84 um, or the far-field set `"far"`, $a < D_{{\rm A},j} \le b$ with $(a, b)$ = `far_field_allen_diameter_um` = (0.6, 0.8) um, for the offsets beyond 0.84 um (D-026); (iii) flat, $\varphi_j \le$ `node_phi_max_deg` (10 deg, both sets, as D-067.4 names it; D-027's tolerance stays open, section 8); (iv) faint, $\hat\alpha_j \le$ `node_alpha_max` (0.5, PROVISIONAL; the dark flag is 1.0). The fit's $\hat d_j$ is no criterion: the kernel being calibrated must not choose its own nodes (D-061.4). Reasons, in this order: those of $\mathcal S$ (`status:<s>`, `registration:<category>`, the flags of `SELECTION_FLAGS`), then `allen_diameter_missing` ($D_{{\rm A},j}$ not finite or $\le 0$) or `allen_diameter_out` (in neither set), `not_flat`, `not_faint`; `criteria_reasons(phi_rad, alpha_hat, allen_diameter_um, cfg)` returns those of (ii)-(iv) from numbers, for the readers of a CSV. `validate()` requires $0 < D_{\rm thin} \le a < b$, all finite and two band values, so that the sets are disjoint and a node belongs to at most one. On a phantom the selection reads the phantom's branch radius ($d/2$ until Block 14's D-062.1 radius) [2026-10-23, D-079, D-081: (iii) `node_phi_max_deg` is 5 deg for both sets (D-081.3, exposed); two criteria added, read from the trace and the pilot (10.I): (v) straight, `not_straight`; (vi) isolated, `not_isolated` -- `calibration_reasons(result, cfg, allen_diameter_um, branch=None, index=None, scene=None, planes=None)` applies them when branch, index and scene are given (the re-measurement before a scan); `criteria_reasons` on numbers, the CSV pre-filter of `candidates_from_rows`, cannot and does not, so a CSV candidate may still fall at the re-measurement. Reasons order: $\mathcal S$, Allen's diameter, `not_flat`, `not_faint`, `not_straight`, `not_isolated`] [amended 2026-10-28, D-094 (the user: "Remove both faint"): two switches of `CalibrationConfig`, both False by default. `node_alpha_check`: (iv) runs only when it is true, so by default no node fails `not_faint`. `node_faint_flag_check`: when it is false, the flag `faint` (a dip under `measure.faint_min_dip_gl` = 6 gl on the pilot's plane) is not among (i)'s reasons, so a node outside $\mathcal S$ by that flag alone can be a calibration node. $\mathcal S$ itself -- Block 6's `reject_reasons`, the table, the deliverable, the CSV's `in_S` -- is unchanged. `calibration.selection_flags(cfg)` returns the flags (i) applies (`SELECTION_FLAGS`, without `faint` unless `node_faint_flag_check`), `calibration.selection_reasons(result, cfg)` the reasons of (i) (those of `reject_reasons` without `faint` unless the switch is true), and `candidates_from_rows` reads the same flags. The order of the reasons is unchanged] [amended 2026-10-28, D-096: `SELECTION_FLAGS` no longer holds `faint`, so `selection_flags(cfg)` is `SELECTION_FLAGS` by default and `("faint",) + SELECTION_FLAGS` with `node_faint_flag_check`, which keeps D-094's meaning for the calibration only; `selection_reasons` lists the flag `faint` first, as before]
- Plane scan (`plane_scan`), for a measured calibration node $i$ with centre $(c_x, c_y)$, heading $\theta_i$ and sharpest plane $k^*_i$: the line is fixed, origin $(c_x, c_y)$, measuring axis $(-\sin\theta_i, \cos\theta_i)$, along-branch axis $(\cos\theta_i, \sin\theta_i)$; for every plane $k = k^*_i - P, \dots, k^*_i + P$ ($P$ = `offsets_planes` = 3): the Eq. 9 profile $I_{i,k}(v_n)$ (the fit's extraction, along-branch averaging on), $\bar B_{i,k}$ by the node's background rule in plane $k$ (Block 5 `node_background`), and the dip $w_{i,k}(v_n) = \bar B_{i,k} - I_{i,k}(v_n)$ (grey levels). In each plane (`dip_statistics`): $n^\circ$ = the smallest sample of $I_{i,k}$ smoothed by `focus_smooth_px` samples among those in the $v_0$ bounds; FWHM = Block 3 `half_depth_width` of the smoothed dip at $n^\circ$; the Gaussian $a\,e^{-(v - v_c)^2/(2s^2)}$ fitted by least squares (scipy `least_squares`, trf, tolerances 1e-12) to $w_{i,k}$ on the samples with $|v_n - v_{n^\circ}| \le$ `core_fit_fwhm_factor` x FWHM (at least `min_window_samples`); $\omega_{i,k} = \hat s^2$ (um$^2$; `statistic = "gaussian_core_width2"`, the deliverable, procedure s.3.4 [corrected 2026-10-04]); the windowed variance $V_{W,i,k} = \sum (v - \bar v)^2 w / \sum w$, $\bar v = \sum v w / \sum w$, and the windowed area $A_{W,i,k} = \Delta v \sum w$, both over $|v_n - \hat v_c| \le$ `window_half_um` (1.5 um, PROVISIONAL). A plane without $\omega$ carries its reason: `invalid_plane`, `bbar_few`, `profile_nan`, `no_dip`, `no_fwhm`, `few_samples`, `fit_failed`. [2026-10-21, S2.1] Only the near-field set is scanned: for a calibration node of the far-field set `scan_node` makes no second provider call and returns no scan, with the one reason `far_field_scan_not_built` (`calibration.FAR_FIELD_NOT_BUILT`); the far-field scan (the planes beyond 0.84 um) and the fit of D-026.1 are not built, their open points in section 8 [2026-10-23, D-078.1, D-079.2, D-081.2 -- the background and the block: the scan reads the patch of 10.P over $k^*_i - P \dots k^*_i + P$ (one provider call of $\pm$`patch_half_um` about the pilot's centre) and measures every dip against the planar far-pixel background $\hat B_{i,k}$ of 10.P, not the masked block median: $I'_{i,k}(v) = I_{i,k}(v) - \hat B_{i,k}({\rm line}(v)) + \hat B_{i,k}({\rm line}(0))$ and `dip_statistics(v, I', B0 = \hat B_{i,k}(line(0)), ...)`, unchanged inside; a plane with too few far pixels carries `bg_few` (`bbar_few` is no longer produced by the scan); `PlaneScan` gains `set` ("near"/"far"), per plane `se_omega_um2`, `amp_gl`, `v_c_um`, `rms_gl`, `n_win`, `passes`, `window_half_um`, `line_end_gl`, `b_coef` (3,) per plane, and the per-plane profiles `v_um` (n_v,), `w_gl` (n, n_v), `bhat_gl` (n, n_v) (D-075: what the figures need), all with defaults so that older constructions and files load; `images` (the patch stack, uint8 (n, H, W), its frame and far masks) only when `keep_images` -- never written to the scans JSON]
- Growth fit (`fit_growth`, Eq. 4 = mathematics Eq. 18): over the nodes with at least `min_planes_per_node` finite values (at least two nodes, else ValueError), minimise $\sum_{i,k}\big[c_i + G(z_{{\rm ax},i} - z_k) - \omega_{i,k}\big]^2$ over $c_i$ (um$^2$), $z_{{\rm ax},i}$ (um, within `z_ax_bound_um` of the node's sub-plane depth $z_{{\rm sub},i}$) and the knot values $G_m = G(\delta_m)$ (um$^2$), $G(0) = 0$, with $\delta = z_{{\rm ax},i} - z_k$ (object minus plane). Knots $\delta_m = m h$, $h$ = `knot_step_um` = $\Delta z$, $m = 0, \dots, M$, $M = \lceil (P + 1/2)\Delta z / h\rceil$; `growth_interp = "cubic"` (PROVISIONAL): $G$ = the not-a-knot cubic spline (scipy `CubicSpline`) through $(\pm\delta_m, G_m)$, or `"linear"`; linear continuation beyond the end knots. `origin_convention = "symmetric"`: $G$ even in $\delta$; `"min_origin"`: knots $-Mh, \dots, Mh$, every $G_m \ge 0$, so its minimum sits at $\delta = 0$ (the shift of mathematics s.3.5 is pinned either way). Start: $z_{{\rm ax},i} = z_{{\rm sub},i}$ ($k^*_i\Delta z$ when NaN), $(c, G)$ by linear least squares at those depths (`lsq_linear`), then all parameters by `least_squares` (trf, analytic Jacobian, tolerances 1e-12). A knot with no observation in either adjacent interval is returned NaN. Reported: the residuals, their RMS by plane offset $|k - k^*_i|$ (the residual check beyond `trust_planes`), the observations per knot interval, and (`area_ratio_by_offset`) the median over nodes of $A_{W,i,k}/A_{W,i,k^*_i}$ per offset (diagnostic: 1 in the model, mathematics s.3.5)
- Knot spacing (finding, 2026-10-06): knots finer than $\Delta z$ are not identified. With linear hats at $h = \Delta z/2$, the zig-zag $Z$ (0 at even, $A$ at odd multiples of $h$) satisfies $Z(z_{{\rm ax},i} - k\Delta z) = A\,|z_{{\rm ax},i}|/h$ for every plane $k$ whenever $|z_{{\rm ax},i}| \le h$, so $G + Z$ with $c_i - A|z_{{\rm ax},i}|/h$ fits the same data (on exact data the fit diverged along it). `validate()` refuses `knot_step_um` < `dz_um`; the procedure's "tabulated per plane offset" is the identifiable resolution
- 10.P, the patch and the planar background (`calibration_scene.py`; D-078.1, D-079.2, D-081.2; 2026-10-23): for a calibration node with the pilot's centre $(c_x, c_y)$ (um, image frame) the patch is the square of pixels whose centres lie within $\pm$`patch_half_um` (25 um) of it, fetched over the stage's planes (`fetch_patch(provider, cx, cy, half_um, k_lo, k_hi, res0_um)` -> the fetch_zblock contract). In plane $k$ the far pixels $F_k$ (`far_pixel_mask(shape, frame, segments, soma, k, cfg)`) are the pixel centres farther than $\max(r_{q0}, r_{q1}) + n_\sigma\,\sigma_{\rm r}(\delta_{q,k})$ from every traced segment $q$ of the scene (Block 13.2's rows; the node's OWN branch included -- its shadow is the dip) and farther than $r_s + n_\sigma\,\sigma_{\rm r}(\delta_{\max})$ from every soma disc $s$ (no soma depth in the scene: the stage's largest offset, PROVISIONAL), $n_\sigma$ = `far_pixel_sigmas` (3), $\sigma_{\rm r}$ the renderer's current law (`model.kernel.sigma_r`), $\delta_{q,k} = \max_{\rm ends}|z_{\rm end} - z_k|$ (the wider blur of the two ends). The background $\hat B_k(x, y) = b_0 + b_x (x - c_x) + b_y (y - c_y)$ is the least-squares plane (`numpy.linalg.lstsq`) through the far pixels' values, refitted once without the pixels whose residual exceeds `background_trim_sigmas` (3) x 1.4826 x MAD (untraced debris; PROVISIONAL); fewer than `background_min_far_frac` (0.05) of the patch far: no background, status `bg_few`. Read on a line: $\hat B_k({\rm line}(v)) = b_0 + b_x (x(v) - c_x) + b_y (y(v) - c_y)$ (`background_on_line`). Verified on the renderer's model (S2b check A3): off by at most 0.003 gl with a trend and a quadrant masked, where Block 10's block median read 2.5 gl low from plane 12
- 10.I, isolation, straightness, tilt (`calibration_scene.isolation_depth`, `straightness_excess`; D-079.1, D-081.1, D-081.3; 2026-10-23): (vi) **isolated** -- for every scene segment $q$ not of the node's own branch (both ends' ids in `branch.ids`: own) and every soma disc, with $\rho_q$ the lateral distance from $q$ to the patch square (0 when it crosses it), the predicted shadow depth in the stage's plane $k$ is $D_{q,k} = \bar B_j \int_{-r_q}^{r_q}\big(1 - e^{-2\hat\mu_j\sqrt{r_q^2 - v'^2}}\big)\,\varphi_{\sigma_{\rm r}(\delta_{q,k})}(\rho_q - v')\,dv'$ (gl; Gauss-Legendre, 16 nodes; $\bar B_j$ and $\hat\mu_j$ the pilot's background and absorption, PROVISIONAL; $r_q$ the larger end radius; both ends' depths tried), a soma disc with $r_q = r_s$ and $\sigma_{\rm r}(\delta_{\max})$; the node fails (`not_isolated`) when $\max_{q,k} D_{q,k} >$ `isolation_depth_gl` (0.03 gl) -- the accuracy the far background needs (D-081.1); (v) **straight** -- the branch points with $|s - s_j| \le$ `node_straight_half_um` (10 um) along the trace lie within `node_straight_tol_radii` (1) x $D_{{\rm A},j}/2$ of their own total-least-squares line (`analysis.path.tls_direction` over those points; the pilot's heading and tilt are not used: their 1 deg error alone would move a line of 10 um by 0.17 um, more than a thin node's radius), else `not_straight` (PROVISIONAL tolerance) [amended 2026-10-27, D-093 (the user: "Relax the straightness. We need just flatness to be strict. The straightness can be removed because we will evaluate only the measuring line. Otherwise relax the straightness as 2 or 4 x r_A"): the test runs only when `node_straight_check` is true, false by default, so no node fails `not_straight`; when it runs, the tolerance is `node_straight_tol_radii` = 2 (D-093's fallback; was 1). On the pilot 529878215 none of Cell 7's 68 candidates was a calibration node; the 57 lines the user pasted all carry `not_straight` at 1 radius, 46 of them `not_isolated` too and 11 `not_straight` alone (the user's Colab output, 2026-10-27; the other 11 lines not seen)]; (iii) `node_phi_max_deg` = 5 deg for both sets (D-081.3). Both (v) and (vi) are applied to the near and the far set
- 10.A, the anchor and its SE (`anchor_from_growth`, `bootstrap_sigma0`; D-078.3, D-081.7; 2026-10-23): $\hat\sigma_0 = (\sigma_{\rm r}(0)^2 + \hat G(\delta_0))^{1/2}$ at $\delta_0$ = `table_max_delta_um` = 0.84 um = knot $m = 3$ (`kernel_from_growth`'s last table value after the cut); ${\rm SE}(\hat\sigma_0)$ = the SD of $\hat\sigma_0^*$ over `bootstrap_resamples` (1000) resamples of the near nodes with replacement, each refitted by `fit_growth` (a resample with fewer than two usable nodes or a failed fit is dropped and counted); both written by `calibrate_kernel.py fit` into the calibration file (`anchor`: `sigma0_um`, `se_um`, `n_boot_ok`, `seed`) for Block 15.5
- 10.F, figures (`plotting/calibration_figures.py`; D-075, D-080.2, D-081.5; 2026-10-23; drawn by `calibrate_kernel.py figures`, shown in Cell 7): (1) **width by plane**, one per stage (near: offsets $k - k^*_j \in [-3, 3]$; far: $[-16, 16]$) -- panel (a) every selected node's $\hat\omega_{j,k}$ against the signed offset in light grey, the median over nodes at each offset in blue; panel (b) the same centred, $\hat\omega_{j,k} - \hat c_j$ ($\hat c_j$ from the stage's fit), with the fitted law drawn over the median (near: $\hat G(\delta)$ against $\delta/\Delta z$; far: $(\hat\sigma_0 + \hat\gamma(|\delta| - \delta_0))^2 - \hat\sigma_0^2$ for $|\delta| \ge \delta_0$ beside the near table); panel (c) the residuals $\hat\omega_{j,k} - \hat c_j - {\rm law}(\hat\delta_{j,k})$, grey per node with the median per offset; the planes the selection dropped marked; (2) **the example node**, the node whose relative deviation from the median by offset is smallest (`example_node`): its $\hat\omega_{j,k}$ with its own curve, and the image of each of its planes (the patch, the line, the last window and the far-pixel mask overlaid), from `patches_<specimen>.npz`; (3) **every fitted Gaussian** (D-075.2): one figure per node, one panel per plane in plane order, the dip $w(v)$, the last pass's core over its window, $\hat\omega$ and the status; (4) **the kernel law** (D-075.1): both stages on one $|\delta|$ axis -- near planes $(\hat\omega_{j,k} - \hat c_j + \sigma_{\rm r}(0)^2)^{1/2}$, far planes $(\hat\omega_{j,k} - \hat c_j)^{1/2}$ against $|\hat\delta_{j,k}|$ from the fitted axes, the two sides distinguished, dropped planes marked, over them the table $\hat\sigma_{\rm r}(m\Delta z)$ and the far line with the anchor marked. The module computes nothing scientific: it takes arrays the script prepares (`figure_inputs`)
- First-stage kernel (`kernel_from_growth`): $\sigma_{\rm r}(\delta_m) = \sqrt{\sigma_{\rm r}(0)^2 + G_m}$ at the knots ($\delta_m \le$ `max_delta_um` when given), $\sigma_{\rm r}(0)$ = `sigma_r0_um` (configured: not identified by the scan) or given; returns a `RendererConfig` (family `gaussian_table`, continuation unchanged); refuses a `min_origin` growth (the renderer's kernel is even in $\delta$; a two-sided growth needs a per-sign table, procedure s.3.10) and any knot with $\sigma_{\rm r}(0)^2 + G_m \le 0$ or NaN. It is a starting point, not a calibrated kernel (status below); procedure s.3.4 step 2 (tune $\sigma_{\rm r}$ so that rendered thin phantoms reproduce the real profiles plane by plane) is not built [2026-10-23, D-080.1, D-081.7: every table written for rendering is cut at `table_max_delta_um` = 0.84 um -- `calibrate_kernel.py fit` and Block 15.6 call `kernel_from_growth(..., max_delta_um=cal.table_max_delta_um)` -- so that the renderer's continuation (`model/kernel.py`: linear from the table's LAST knot) starts at the anchor; the growth fit keeps its knots to $Mh$ = 1.12 um for the fit itself. The knot at 1.12 um rests on off-plane axes only (3 observations in the S2b junction check) and would otherwise start the continuation 0.125 um low at $\gamma$ = 1.21]
- Cell level (`scan_cell`): every dendrite stretch (Block 8) holding a candidate node id is built in the image frame and each candidate goes through `scan_node` (Block 5 `measure_node`, then a second provider call over $k^* \pm P$ around the centre, then `plane_scan`); `scripts/calibrate_kernel.py real` takes the candidates from a `run_cell.py` table (the same rule on its columns) and re-measures them [2026-10-21, S2.1: `scan_cell` logs per node `scanned (near field)`, `far-field calibration node, not scanned (the far-field scan is not built)` or `not a calibration node (<reasons>)`; `calibrate_kernel.candidates_from_rows` takes the candidates of either set from a `run_cell.py` or `run_node.py` table read as the pilot's (`cell.pilot_row_view`): $\mathcal S$ from `fit_status` and the flags of `SELECTION_FLAGS`, and `criteria_reasons` on `phi_rad`, `alpha_hat` and $2\times$`allen_radius_um` (the registration is checked when the node is re-measured); the scans file's meta gains `calibration` (the `CalibrationConfig` as a dict: `to_json` holds no calibration field) in both modes and, in `real`, `nodes` (per candidate: `node_id`, `allen_diameter_um`, `set`, `reasons`, `scanned`); `real` reports the scanned and the far-field counts] [2026-10-23: `scan_node(branch, i, provider, cfg, reg=None, scene=None, keep_images=False)` scans BOTH sets -- the near set by `plane_scan` on the patch, the far set by Block 15.1's `far_plane_scan` (`FAR_FIELD_NOT_BUILT` retired); `scan_cell(swc, provider, cfg, transform=None, regs=None, candidates=None, log=None, keep_images=False)` builds the cell's scene (`cell.cell_scene`, D-064) and passes it; the verdicts read `scanned (near field)` / `scanned (far field)`; `calibrate_kernel.py real` writes one scans file holding both sets (`set` per scan) and, per set, the example node of 10.F (the node nearest the median width by plane) re-scanned with `keep_images` into `patches_<specimen>.npz`; `synthetic --set near|far` draws the set's phantoms ($d$ ranges (0.15, 0.25) and (0.6, 0.8) um)]
- Public API: `calibration.dip_statistics(v, I, B_bar, mcfg, cal) -> dict`; `calibration.PlaneScan` (frozen: `node_id, d_hat_um, phi_rad, alpha_hat, k_star, z_sub_um`, arrays over the planes `ks, z_um, omega_um2, vw_um2, area_gl_um, depth_gl, B_bar`, `status`; `values(statistic)`); `calibration.plane_scan(blk, node, branch, cfg) -> PlaneScan`; `calibration.calibration_reasons(result, cfg, allen_diameter_um) -> list` [the third argument added 2026-10-21, S2.1]; `calibration.selection_flags(cfg) -> tuple` and `calibration.selection_reasons(result, cfg) -> list` [2026-10-28, D-094]; `calibration.CALIBRATION_SETS` = ("near", "far"), `calibration.calibration_set(allen_diameter_um, cfg) -> str` ("near", "far" or ""), `calibration.criteria_reasons(phi_rad, alpha_hat, allen_diameter_um, cfg) -> list`, `calibration.FAR_FIELD_NOT_BUILT`, `calibration.scan_verdict(scan, reasons) -> str` (the log phrase of a `scan_node` result) (2026-10-21, S2.1); `calibration.scan_node(branch, i, provider, cfg, reg=None) -> (NodeResult, PlaneScan or None, reasons)`; `calibration.scan_cell(swc, provider, cfg, transform=None, regs=None, candidates=None, log=None) -> list of (node_id, NodeResult, PlaneScan or None, reasons)`; `calibration.area_ratio_by_offset(scans) -> {offset: ratio}`; `calibration.GrowthFit` (frozen: `convention, statistic, interp, knot_step_um, knots_um, g_um2, node_ids, c_um2, z_ax_um, obs_node, obs_offset, delta_um, residual_um2, n_obs_per_interval, dropped_nodes, cost, success, message, nfev`; `growth(delta)`, `rms_by_offset()`); `calibration.fit_growth(scans, cfg) -> GrowthFit`; `calibration.kernel_from_growth(growth, rcfg, sigma_r0_um=None, max_delta_um=None) -> RendererConfig`; `calibration_io.save_scans(scans, path, meta)`, `load_scans(path) -> (scans, meta)`, `scan_to_dict`, `scan_from_dict`, `growth_to_dict`, `save_calibration(path, growth, kernel, kernel_error, area_ratio, meta)`; `scripts/calibrate_kernel.py synthetic --n --seed --out [--d-range --alpha-range --phi-max --workers --config-json]`, `real --specimen --nodes-csv --out [--swc --cache-dir --shift-x --shift-y --flip-h --z0 --registration-json --config-json]`, `fit --scans --out [--sigma-r0 --config-json]` [2026-10-23: `calibration_scene.fetch_patch`, `far_pixel_mask`, `planar_background(stack_k, far) -> (b (3,), n_used, ok)`, `background_on_line(b, cx, cy, xy (n, 2)) -> (n,)`, `segment_rows(scene, frame, shape, branch)`, `isolation_depth(branch, index, scene, cx, cy, half_um, planes_z, B_bar, mu, cfg) -> (max depth gl, worst segment id or "soma")`, `straightness_excess(branch, index, half_um) -> max distance (um)`; `calibration.calibration_reasons(..., branch=None, index=None, scene=None, planes=None)`, `calibration.scan_node(..., scene=None, keep_images=False)`, `calibration.scan_cell(..., keep_images=False)`, `calibration.plane_scan(patch, node, branch, scene, cfg, keep_images=False)`, `calibration.anchor_from_growth(growth, cfg, sigma_r0_um=None) -> float`, `calibration.bootstrap_sigma0(scans, cfg, rng, sigma_r0_um=None) -> (se_um, n_ok, draws)`, `calibration.example_node(scans) -> node_id or None`, `calibration.median_by_offset(scans) -> {offset: median}`; `calibration_io.save_patches(path, records)`, `load_patches(path)`; `scripts/calibrate_kernel.py synthetic --set near|far ...`, `fit --scans --out [--sigma-r0 --seed --config-json]`, `figures --scans --calibration [--far-fit --patches] --out-dir`]
