- Confirmed: procedure s.3.4 and mathematics s.3.5 (the statistic, Eq. 4, the conventions, $\sigma_{\rm r}(0)$ configured); the selection by Allen's diameter and its two sets [user, D-061.4, D-067.4; values PROVISIONAL], the other criteria from the pilot [user, D-067.4]; the selection thresholds `node_alpha_max`, the windows, the cubic interpolation, `z_ax_bound_um`, `min_planes_per_node` and the knot bookkeeping are the assistant's, PROVISIONAL (2026-10-06) [2026-10-23: the planar far-pixel background in the near scan, near fitted before far, the isolation by predicted shadow with the own dendrite excluded, 5 deg for both sets, the table cut at 0.84 um, the $\hat\sigma_0$ bootstrap with 1000 resamples and the figures' layout: the user (D-078 to D-081); the straightness tolerance, the soma margin, the trimming and `background_min_far_frac` are the assistant's, PROVISIONAL] [2026-10-28: the calibration nodes selected without the optical-depth criterion and without the flag `faint`: the user (D-094); `node_alpha_max` = 0.5 stays the assistant's PROVISIONAL value for when `node_alpha_check` is switched on]
- Status: smoke-tested 2026-10-06 (7 pass). Phase I finding: on 8 noise-free thin phantoms the core-width growth is low against the projected truth by 0.010 um$^2$ at 1 plane and 0.012 at 2 planes (0.003 at 3): the core width of a mixture of defocus values (a tube 0.25 um thick spans $\delta \pm 0.125$ um) is narrower than its mean variance, most where $\sigma_{\rm r}$ grows fastest. Near focus the growth (0.0085 um$^2$ at 1 plane in the default table) is of the size of that bias, so the fitted $G$(1 plane) came out $-0.004$ um$^2$ and the first-stage table 0.080, 0.049, 0.399, 0.604, 0.806 um at 0, 1, 2, 3, 4 planes against the default 0.080, 0.122, 0.438, 0.603, 0.824: not monotone, not usable for rendering before step 2. With linear interpolation $G$(1 plane) was $-0.012$ and the table was refused ($\sigma_{\rm r}(0)^2 + G < 0$). On 4 noisy phantoms (`calibrate_kernel.py synthetic`) the residual RMS by offset is 0.008-0.035 um$^2$. Phase II (real scans, Colab) not run. S2.1 (2026-10-21): implemented and smoke-tested -- `test_smoke_calibration` 7 pass with the S2.1 checks, `test_smoke_phase2` 4 pass and 3 skip, `test_smoke_block13` 6 pass and 1 skip, `test_smoke_config` 6 pass and 1 skip; 27 of 27 mutants of the selection killed (the edges of both sets, the NaN and infinite diameters, the pilot's reading, the reasons' order, the far-field branch of `scan_node`, the CSV rule, the per-set counts, `validate()`'s rule, the defaults); the estimator and simulation hashes unchanged (`0508e7ff12f48728`, `bf65d525963d5f0f`: no signature holds the calibration settings) **[run, sandbox]**. 2026-10-23 (D-077 to D-081; the amendments 10.P, 10.I, 10.A, 10.F built together with Block 15): the scan reads a $\pm$25 um patch per plane with the planar far-pixel background and the flattened profile (10.P); the trace criteria straightness (one radius about the trace's own TLS line over $\pm$10 um) and isolation (a predicted shadow deeper than 0.03 gl from any other traced segment or the soma in a scanned plane; the node's own branch exempt) (10.I), `node_phi_max_deg` = 5 deg for both sets; the table cut at `table_max_delta_um` = 0.84 um with the anchor $\hat\sigma_0$ and its node-bootstrap SE (10.A); the figure inputs and figures (10.F; `analysis/calibration_report.py`, `plotting/calibration_figures.py`). The estimator and simulation hashes are unchanged against `cc60f3c` (`0508e7ff12f48728`, `0ea5a6d2ec069c68`; `CalibrationConfig` is in no signature). Suites: `test_smoke_calibration.py` 8 pass (`test_patch_scan`: the patch scan on a rendered phantom against the block scan of the same noise draw, planar background within 0.1 gl of the block median and $\hat\omega$ within 5 %, the 5 deg rule, the reasons' order, the anchor at the cut table's last knot and its bootstrap SE within 30 % of the SD over 40 regenerated data sets; the JSON round trip with a pre-2026-10-23 file, the synthetic cell through `scan_cell`, the CLI `synthetic` -> `fit` -> `figures` with a configuration file carrying a `calibration` section), `test_smoke_calibration_scene.py` 6 pass, 1 skip (the far-pixel mask, the planar background on a planted plane, the shadow depth, the straightness and isolation rules), `test_smoke_calibration_figures.py` 6 pass, 1 skip; all 28 smoke suites pass on the working tree (0 s to 11 min each, two at a time on 2 cores; no files left in the tree). Phase II on Allen's data not run (Colab, Cell 7). Smoke-tested **[run, sandbox]** [2026-10-27, D-093: on Allen's pilot none of Cell 7's 68 candidates was a calibration node, and the 57 lines the user pasted all carry `not_straight` at 1 radius (46 also `not_isolated`; the user's run); the test is now off by default (`node_straight_check`), its tolerance 2 radii when on; `test_smoke_calibration.py` 8 pass with the new checks, `_scene`, `s3c` and `config` pass **[run, sandbox]**] [2026-10-28, D-094: both faint criteria off by default (`node_alpha_check`, `node_faint_flag_check`); `test_smoke_calibration.py` 8 pass with the new checks; six mutants killed (each switch ignored or inverted, the CSV filter's old flags, the reasons unfiltered); the 36 smoke suites pass; on noise-free rendered near-set phantoms the fitted table moves by under 1 % at 0.84 um and under 2 % at 0.56 um between $\alpha$ = 0.1 and 2 (`checks/d094_2026-10-28/`) **[run, sandbox]**]

### Block 11: Phase II tools -- pilot, registration survey, camera inputs, Colab bootstrap (design handoff Next actions 1-3, 7; procedure s.3.5, s.3.6, s.3.10)

- Modules: `src/allen_diameter/analysis/survey.py`, `analysis/registration.py`, `analysis/camera_fit.py`, `plotting/figures.py`, `loading/jpeg_tables.py` (`collect_qtables`); `scripts/colab_bootstrap.py`, `scripts/run_node.py`, `scripts/registration_survey.py`, `scripts/camera_calibration.py`, `scripts/list_stretches.py` (2026-10-07), `scripts/node_planes.py` (2026-10-07, plane montage), `scripts/plane_differences.py` (2026-10-08: consecutive-plane differences, profile areas and, in the evening, histogram entropies; the measures live in `analysis/focus.py`, the entropy's strip in `analysis/profiles.py`); runbook `docs/TEEG_diameter_phase2_colab_runbook_2026-10-06.md`; the runbook as a notebook, `notebooks/phase2_colab.ipynb` (2026-10-07: it only calls the scripts) [2026-10-20, Block 13.3b: `measure_nodes` runs Block 13's `measure_node` with the cell's scene, `pilot_row` adds `cell.ITER_COLUMNS`, `in_S_pilot` and `reject_pilot`, `node_planes` measures the pilot (`measure_pilot`), `profile_at_node` draws `pilot_view(result)`, `scripts/run_node.py`'s figure the pilot; the node selections and comparisons of `scripts/plane_differences.py` and `scripts/node_planes.py` read the pilot CSV through `cell.pilot_row_view`]
- Bootstrap (`colab_bootstrap.bootstrap(repo_dir, branch, url, pull, clone, depth)`): shallow clone of the public repository on the branch when absent (`clone=False`: FileNotFoundError), else fetch + checkout + fast-forward; puts the workstream's `src/` and `scripts/` on `sys.path`; imports `allen_diameter` and `allen_image_io`; returns paths, commit, Python and package versions. Nothing is copied to Drive (D-020, D-022); Drive holds the HttpFetcher cache and outputs
- Registration verdicts (`registration.registration_category(verdict)`): the 2026-09-23 `registration_check` returns a sentence ("ON THE PROCESS (lateral offset +0.00 um)", "ALONGSIDE: ...", "FAR: ...", "NOT ON A VISIBLE PROCESS (...)", optionally "[... probably a DIFFERENT process]"); the category is the sentence's prefix: `ON`, `ALONGSIDE`, `FAR`, `NOT_ON`; `""` without a verdict; `UNKNOWN` otherwise (bare category names are accepted). The selection $\mathcal S$ (Block 6 `reject_reasons`) and the surveys compare categories [corrected 2026-10-06: `reject_reasons` compared the sentence with `"ON"`, which no sentence equals, so every registered real node would have failed $\mathcal S$; phantoms carry no verdict and were not affected]
- Pilot (`survey.measure_nodes`, `pilot_row`, `summarize`; `scripts/run_node.py`): every node of the dendrite stretches holding the requested node ids (or all), measured by Block 5 without a table; one row per node with the NodeResult columns of the per-node CSV, `allen_radius_um`, `sigma_fit_um`, `in_S`, `reject`, `calibration_node` (a calibration node of either set, `calibration_reasons` with $D_{\rm A} = 2 r_{\rm Allen}$ [2026-10-21, S2.1]), `calibration_set` ("near", "far" or ""; 2026-10-21, S2.1); the summary: counts, rejection reasons, percentiles (5, 10, 25, 50, 75, 90, 95; `numpy.percentile`) over the nodes in $\mathcal S$ of $\hat d$, $\hat\mu$, $\hat\alpha$, $\hat d/(2 r_{\rm Allen})$ and $\varphi$, the suggested phantom $\mu$ range [p10, p90] of $\hat\mu$ (procedure s.3.10, "$\mu$ at the 10th and 90th percentile"), the share of converged nodes the dark flag removes, the number of calibration nodes (Block 10) and, since 2026-10-21 (S2.1), their number per set (`n_calibration_by_set`, rows without `calibration_set` counted under ""), the estimator hash. Figures (`--figures`): the final pass's focus curves against the plane index -- the configured rule with $k^*$, the dip depth on a second axis with the plane it would have chosen, and the SWC's own plane round($z/\Delta z$) -- and the fitted profile with the model at $(\hat d, \hat\alpha, \hat v_0)$ (`survey.profile_at_node`, a fresh one-plane block; `plotting.figures.node_figure`). D-030 additions (2026-10-07): each row carries `focus_rule` and `k_star_depth`; the summary carries `focus_rule` and `k_star_vs_dip_depth` (`survey.focus_agreement`: over the nodes with a sharpest plane, their number, how many have `k_star` != `k_star_depth`, the largest difference in planes and the count per difference)
- Background (`survey.background_stats`, `--background`): in the node's block ($\pm$`block_half_um` about the centre) in plane $k^*$, the pixels outside the branch mask of D-018.1: median, robust SD $1.4826\,$MAD, clipped SD (`camera_fit.clipped_sd`: ddof-1 SD within 5 robust SDs, the robust SD floored at 1 grey level), unmasked fraction
- Camera inputs (`camera_fit`, `scripts/camera_calibration.py`): JPEG tables of the cached crops (the HttpFetcher cache holds the bytes as served; `jpeg_tables.collect_qtables`: distinct sets, most common first, non-JPEG files under `None`); the most common set suggested as `renderer.jpeg_qtables`, the tables themselves, so that the configuration JSON copied to davinci is self-contained, and also written to `jpeg_qtables_<specimen>.json` for reference, with the Pillow version that read them [corrected 2026-10-07: the set was suggested as `renderer.jpeg_qtables_file`, a path the renderer never read]; `background_B_gl` = median over nodes of the masked median; `noise_sd_gl` = the injected SD $\sigma$ whose post-chain clipped SD of a flat field (`camera_fit.post_chain_sd`: $\tau = 1$, 256 x 256 px, the camera chain with the tables found, else the configured quality) equals the median clipped SD of the real background (`noise_for_post_chain_sd`: $\sigma$ on a grid 0-10 grey levels, running maximum, linear inverse; NaN outside the range). JPEG removes much of white noise (an injected SD of 3 grey levels reads 2.13 after the default chain, 0.5 reads 0.10), so the background SD is not the injected SD; the match is an upper bound, since tissue texture adds to the real background SD. Black level and gain are not identified (no dark frame) and stay configured. Nothing is applied automatically: the suggestions go into the table build's configuration JSON after review
- Stretch list (`survey.stretch_table`, `survey.suggest_nodes`, `scripts/list_stretches.py`; 2026-10-07): picks the node list of the registration survey and the pilot ("5-10 stretches: basal, oblique, trunk, tuft", design handoff Next actions 2-3). One row per unbranched dendrite stretch in the order of Block 8's `cell.stretches`: `stretch` (index), `type` (SWC type of its first node), `n_nodes`, `first_node`, `mid_node` (the node at index $\lfloor n/2 \rfloor$ of the stretch), `last_node`, `length_um` (sum over the stretch's nodes of the segment to the parent, the first node's link to its branch point included), `path_start_um` (path distance along the SWC from the tree's root to the first node), `order` (number of ancestor stretches: 0 when the first node's parent is not a dendrite node), `allen_diameter_um` (median of $2r$ over the stretch's nodes), `terminal` (the last node has no dendrite child). The suggestion, a deterministic rule and not a classification: for each SWC type in ascending order, among the stretches with at least `min_nodes` nodes sorted by `path_start_um` (ties by index), the `per_type` stretches at ranks $\lfloor j(m-1)/(k-1) + 1/2 \rfloor$, $j = 0, \ldots, k-1$ ($m$ eligible stretches, $k$ = `per_type`; all of them when $m \le k$; rank $\lfloor (m-1)/2 \rfloor$ when $k = 1$), each represented by its `mid_node`; every id in `include` comes first and replaces the mid node of its own stretch; duplicates dropped; an `include` id that is not a dendrite node raises ValueError. Spreading over path distance within each type reaches the proximal (trunk) and distal (tuft) apical stretches and an intermediate one, without naming them; the table's `order`, `allen_diameter_um` and `path_start_um` let the user name them
- Plane montage (`survey.node_planes`, `plotting.figures.plane_montage`, `scripts/node_planes.py`; 2026-10-07): the planes one node's focus rule scored, each with Allen's reconstruction drawn on it, so that the trace's position, the choice of $k^*$ and a crossing neurite can be judged by eye. `node_planes(swc, provider, cfg, node_id, transform)` measures the node by Block 5 on its own stretch exactly as `measure_nodes` does (the same `cell.stretches` run, `cell.stretch_branch` Branch and index, hence the same single provider request: on real data the HttpFetcher serves it from the cache), recording the block it was measured on; a node id not in the SWC, or not a dendrite node of `dendrite_swc_types`, raises ValueError. It returns `result`, `branch`, `index`, the block (`block`, `ks`, `valid`, `frame`), `k_swc` = round($z/\Delta z$) of the node, `allen_radius_um`, and `segments`, shape (n, 9): one row per SWC segment whose child is a dendrite node and whose xy bounding box meets the block -- parent end $(x_0, y_0, z_0)$, child end $(x_1, y_1, z_1)$ in um of the image frame (`cell.to_image_um` with the transform, the frame of the Branch), Allen's radii $r_0$, $r_1$ at the parent and child ends (the SWC frustum; $r_0 = r_1$ when the parent is not a dendrite node, so that the soma's radius is not drawn as a neurite's), and `in_stretch` (1.0 when both ends belong to the Branch) [2026-10-20, Block 13.2: computed by `plane_iteration.scene_segments` on the cell's `Scene` (`cell.cell_scene`), which `node_planes` also returns as `scene`]. `image_extent_um(frame, shape)` = $((\ell - \tfrac12)p, (\ell + W - \tfrac12)p, (t + H - \tfrac12)p, (t - \tfrac12)p)$ for `imshow(origin="upper")`, $\ell$, $t$ the frame's left and top (full-resolution pixels), $p$ = `res_um_px`, $H \times W$ the array: pixel (row, col) is centred on $((\ell + \text{col})p, (t + \text{row})p)$, the convention of `profiles.sample_profile`; a frame with downsample $\neq 0$ raises ValueError. `plane_montage(planes, dz_um, profile_half_um, view_half_um=None, ncol=6, depth_solid_um=1.0, depth_fade_um=3.0, title="")`: one panel per plane of `result.focus_planes` (all of `ks` when there are none), square, $\pm$`view_half_um` about the SWC node (None: the largest such square inside the block, so no panel shows ground outside the image), one grey scale for all panels (1st to 99.5th percentile of the shown windows of the valid planes); an invalid plane is a grey panel labelled `missing`, without an image. On each panel: the segments' centre lines and frustum silhouettes ($(x_0, y_0) \pm r_0\hat n$ to $(x_1, y_1) \pm r_1\hat n$, $\hat n$ the segment's unit xy normal; a segment with no xy extent as a circle of radius $\max(r_0, r_1)$), cyan for the stretch, amber for the others, with opacity `depth_alpha`($z_0$, $z_1$, $k\,\Delta z$): 1 while the plane's depth is within `depth_solid_um` of the segment's depth range, falling linearly to 0.2 at `depth_fade_um` and beyond; the SWC node and the final fit's profile line (through $(c_x, c_y)$ along $\hat y = (-\sin\theta, \cos\theta)$, $\pm$`profile_half_um`); in plane $k^*$ (red frame, only with a finite $z_{\rm sub}$) the fitted edges at $(c_x, c_y) + (\hat v_0 \pm \hat d/2)\hat y$; the dip depth's plane, when another, in a grey dashed frame; titles with the plane, its depth minus the node's, $G$ and $F$. Presentation parameters are function arguments, not configuration fields, and enter no signature hash (PROVISIONAL, the assistant's). `scripts/node_planes.py`: node ids explicit or `differ` (the rows of `--pilot-csv` with a finite `z_sub_um` and `k_star` != `k_star_depth`; a CSV without `k_star_depth` raises ValueError), at most `--max-nodes`; per node `planes_<id>.png`, and `planes_<specimen>.json` with $k^*$, `k_star_depth`, `k_swc`, $\hat d$, Allen's $2r$, the segment counts and, with `--pilot-csv`, `pilot`: `same`, or the fields of (`k_star`, `k_star_depth`, `z_sub_um`, `d_hat_um`, `mu_hat_per_um`, `focus_rule`) that differ (relative tolerance $10^{-9}$; CSV floats are written by repr and read back exactly), each with the pilot's and the new value; a node that raises ValueError is recorded as `skipped` and the others proceed; a pilot summary whose estimator hash differs from the configuration's is reported; the last line counts the crops read from the cache and those downloaded
- Consecutive-plane differences (`focus.plane_differences`, `focus.difference_dip`, `plotting.figures.plane_difference_figure`, `scripts/plane_differences.py`; 2026-10-08, the user's proposal; a diagnostic that changes no measurement): for a stack of planes $I_k$ in increasing, consecutive plane index $k$ and the $N$ pixels $p$ counted, $D_n(p) = I_{k_n + 1}(p) - I_{k_n}(p)$, $S^+_n = N^{-1}\sum_p \max(D_n(p), 0)$ and $S^-_n = N^{-1}\sum_p \max(-D_n(p), 0)$ (grey levels per pixel; $S^-$ is $S^+$ taken from the other end of the stack, and $S^+_n - S^-_n$ is the mean of $D_n$); a pair touching an invalid plane, or with a non-finite counted pixel, is NaN. The dip (`difference_dip`; PROVISIONAL, the assistant's reading of the proposal): the minimum of $S^+$ between its two largest local maxima, the two ends counting as maxima; the global minimum when fewer than two maxima exist or the two are adjacent; NaN entries skipped. `scripts/plane_differences.py`: per node, the block of the pilot's measurement (`survey.node_planes`, the same request, so from the cache) requested again over $k_{\rm SWC} \pm$ `--planes-half` (default 6; planes beyond the pilot's are fetched, one crop each), cropped to the square $\pm$`--half-um` (default `block_half_um`) about the SWC node [2026-10-08, evening: for the profile and entropy evaluations the square is widened to the measuring line's half-length + 2 pixels, and requested from the provider as such when it reaches past the pilot's block (one new crop per plane on real data); the pilot's block still serves $B_k$; the record carries `half_um` and `square_fetched`]; `--band-um` > 0 counts only the pixels within that distance of the stretch's traced centre line (`background.mask_near_branch` with radius 0). Figure: the planes (one grey scale; frames at $k^*$, the dip depth's plane and the SWC plane), the positive part of each $D_n$ (one blue scale from 0), and $S^+$, $S^-$ at the pair midpoints with the dip. Writes `planediff_<id>.png` and `planediff_<specimen>.json` (planes, valid, $S^+$, $S^-$, the dip pair, $k^*$, the dip depth's plane, the SWC plane, pixels). On synthetic tubes (Block 4 renderer and camera chain, planes $-6 \ldots 6$, centre in plane 0, the whole 10 x 10 um block, 8 noise draws): the dip is the pair $0 \to 1$ for a 0.5 um tube ($\mu$ 1/um) and a 1.5 um tube ($\mu$ 0.5/um) in 8 of 8 draws and without noise; for a faint 5 um tube ($\mu$ 0.065/um, ground 80 grey levels) it is $-1 \to 0$ without noise and falls at random with the camera's noise: the change between its planes, under 0.4 grey levels per pixel, is buried under the positive part of the noise, about 1.2 per pixel, and smoothing each plane by 0.3-1 um before differencing did not recover it. The global minimum of $S^+$ is not the reading for thin tubes: the far planes change least **[run, sandbox, 2026-10-08]**
- Profile-area evaluation (`focus.profile_areas`, `plotting.figures.profile_area_figure`, `scripts/plane_differences.py --evaluation profile`, the default since 2026-10-08, later; D-036, the user's evaluation; a diagnostic that changes no measurement): in every plane $k$ of the node's block (the same planes and request as above), the profile $I_k(v)$ along the node's measuring line -- through the SWC node, across the node's fitted heading $\theta$ (`NodeResult.theta_rad`), $|v| \le$ `--profile-half-um` (default `profile_half_um`, 3 um), step `profile_step_um`, bilinear (`profiles.sample_profile`, no along-branch averaging) --; $A_k = \int I_k(v)\,dv$ (trapezoid; grey levels x um); $\Delta A_n = A_{k_n+1} - A_{k_n}$; a plane that is invalid or holds a non-finite sample is NaN, and so are the two differences that touch it. Normalised beside it (PROVISIONAL, the assistant's): $A^{\rm norm}_k$ = the area of $I_k(v)\,\bar B/B_k$, with $B_k$ the interquartile mean (25th-75th percentile) of the block's pixels farther than Allen's radius + `--bg-margin-um` (default 4 um; it shrinks to 3, 2, 1, 0.5 um until 5 % of the block's pixels are left) from every traced dendrite segment in the block and from the soma (SWC type 1, its radius), and $\bar B$ the mean of $B_k$ over the valid planes; a plane-wide gain change cancels exactly. The smallest $A_k$ and the smallest $A^{\rm norm}_k$ are framed. Figure: the planes with the measuring line; the profiles; $A_k$ and $A^{\rm norm}_k$ on one axis; their differences; $B_k$. Why the area is a weak focus measure **[reasoning; run]**: a blur that conserves light leaves $A_k$ unchanged while the dip stays inside the line; on the synthetic tubes of the bullet above (planes $-6 \ldots 6$) $A_k$ is flat within 1.1 (0.5 um tube) and 4.7 (1.5 um tube) grey levels x um against a noise SD of 2.0, and its minimum falls at random; for the faint 5 um tube, whose blurred edges leave the $\pm 3$ um line, $A_k$ is V-shaped with its minimum at plane 0 without noise and within one plane of it in 6 of 8 noise draws. A 1 % change of one plane's brightness moves its $A_k$ by 12.2, 11.1 and 3.9 grey levels x um on the three tubes **[run, sandbox, 2026-10-08]**. Real nodes 2 and 3 (pixel-difference run of 2026-10-08 16:22, planes 113-125): the mean of $I_{118} - I_{117}$ over the 10 x 10 um square is $-0.92$ and $-1.13$ grey levels, against $-0.19$ to $+0.49$ for the other pairs, so the dip of $S^+$ at 117->118 marks a darkening of the whole square, not a focus **[data read: planediff_529878215.json on Drive]**. [added 2026-10-08, evening] Line length **[run, sandbox]**: the 5 um tube's signal comes from the line being shorter than the blurred tube (defocus pulls ground light into the line); once the line holds the blurred tube the line keeps its light and the signal fades -- without noise the range of $A_k$ over the planes is 9.0, 5.0 and 2.5 grey levels x um at $\pm 3$, $\pm 4$ and $\pm 5$ um, and with noise the smallest area is within two planes of the centre in 8 of 8 draws at $\pm 3$ um and scattered from $-4$ to $+5$ at $\pm 5$ um. The trunk nodes 2 and 3 (profile run of 2026-10-08, 17:46 UTC, line $\pm 3$ um) **[data read: `planediff_529878215.json` on Drive]**: the smallest $A_k$ at planes 113 (node 2) and 118 (node 3), normalised 115 and 119; the line's mean grey level ($A_k$ over the line's 5.95 um) ranges over 59.2-63.3 (node 2) and 65.3-68.1 (node 3), its highest values at planes 122-125, while $B_k$ ranges over 95.4-98.3 and 96.0-98.0, lowest at plane 125
- Entropy evaluation (`focus.grey_histogram`, `focus.histogram_entropy`, `focus.plane_entropies`, `profiles.stripe_mask`, `plotting.figures.entropy_figure`, `scripts/plane_differences.py --evaluation entropy`; 2026-10-08, evening; D-037, the user's proposal of 17:07; a diagnostic that changes no measurement): in every plane $k$ (the same planes and request as above), two sets of grey levels -- the $M$ bilinear samples $I_k(v_m)$ along the profile evaluation's measuring line ($|v| \le h$, $h$ = `--profile-half-um`, default `profile_half_um` 3 um; $M = 2\,\mathrm{round}(h/\Delta v) + 1$ = 53 at $\Delta v$ = 0.1144 um), and the pixels $p$ of the strip centred on the line, $|(\mathbf x_p - \mathbf o)\cdot\hat y| \le h$ and $|(\mathbf x_p - \mathbf o)\cdot\hat e_u| \le s$, with $\mathbf x_p$ the pixel centre (the convention of `profiles.sample_profile`), $\mathbf o$ the SWC node, $\hat y$ across the fitted heading, $\hat e_u$ along it and $s$ = `--stripe-half-um` (default 1 um, PROVISIONAL, the assistant's; 917 pixels at 0.1144 um/px and heading 0.5 rad, 917-918 on the synthetic cell's nodes). For each set: bins of width $w$ = `--entropy-bin-gl` (default 1 grey level, PROVISIONAL) centred on the multiples of $w$ (value $x$ in bin $j = \lfloor x/w + 1/2 \rfloor$, the half-open interval $[(j - 1/2)w, (j + 1/2)w)$; `grey_histogram`); with $n_j$ of the $N$ values in bin $j$, $\hat H = -\sum_j (n_j/N)\log_2(n_j/N)$ over the occupied bins (bits; `scipy.stats.entropy`, base 2). Level: $\hat H$ is computed from the one image of each plane; it is the plug-in estimate of the entropy $H$ of the set's grey-level distribution (a value drawn uniformly from the set's positions, with its camera noise), and reads low when $N$ is not large against the number $m$ of occupied bins. A plane that is invalid, or holds a non-finite value in a set, has that set's $\hat H$ NaN and $N = m = 0$, so every finite value rests on the same $N$. Adding a multiple of $w$ to every value, or permuting the values, leaves $\hat H$ unchanged exactly: a plane-wide offset by whole grey levels does not enter, unlike the area. ~~The plane of each curve's dip (`focus.difference_dip`, PROVISIONAL: the minimum between its two largest maxima) is framed (blue: line, `Hl`; amber: strip, `Hs`).~~ [corrected 2026-10-08, evening: the framed plane (blue: line, `Hl`; amber: strip, `Hs`) is the one `plane_differences.entropy_pick` returns -- by default each curve's global minimum over the valid planes (`--entropy-pick min`, the first of equal values); the dip rule stays as `--entropy-pick dip`. Reason **[data read: `planeentropy_529878215.json` on Drive, the run of 2026-10-08 17:56 UTC]**: on the trunk nodes 2 and 3 (planes 113-125, line $\pm 3$ um) the curves have no W. Node 2's line entropy is lowest at plane 114 (2.90 bits), but its two largest maxima are planes 124 (3.97) and 119 (3.80), so the dip rule searched 120-123 and took 121 (3.41); its strip entropy is lowest at 114 (3.50), where the dip rule took 123, a 0.06-bit wiggle on a rising curve. Node 3: line 119 by both rules; strip 116 (global) against 124 (dip). PROVISIONAL, the assistant's; D-037 annotated.] Figure: the planes with the line and the strip's outline; the grey-level histograms of the line's samples and of the strip's pixels, coloured by plane; both $\hat H$ on one axis with their dips. Writes `planeentropy_<id>.png` and `planeentropy_<specimen>.json` (planes, valid, $\hat H$, $N$ and $m$ of both sets, ~~the dips~~ the picked planes `k_h_line`, `k_h_strip` and the rule `entropy_pick` [corrected 2026-10-08, evening: the fields were `k_dip_h_line`, `k_dip_h_strip`], $k^*$, the dip depth's plane, the SWC plane, $h$, $s$, $w$; since 2026-10-08, evening, also the line's offsets `v_um` and every plane's profile `profiles`, 2 decimals, as the profile evaluation's record does). On the synthetic tubes of the bullets above (planes $-6 \ldots 6$, centre in plane 0; line and strip on the true axis) **[run, sandbox, 2026-10-08]**: $\hat H$ is lowest in focus, rises on both sides and falls again far out (a W). Reading **[reasoning]**: in focus most of the neighbourhood sits at the ground level and the tube covers few pixels; the blur spreads its darkness over more pixels and more grey levels; far out the tube fades into the noise. Without noise both dips are at plane 0 for the 0.5 um and the 1.5 um tube. With the camera's noise and JPEG, over 8 draws: the strip's dip is within one plane of the centre in 8 of 8 draws for both tubes (planes 0 and +1 for the 0.5 um tube, 0 always for the 1.5 um tube), the line's in 7 of 8 (0.5 um tube; one draw at $-4$) and 8 of 8 (1.5 um tube). For the faint 5 um tube both curves are flat within the draws' spread (the strip's mean over the planes 4.17-4.30 bits, its SD per plane 0.03-0.06 bits) and the dips fall at random, between $-5$ and $+2$: the same absence of a focus signal as for every other score. Why the strip: on flat ground with noise SD 3 grey levels, rounded (2000 draws per $N$; reference $N = 2 \times 10^6$, 3.638 bits), the plug-in reads 0.25 bits low with an SD of 0.13 at $N$ = 53 (the line) and 0.02 bits low with an SD of 0.03 at $N$ = 917 (the strip) **[run, sandbox, 2026-10-08]**; the second is what lets a dip of a few tenths of a bit stand out. Not checked: other neurites or the soma inside the strip enter its histogram (node 2 lies next to the soma). Line length (D-038, the user's proposal to enlarge the line beyond $\pm 3$ um; `--profile-half-um`, the strip following it across the branch) **[run, sandbox, 2026-10-08, evening]**, on the synthetic tubes with the global-minimum pick, 8 noise draws: at $\pm 3$ um the strip's lowest entropy falls on the end plane $-6$ in 7 of 8 draws for the 0.5 um tube (the tube fades into the noise there; the line's in 1 of 8); at $\pm 4$ and $\pm 5$ um both curves' minima are within one plane of the centre in 8 of 8 draws for the 0.5 um tube, and for the 1.5 um tube in 8 of 8 (strip, always plane 0) and 8 and 7 of 8 (line); the faint 5 um tube stays without a signal at every length. The ends of the profile in plane 0 of the 5 um tube are at 77 grey levels at $\pm 3$ um, 79.5-79.9 at $\pm 4$ um and 80.0 at $\pm 5$ um, against a ground of 80. [added 2026-10-09] The trunks at $\pm 5$ um (the user's run of 2026-10-09, 12:22 UTC; the picks as printed in the figures' labels): node 2, line and strip 114 (as at $\pm 3$ um); node 3, line and strip 115 (119 and 116 at $\pm 3$ um); $k^*$ 123 on both. Node 3's line curve at $\pm 5$ um has a second minimum at 119 within a few hundredths of a bit of the one at 115 **[figure read]**. Thin-dendrite test (D-039, the user's request of 2026-10-09: the entropy at $\pm 5$ um on thin dendrites, where the gradient energy works): `--nodes thin --pilot-csv` takes the pilot rows that pass `thin_reference_node` -- `in_S` true, $2 r_{\rm Allen} \le$ `--thin-max-2r-um` (0.6 um), $\hat d \le$ `--thin-max-dhat-um` (1.0 um), a finite `z_sub_um`, `k_star` equal to `k_star_depth` (the two focus rules agree, so $k^*$ is the reference), `steep` false -- then `select_thin_nodes`: in each unbranched stretch (`cell.stretches`, proximal first) up to `--per-stretch` (3) of them evenly spread along it (`survey.spread_ranks`), and of those at most `--max-nodes` (12) evenly spread over the stretches (thresholds PROVISIONAL, the assistant's). With more than one node measured, the run writes `<prefix>_summary_<specimen>.csv` (`pick_summary`: per node the SWC type, Allen's $2r$, the fitted $\hat d$, the SWC plane, $k^*$, the dip depth's plane, the evaluation's picks and each pick minus $k^*$), logs `[planediff] summary over N nodes: <pick> within 1 plane of k* in a of n (median |diff| m planes); ...`, and, for the entropy, draws `planeentropy_summary_<specimen>.png` (`figures.entropy_summary_figure`: one panel per node, both curves rescaled 0-1 against $k - k_{\rm SWC}$, with $k^*$, the dip depth's plane, the SWC plane and the picks). The records carry `node_type`, `allen_radius_um` and `d_hat_um`. Notebook Cell 4g. The pilot of 529878215 holds 336 nodes in S with $2r_{\rm Allen} \le 0.8$ um; 91 pass the thin test with its defaults **[data read: `pilot_529878215.csv`, Drive copy of 2026-10-07 15:07 UTC]**. [added 2026-10-09, afternoon] `--hide-line` (the user's request of 14:53, to judge the focus by eye): the profile and entropy figures draw their planes without the measuring line and the strip's outline (`show_line=False` in `figures.profile_area_figure`, `figures.entropy_figure` and `plane_differences.run`); the frames stay; the legend says the planes are drawn without them and that the node is at the centre of each panel (the square is cut about the SWC node's xy, to within half a pixel, and the line runs through it); the records do not change. Notebook Cell 4g uses it and shows each node's figure after the summary
- Gradient energy along lines of three lengths (`focus.plane_gradient_energies`, `plane_differences.gradient_evaluation`, `figures.gradient_lines_figure`, `scripts/plane_differences.py --evaluation gradient`; 2026-10-09, later; D-040, the user's request of 16:19 and answers; a diagnostic that changes no measurement) [smoke-tested 2026-10-09, later]: for node $j$ with the pilot's fitted diameter $\hat d_j$ (`NodeResult.d_hat_um` of the re-measured node, the pilot's own) and each plane $k$ of $k_{\rm SWC} \pm$ `--planes-half`, the profile $I^{(h)}_{j,k}(v_n)$ along node $j$'s measuring line (through the SWC node, across the fitted heading, bilinear; $v_n = n\Delta$, $|n| \le N_h = \mathrm{round}(h/\Delta)$, $\Delta$ = `profile_step_um`) for each half-length $h$ of `--grad-lines-um` (3, 5) and for $h_{d,j} = m \hat d_j / 2$, $m$ = `--line-mult` (2: the line $[-\hat d_j, \hat d_j]$); $B_{j,k}$ the plane's background of the profile evaluation (the interquartile mean of the block's pixels farther than Allen's radius + `--bg-margin-um` from every traced segment and the soma, the margin shrinking to 3, 2, 1, 0.5 um until 5 % of the block's pixels are far), the same for every line; $G^{(h)}_{j,k} = B_{j,k}^{-2} \int_{|v| \le N_h \Delta} (\partial_v \tilde I^{(h)}_{j,k})^2\,dv$ over the whole line (`focus.gradient_energy` with `focus_grad_window` "whole_profile", `focus_bg_rule` "same_as_bbar" and $B_{j,k}$ as its override; $\tilde I$ smoothed by `focus_smooth_px` samples); NaN for an invalid plane, a non-finite sample or $B_{j,k}$ not positive. Each line picks $\arg\max_k G^{(h)}_{j,k}$ (the first of equal values; none without a finite value). The square around the node grows to the longest line + 2 pixels (`node_stack(..., d_line_mult=m)`). A node without a finite positive $\hat d_j$, or whose $d$-line holds fewer than 3 samples, keeps the fixed lines and has no $d$-line; with no fixed line either (`--grad-lines-um ''`) it is skipped. The fixed lines are checked before any node (`fixed_lines_um`: at most 3, distinct, finite and positive). Records: `lines_um`, `line_mult`, `d_line_half_um`, per line `G_<key>` and `k_<key>` (keys `G3`, `G5` for the default lines, `Gd`), `background`, `bg_frac`, `bg_margin_used`, and the longest line's `v_um`, `profiles`. Files `planegrad_<id>.png`, `planegrad_<specimen>.json`, `planegrad_summary_<specimen>.csv` and `.png`. Notebook Cell 4h
- Blend of the gradient energy and the strip entropy (`focus.sigmoid_weight`, `focus.minmax_scores`, `focus.blend_scores`, `plane_differences.blend_evaluation`, `figures.blend_figure`, `scripts/plane_differences.py --evaluation blend`; 2026-10-09, later; D-040; changed 2026-10-10, D-041) [smoke-tested 2026-10-10]: $G_{j,k} = G^{(h_{d,j})}_{j,k}$ on node $j$'s d-line of half-length $h_{d,j} = m\hat d_j/2$, as above; $H_{j,k}$ the entropy (bits, one bin per grey level) of the strip's pixels, $|v| \le h_H$ across the branch and $|u| \le$ `--stripe-half-um` (1 um) along it (D-037), $h_H$ = `--entropy-half-um` (5 um; the user's choice of 2026-10-10, D-041: the strip on which the entropy's minimum was judged right on the trunks, D-039; until then $h_H = h_{d,j}$, on which the minimum fell on or next to an end plane on all 11 non-trunk nodes of the first real run); over $V_j$, the planes where both are finite, $g_{j,k} = (G_{j,k} - \min_{V_j} G_j)/(\max_{V_j} G_j - \min_{V_j} G_j)$ and $h_{j,k} = (H_{j,k} - \min_{V_j} H_j)/(\max_{V_j} H_j - \min_{V_j} H_j)$ (0 at the lowest entropy; a curve flat on $V_j$ scores 0 on every plane of $V_j$; NaN outside $V_j$); $w_j = w(\hat d_j) = 1/(1 + \exp((\hat d_j - d_0)/s))$ (`scipy.special.expit`), $d_0$ = `--sigmoid-d0-um` (1.5 um) and $s$ = `--sigmoid-s-um` (0.3 um), the user's choice; $J_{j,k} = w_j\, g_{j,k} - (1 - w_j)\, h_{j,k}$, the user's form (until 2026-10-10 written $w_j g_{j,k} + (1 - w_j)(1 - h_{j,k})$, which differs from it by $1 - w_j$, a constant for each fixed node, and so has the same maximum). Picks: the gradient energy alone $\arg\max_k G_{j,k}$ (`k_Gd`), the blend $\arg\max_{k \in V_j} J_{j,k}$ (`k_blend`), the strip's entropy alone $\arg\min_k H_{j,k}$ (`k_Hs`; `k_Hd` until 2026-10-10); ties to the first. The square around the node grows to $h_H$ + 2 pixels, or to the d-line's when longer. A node without a finite positive $\hat d_j$, or whose d-line holds fewer than 3 samples, is skipped; $h_H$ must be finite and positive. Records: `d_line_half_um`, `entropy_half_um`, `stripe_half_um`, `line_mult`, `w`, `d0_um`, `s_um`, `G_Gd`, `h_strip`, `n_strip`, `g`, `h_norm`, `J`, `k_Gd`, `k_blend`, `k_Hs`, `pick_keys`, `background`, and the $\pm h_H$ line's `v_um`, `profiles` (`profile_half_um` = $h_H$). The figures draw the entropy as it is, its minimum at the bottom (until 2026-10-10 inverted, as $1 - h$). Files `planeblend_*`. Notebook Cell 4i
- Allen-guided iteration of the gradient energy [moved 2026-10-20 to `analysis/plane_iteration.py` with their bodies unchanged, Block 13.1; the script imports them under the same names] (`plane_differences.iterate_planes`, `plane_differences.refit_at_plane`, `plane_differences.gradient_iteration`, `figures.iteration_figure`, `scripts/plane_differences.py --evaluation iterate`; 2026-10-10; D-042, the user's request of 15:30 and the option chosen; a diagnostic that changes no measurement) [smoke-tested 2026-10-10]: for node $j$ with Allen's radius $r_{A,j}$ (`allen_radius_um`), the multiplier $m$ = `--line-mult` (~~2~~ [changed 2026-10-12, D-044: 3, the default of this evaluation (`ITER_LINE_MULT`), the lines $\pm 1.5\,d$; the gradient and blend evaluations keep 2, `D_LINE_MULT`, D-040] [smoke-tested 2026-10-12]) and at most $R$ = `--max-rounds` (5) rounds, $d^{(0)}_j = 2 r_{A,j}$ and, in round $i = 1, \ldots, R$: the line of half-length $h_{j,i} = m\, d^{(i-1)}_j / 2$ (through the SWC node, across the fitted heading, as in the gradient evaluation), $G^{(h_{j,i})}_{j,k}$ over the whole line with the gradient evaluation's background $B_{j,k}$ (the same in every round), and the plane $k_{j,i} = \arg\max_k G^{(h_{j,i})}_{j,k}$ over $k_{\rm SWC} \pm$ `--planes-half` (the first of equal values). If $k_{j,i} \in \{k_{j,1}, \ldots, k_{j,i-1}\}$ the iteration stops -- `converged` when $k_{j,i} = k_{j,i-1}$, `cycle` otherwise -- and the fit already made at that plane is reused; else $d^{(i)}_j = \hat d_j(k_{j,i})$, the deliverable fit (`fit.fit_profile`, D-019.1) at plane $k_{j,i}$ of the node's block with every other input the pilot's: the measurement's offsets $v_n$ ($\pm$`profile_half_um`, step $\Delta$), the profile through the pilot's fitted centre $(c_{x,j}, c_{y,j})$ across its heading $\theta_j$, averaged over the $2A + 1$ lines along the branch (`profiles.sample_profile(..., n_along, profile_step_um)`), its tilt $\phi_j$, and $\bar B_{j,k}$ = `node_pipeline.node_background` of plane $k$ (the masked median of the node's block about the SWC node) -- so that at $k = k^*_j$ the fit is the pilot's own, $\hat d_j(k^*_j) = \hat d_j$. It stops with `fit_failed` when that fit returns no finite positive $d$ (a profile sample outside the block, $\bar B_{j,k} \le 0$), `no_plane` when $G$ has no finite value, `line_short` when the line would hold fewer than 3 samples, `square_small` when the square cannot be made to hold the line, and `max_rounds` after $R$ rounds without a repeat; a fit at a bound (`at_bound`) sizes the next line like any other. Results: `k_allen` $= k_{j,1}$; `k_iter`, the plane of the last round that picked one; `d_iter_um`, the fit there (NaN when it failed); `iter_status`; `n_rounds`; and, for comparison, `k_Gd`, the pick of the line sized from the pilot's $\hat d_j$ (Cell 4h's d-line: `gradient_evaluation` with no fixed line) [corrected 2026-10-12, D-044: at the iteration's own $m$, so Cell 4h's d-line only when $m$ = 2]. The square holds the Allen line and the pilot's d-line (`node_stack(..., allen_line_mult=m)`) and is made again, larger, when a later round's line does not fit (`n_restacks`; from the cached block when it lies inside it). A node without a finite positive Allen radius is skipped; $m$ (finite, positive) and $R \ge 1$ are checked before any node. PROVISIONAL, the assistant's reading of the option the user chose: "the plane repeats" taken as any earlier plane of the node's own trajectory; "fit there" holding the pilot's centre, heading, tilt and offsets fixed; round 1 counted among the $R$. Records: `line_mult`, `max_rounds`, `d_start_um`, `rounds` (per round `round`, `d_in_um`, `d_in_k` (null in round 1, else the plane whose fit sized the line), `half_um`, `n_samples`, `k`, `G`, `d_hat_um`, `fit_status`, `v0_hat_um`, `mu_hat_per_um`, `B_bar`, `bbar_ok`, `refit`), `iter_status`, `n_rounds`, `k_allen`, `k_iter`, `d_iter_um`, `G_allen`, `G_iter`, `G_Gd`, `k_Gd`, `d_line_half_um`, `trajectory`, `iter_note`, `n_restacks`, `background`, `bg_frac`, `bg_margin_used`, and the longest line's `v_um`, `profiles` (`profile_half_um`). Files `planeiter_<id>.png`, `planeiter_<specimen>.json`, `planeiter_summary_<specimen>.csv` (the three picks, each minus $k^*$, with `d_iter_um`, `iter_status`, `n_rounds`, `n_restacks`, `trajectory`) and `.png`. Notebook Cell 4j
- The iteration's planes with the line that chose them and the diameter fitted there (`scripts/iteration_fits.py`, `plane_differences.refit_at_plane(..., with_profile=True)`, `figures.iteration_fit_figure`, `figures.iteration_fits_overview`; notebook Cell 4k; 2026-10-12; D-045, the user's request of 17:58 and answers; a figure that changes no measurement) [smoke-tested 2026-10-12]: for each node $j$ of a Cell 4j record with a plane $k^{\rm it}_j$ (`k_iter`), let $i^\ast_j$ be the last round of its trajectory that picked a plane ($k_{j,i^\ast_j} = k^{\rm it}_j$; at convergence the repeat round, whose line is sized from $d^{\rm it}_j$ itself) and $h^{\rm sel}_j = h_{j,i^\ast_j}$ (um) its half-length; $o_j \in \mathbb{R}^2$ (um) the SWC node's image-plane position (`node_stack`'s `o`), $\theta_j$ the pilot's heading, $\hat y_j = (-\sin\theta_j, \cos\theta_j)$, $\hat e_{u,j} = (\cos\theta_j, \sin\theta_j)$. The line that chose the plane is $L_j = \{o_j + v\,\hat y_j : |v| \le h^{\rm sel}_j\}$ (the user's answer: "the line chosen to select the focal plane"). The diameter is Block 5's fit at $k^{\rm it}_j$ (`refit_at_plane`): $d^{\rm it}_j = \hat d_j(k^{\rm it}_j)$ and $\hat v_{0,j}(k^{\rm it}_j)$, measured along $\hat y_j$ from the pilot's fitted centre $c_j$ (`cx_um`, `cy_um`); with $s_j = (c_j - o_j)\cdot\hat y_j$ (um) its edges lie on $L_j$ at $v^{\pm}_j = s_j + \hat v_{0,j}(k^{\rm it}_j) \pm d^{\rm it}_j / 2$, the points $E^{\pm}_j = o_j + v^{\pm}_j\,\hat y_j$ (the caliper; none when $d^{\rm it}_j$ is not finite). The fit's line through $c_j$ is parallel to $L_j$, $u_j = (c_j - o_j)\cdot\hat e_{u,j}$ (um) apart along the branch (reported; $c_j = o_j + \hat v_0\,\hat y$ of the pilot's last pass, so $u_j$ is small). Per node, the square is rebuilt as Cell 4j's last one (`node_stack` with the record's planes, `line_half_um` = the record's `profile_half_um`, the longest line, `d_line_mult` = `allen_line_mult` = the record's `line_mult`; its half must equal the record's `half_um`) and the fit is redone at $k^{\rm it}_j$ (its $d$ must equal the record's `d_iter_um` to the last bit); a difference is reported in the node's notes, not hidden. Figures: per node `planefit_<id>.png`, the plane $k^{\rm it}_j$ of the square on its own grey scale (1st-99.5th percentile) with $L_j$ (black, white halo), the caliper $E^-_j E^+_j$ (blue, white halo, end ticks along $\hat e_{u,j}$) and the SWC node, beside the profile panel against $v$ on $L_j$: the profile of $k^{\rm it}_j$ along $L_j$ (bilinear, no average; grey), the fit's profile (the 9-line average through $c_j$ over $|v_n| \le$ `profile_half_um`, drawn at $s_j + v_n$; black dots), the fitted model $I_{\rm model}(v_n \mid d^{\rm it}_j, \hat\alpha_j, \hat v_{0,j})$ (`tube_model.model_profile`, $\hat\alpha_j = \hat\mu_j d^{\rm it}_j / \cos\phi_j$, `sigma_fit_um`, $\bar B_j(k^{\rm it}_j)$; blue), $\bar B_j(k^{\rm it}_j)$ (dashed), $v^{\pm}_j$ (blue verticals) and the fit's window (shaded); `planefit_overview_<specimen>.png`, every node's plane with $L_j$ and the caliper in the records' order (Cell 4j's, by the pilot's $\hat d$); `planefit_<specimen>.json` (per node `k_iter`, `k_star`, `k_swc`, `d_iter_um`, `d_refit_um`, `fit_status`, `v0_hat_um`, `s_um`, `u_um`, `edges_v_um`, `edges_xy_um`, `line_xy_um`, `sel_round`, `sel_half_um`, `line_mult`, `iter_status`, `d_hat_um` (the pilot's), `allen_2r_um`, `square_half_um`, `notes`, `png`). A record without `k_iter`, or skipped by Cell 4j, is listed with its reason and drawn nowhere. Notebook Cell 4k reads Cell 4j's run at $m$ = 3 (`pilot/planeiter/bydiameter_m3/`, D-044; a folder variable) and writes into its `fits/`.
- Fit variants at the iteration's plane: a weighted fit and other blur-kernel families (`model/blur_kernels.py`, `tube_model.model_profile_kernel`, `analysis/fit_variants.py`, `scripts/fit_variants.py`, `figures.fit_variants_node_figure`, `figures.fit_variants_summary_figure`; notebook Cell 4l; 2026-10-13; D-046, the user's request of 14:50 and answers; a diagnostic that changes no measurement and leaves Block 5's fit, its Gaussian kernel (D-026) and the estimator hash as they are) [smoke-tested 2026-10-13]: for each node $j$ of a Cell 4j record with a plane $k^{\rm it}_j$, the inputs are Block 5's at that plane (`refit_at_plane(..., with_profile=True)` on Cell 4j's last square, rebuilt by `iteration_fits.node_square`): the samples $(v_n, I_n)$, $n = 1, \ldots, N$ ($v_n$ um from the pilot's fitted centre along $\hat y_j$, $|v_n| \le$ `profile_half_um`; $I_n$ grey levels, the average of $2A+1$ parallel lines), the tilt $\phi_j$ and $\bar B_j(k^{\rm it}_j)$; the plane is not re-chosen. The model is (B3.1) with the Gaussian $g_\sigma$ replaced by a kernel $g$ of unit integral: $I_{\rm model}(v \mid d, \mu, v_0; g) = \bar B\,[1 - (d/2) \int_{-\pi/2}^{\pi/2} (1 - e^{-\alpha \cos\psi}) \cos\psi\; g(v - v_0 - (d/2)\sin\psi)\, d\psi]$, $\alpha = \mu d / \cos\phi_j$, evaluated with the Gauss-Legendre rule of Block 3 (`model_profile_kernel`; equal to `model_profile` for the Gaussian to 1e-11 grey levels). A kernel is a family $F$, a full width at half maximum $w$ (um) and a shape $s$: $g(z) = f_F(z / a + m_F(s); s) / a$, $a = w / W_F(s)$, with $f_F(\cdot; s)$ the family's density at unit scale, $W_F(s)$ its FWHM (closed forms: gauss $2\sqrt{2 \ln 2}$, lorentz 2, student $2\sqrt{\nu\,(2^{2/(\nu+1)} - 1)}$, gennorm $2 (\ln 2)^{1/\beta}$; numeric for voigt and skewnorm, `scipy.optimize.brentq` on each side of the mode) and $m_F(s)$ its mode (0 except for skewnorm, the root of $-z + a\,\varphi(a z) / \Phi(a z)$, $\varphi$ and $\Phi$ the standard normal density and distribution), so that $g$ has unit integral, FWHM $w$ and its maximum at $z = 0$ (PROVISIONAL, the assistant's: the mode, not the mean, sits at $v_0$, where an infinitely thin line's blurred image is darkest). Families (scipy): `gauss` (normal), `lorentz` (Cauchy), `voigt` (`scipy.special.voigt_profile`, $s = r$, the ratio of the Lorentzian component's FWHM to the Gaussian component's), `student` ($s = \nu$), `gennorm` (generalized normal, $s = \beta$: 1 Laplace, 2 Gaussian, large flat-topped), `skewnorm` ($s = a$, the skew). Width: fixed at $w = 2\sqrt{2 \ln 2}\, \sigma_{\rm fit}$ (0.233 um), the FWHM of Block 5's Gaussian, or fitted in [0.1, 2.0] um. Shape: fixed at $r = 1$, $\nu = 3$, $\beta \in \{1, 4\}$, or fitted ($\log_{10} r \in [-2, 2]$, $\log_{10} \nu \in [0, 2]$, $\beta \in [0.5, 8]$); the skew $a \in [-10, 10]$ is always fitted (its sign is not known in advance). The ten kernels, in this order: gauss, lorentz, voigt $r$ = 1, student $\nu$ = 3, gennorm $\beta$ = 1, gennorm $\beta$ = 4, voigt $r$ fitted, student $\nu$ fitted, gennorm $\beta$ fitted, skewnorm $a$ fitted. Weights: none, or $\omega_n = \exp(-|v_n - v_0| / \lambda)$ with $\lambda \in \{0.25, 0.5, 1, 2\}$ um, centred on the fit's own axis (the user's choice) by iteratively re-weighted least squares: $\omega^{(t)}_n = \exp(-|v_n - v_0^{(t)}| / \lambda)$, $\theta^{(t+1)} = \arg\min_\theta \sum_n \omega^{(t)}_n [(I_{\rm model}(v_n \mid \theta) - I_n) / \bar B]^2$ from $\theta^{(t)}$, $\theta^{(0)}$ the unweighted fit of the same kernel and width mode, until $|v_0^{(t+1)} - v_0^{(t)}|$ and $|d^{(t+1)} - d^{(t)}|$ are both at most 1e-4 um (`irls_ok`), or after 30 iterations (`irls_ok` false); a run that does not converge ends it (status `failed`). The fixed point is that of the user's rule, not the joint minimum of $\sum_n \omega_n(v_0)\, r_n(\theta)^2$ over $v_0$ (which would let $v_0$ run to where the weights see background). Grid: 10 kernels x 2 width modes x 5 weightings = 100 variants per node. $\theta = (d, \mu, v_0)$, plus $w$ and the shape's parameter when fitted; $d$, $\mu$, $v_0$ in Block 5's bounds; `scipy.optimize.least_squares` (trf, 2-point Jacobian, `x_scale="jac"`, Block 5's tolerances, at most max(`fit_max_nfev`, 100 x the number of parameters) evaluations). Unweighted fits start from Block 5's start points (`fit.start_points`) with $w_0$ the fixed width and the shape at the family's member nearest the Gaussian ($r$ = 0.05, $\nu$ = 30, $\beta$ = 2, $a$ = 0), and from warm starts: a fitted shape at the fixed width also from the family's fixed members and from the Gaussian, a fitted width also from the same kernel at the fixed width; the converged run of least cost is reported (the first in start order on ties), else the finite run of least cost with status `failed`, as Block 5 (PROVISIONAL, the assistant's). Quadrature: `n_quadrature_nodes(d_hi, w_lo / (2\sqrt{2 \ln 2}), ...)`, $w_{\rm lo}$ the narrowest width the fit can take: $\sigma_{\rm fit}$'s FWHM at fixed width (364 nodes, Block 5's), 0.1 um when the width is fitted (848 nodes). A generalized normal with $\beta \le 1$ has a cusp at its peak where the rule converges slowly (0.05-0.27 grey levels from a direct convolution at $w$ = 0.1 um, $d$ = 2 um); tripling or sextupling the nodes moved its fitted $d$ by at most 0.003 um on synthetic profiles, so the rule is kept [run, sandbox]. The variant gauss, fixed width, no weights is Block 5's fit (the baseline, $d_{\rm base}$; equal to `fit.fit_profile` to 1e-6 um, observed 2e-10 to 4e-10 um). Per node and variant (`fit_variants.variant_row`): `variant`, `family`, `kernel`, `shape_mode`, `width_mode`, `weight`, `lam_um`, `d_um`, `mu_per_um`, `v0_um`, `fwhm_um`, `shape`, `alpha_hat`, `status` (converged / at_bound / failed, as Block 5), `at_bound`, `rss_w_gl2` and `rss_gl2` (grey levels squared, with the last iteration's weights and without), `n_irls`, `irls_ok`, `nfev`, `n_starts`, `best_start`, `n_nodes`, `d_base_um`, `ratio` = `d_um` / `d_base_um`. Files in `ITER_DIR/fitvar/`: `fitvar_<specimen>.csv` (one row per node and variant, with `node_id`, `k_iter`, `k_star`, `k_swc`, `d_iter_um`), `fitvar_<specimen>.json` (per node those, `d_block5_um`, `d_base_um`, `phi_rad`, `B_bar`, `v_um`, `profile`, `variants`, `notes`, `png`, `seconds`; a skipped node with its reason), per node `fitvar_<id>.png` (three columns against $v$: every kernel unweighted at the fixed width; every kernel unweighted at a fitted width; the Gaussian at the fixed width, unweighted and for each $\lambda$, with the weights below; above each, the fits' edges $v_0 \pm d/2$ as stacked calipers with their $d$; below, $d$ of every variant as a table coloured by $d / d_{\rm base}$ on a log scale, flagged * at a bound, x failed, ~ IRLS not settled) and `fitvar_summary_<specimen>.png` ($d / d_{\rm base}$ against $d_{\rm base}$, both log, one panel per kernel and width mode, one series per weighting, hollow where flagged). A note when Block 5's fit at $k^{\rm it}$ differs from Cell 4j's `d_iter_um`, or the baseline from Block 5's fit by more than 1e-6 um. CLI `--families` (the Gaussian always kept: it is the baseline and the weighted column), `--lambdas` (empty: unweighted only), `--width-modes`, `--nodes`. Notebook Cell 4l reads Cell 4j's run at $m$ = 3 (`ITER_DIR`) and writes into `ITER_DIR/fitvar/`; 25-75 s per node on one CPU of the sandbox, the longer on thick tubes [run, sandbox]. Seen on synthetic profiles [run, sandbox], a finding, not a rule: when the profile's true blur is wider than $\sigma_{\rm fit}$ (a Gaussian of FWHM 0.5 um), Block 5 overestimates a 0.6 um tube (0.82 um) and the Gaussian of fitted width comes nearer (0.70 um, FWHM 0.43 um); when a darker neighbour merges with the dip, the unweighted fit takes both as one tube and the re-centred weights at $\lambda$ = 0.25 um can move the fit onto the neighbour
- Each fit variant's own Allen-guided iteration (`scripts/variant_iteration.py`, `fit_variants.PlaneFits`, `plane_differences.gradient_iteration(..., fit_at=...)`, `figures.variant_stack_figure`, `figures.variant_planes_figure`; notebook Cell 4m; 2026-10-14; D-047, the user's request of 15:35 and three answers; a diagnostic that changes no measurement) [smoke-tested 2026-10-14]: for each node $j$ of a Cell 4j record with a plane, and each variant $V$ of the fit-variant grid at the fitted width (the user's "one 50-row caliper stack": 10 kernels x unweighted and $\lambda \in \{0.25, 0.5, 1, 2\}$ um; `--families`, `--lambdas`, `--width-modes` restrict or extend it), $V$'s own iteration (the user's "each of the 50, own iteration"): Cell 4j's rule (`gradient_iteration`, D-042) with Cell 4j's own multiplier $m$ and round limit $R$ read from the record (`line_mult`, `max_rounds`: the user's "inherited", 3 and 5), its squares (`node_stack` with the record's planes and `d_line_mult` = `allen_line_mult` = $m$, made larger for longer lines) and its background ($B_k$ of `block_background` with the record's `bg_margin_um`): $d^{(0)}_{j,V} = 2 r_{A,j}$; in round $i$ the line of half-length $h_{j,V,i} = m\, d^{(i-1)}_{j,V} / 2$ through the SWC node picks $k_{j,V,i} = \arg\max_k G^{(h)}_{j,k}$; a plane already picked ends it (`converged` or `cycle`), else $d^{(i)}_{j,V} = \hat d_V(k_{j,V,i})$, $V$'s fit (fit_variants, D-046) to Block 5's inputs at that plane (`refit_at_plane(..., with_profile=True)`: the profile through the pilot's fitted centre, $\phi_j$, $\bar B_j(k)$), a weighted $V$ re-weighted from the unweighted fit of its kernel at that plane; statuses as Cell 4j (`fit_failed` when the plane gives no profile or no positive $\bar B$, or the fit no finite positive $d$). Per $V$: `k_iter` (the plane of the last round that picked one), `d_iter_um` and $\hat v_0$ there, the selection line $L_{j,V} = \{o_j + v\,\hat y_j : |v| \le h^{\rm sel}_{j,V}\}$ (the last round that picked a plane; at convergence $h^{\rm sel} = m\, d_{\rm iter} / 2$), $s_j = (c_j - o_j)\cdot\hat y_j$ and the edges $v^\pm = s_j + \hat v_0 \pm d_{\rm iter}/2$ on $L_{j,V}$ (as Cell 4k). The reference, Cell 4j's own iteration (Block 5's fit, `fit_at` None), is run first through the same squares and must give the record's planes, `k_iter`, `d_iter_um` (to the last bit), status and restack count; a difference is noted. Fits at a plane are made once per node and plane and shared by every variant that visits it (`PlaneFits`, keyed by plane: Block 5's inputs come from the pilot's block, the same in every square); a square is made once per line that no square made so far holds, and a variant whose line a square made earlier holds is sampled from the smallest such square (PROVISIONAL, the assistant's: on real data a fetched square's JPEG pixels can differ slightly from another fetch of the same place, so a variant's line beyond the pilot's block may be sampled from a square made for another line; the reference, run first, makes Cell 4j's own squares). Files in `ITER_DIR/variter/`: per node `variter_stack_<id>.png` (one row per variant, the reference first: its label and $d$, its edges $\hat v_0 \pm d/2$ in um from the pilot's fitted centre at its own plane, and the planes its rounds picked ending on `k_iter`, against $k^*$ and the SWC plane) and `variter_planes_<id>.png` (per variant, the plane `k_iter` of the node's largest square, on one view for every panel, with $L_{j,V}$ (black), the fitted dendrite's two edges drawn along the branch through $E^\pm = o_j + v^\pm \hat y_j$ (the kernel's colour), the SWC node, titled `k_iter` and $d$; the reference on top, then one row per kernel and one column per weighting); `variter_<specimen>.csv` (one row per node and variant, the reference first: `node_id`, `k_swc`, `k_star`, `line_mult`, `max_rounds`, `allen_2r_um`, `d_iter_cell4j_um`, `k_iter_cell4j`, `variant`, `family`, `kernel`, `shape_mode`, `width_mode`, `weight`, `lam_um`, `k_allen`, `k_iter`, `d_iter_um`, `iter_status`, `n_rounds`, `planes`, `v0_um`, `mu_per_um`, `fwhm_um`, `shape`, `fit_status`, `at_bound`, `n_irls`, `irls_ok`, `B_bar`, `sel_round`, `sel_half_um`, `s_um`, `n_restacks`, `ratio_ref` = `d_iter_um` / the reference's) and `variter_<specimen>.json` (per node those, `variants` with `trajectory`, `edges_v_um`, `edges_xy_um`, `line_xy_um`, `notes`, `png_stack`, `png_planes`, `seconds`, `n_squares`, `n_planes_fitted`; a skipped node with its reason). Notebook Cell 4m reads Cell 4j's run at $m$ = 3 (`ITER_DIR`) and writes into `ITER_DIR/variter/`. On the synthetic tubes every variant ended on the reference's plane (0.8 um: plane 0, $d$ 0.81-0.86 um, 1 plane fitted, 19 s; 3.0 um: plane -1, $d$ 3.20-3.35 um, 2 planes fitted, 6 squares, 2 min) [run, sandbox]
- Node choice by diameter (`plane_differences.diameter_candidate`, `select_nodes_by_diameter`; `--nodes bydiameter --pilot-csv`; D-040; PROVISIONAL, the assistant's) [smoke-tested 2026-10-09, later]: the pilot rows with `fit_status` converged, a finite `z_sub_um`, a finite positive `d_hat_um`, `steep` false and no flag `crossing`, of a dendrite type, in stretch order (`cell.stretches`, proximal first, then along each stretch), split by $\hat d$ into the bins $(0, e_1], (e_1, e_2], \ldots, (e_n, \infty)$, $e$ = `--dhat-bins` (0.8, 1.0, 1.5, 2.0, 3.0 um); per bin up to `--per-bin` (2) spread evenly over that order (`survey.spread_ranks`); then the ids of `--add-nodes` (2, 3: the trunks judged by eye) that the pilot holds, whatever their row says; all returned by increasing pilot $\hat d$

`figures.variant_stack_figure(block, title)`, `figures.variant_planes_figure(block, title)`
- Parameters: none new; `MeasureConfig` (the chain, `block_half_um`, `bbar_mask_margin_um`, `alpha_dark_flag`), `RendererConfig` (camera chain), `CalibrationConfig` (candidates)
- Library calls relied on: numpy (`percentile`, `median`), Pillow (JPEG tables), matplotlib (Agg), pandas (plane table), git (bootstrap); the 2026-09-23 modules (Block 0); Blocks 4-8, 10
- Custom code: the summaries, the category reader, the noise match (no library inverts a JPEG camera chain); the Allen-guided iteration's loop, `plane_differences.iterate_planes` (2026-10-10, D-042: `scipy.optimize.fixed_point` accelerates a continuous map, and this map is piecewise constant in $d$, its value set by a discrete plane; the loop ends on a repeated plane); the re-weighting loop of `fit_variants.fit_weighted` (2026-10-13, D-046: statsmodels' iteratively re-weighted least squares serves linear models with robust norms; here the model is non-linear and the weights depend on the fitted centre, so each iteration is one `scipy.optimize.least_squares` with the weights held fixed); the numeric FWHM and mode of `blur_kernels` (`scipy.optimize.brentq` on the family's own scipy density; scipy gives neither)
- Test oracles (`tests/smoke/test_smoke_phase2.py`, on the synthetic cell of `tests/smoke/fixtures_cell.py` served through `fetch_zblock`): summary percentiles equal `numpy.percentile`, the $\mu$ range is [p10, p90], the dark share by hand; the selection reads the sentences by category (ON passes, ALONGSIDE and NOT_ON fail); the registration survey on the cell's own traced path: ON, $|s^*| \le$ 0.2 um, $|\Delta z^*| \le$ 0.3 um, the JSON as `run_cell.py` reads it; the pilot: files, every node measured, background median within 4 grey levels of `background_B_gl`, its clipped SD within 15 % of a flat field's post-chain SD, one figure per node; the camera script on a cache of JPEGs saved with a known table (+ one non-JPEG): the table recovered exactly, suggested as `renderer.jpeg_qtables` and accepted by `config_from_dict` [corrected 2026-10-07], the background carried over, and the injected noise matched back through the camera chain within 15 % of the fixture's 3 grey levels; the bootstrap on this clone (no pull); refusals and empty cases (no tables, no matching stretch, no clone with `clone=False`). Plane montage (2026-10-07): the extent puts a pixel's centre where `profiles.sample_profile` reads it (one dark pixel read back as itself to $10^{-6}$ grey levels; half a pixel off would read the mean of the two levels); `depth_alpha` at its closed-form points; `node_planes` equals `measure_nodes` on the node field by field (NaN equal to NaN) and makes the same provider request, also under a 2-px alignment shift, which moves the request; on the fixture's known geometry (the tube rendered on the trace at heading 0.3 rad), the darkness-weighted mean offset across the trace in plane $k^*$, over pixels placed by the extent within 1 um along and 1.5 um across node 4, is within 0.05 um of 0 (below half a pixel; the half-pixel convention is pinned by the first check); `node_planes.run` writes a PNG and records `same` against the pilot's CSV; the montage has one image panel per valid focus plane, and a 'missing' panel without an image for each invalid one (planes outside a 5-plane stack, and one invalidated by hand); the stretch's segments are its consecutive Branch nodes, parent first, and the soma's child segment is not the stretch's and carries the child's radius at both ends; the soma and an id not in the SWC are refused, and `run` records them as skipped; a downsampled frame is refused by the extent; `select_nodes` ('differ' drops NaN rows and equal planes; refuses rows without `k_star_depth`) and `compare_with_pilot` on hand-made rows. Stretch list (`tests/smoke/test_smoke_stretches.py`, a hand-built SWC: soma, two basal stretches, an apical trunk with one oblique, bifurcating into two tuft stretches): the stretches equal `cell.stretches`; lengths, path distances, orders, median diameters, mid nodes and terminal flags by hand; `suggest_nodes` returns the hand-derived ids for `per_type` 1, 2 and 3, filters by `min_nodes`, puts an `include` id first in place of its stretch's mid node, refuses a non-dendrite id; the script writes the CSV and the JSON and prints the suggestion Consecutive-plane differences (`tests/smoke/test_smoke_plane_diff.py`, 2026-10-08): a 3-plane 2 x 2 stack by hand ($S^+$ 0.75 and 0, $S^-$ 0.5 and 0.25) and the dip of hand-made curves (two maxima; one; adjacent ones; NaN; all NaN); a masked random stack against hand loops to 1e-12; reversing the plane order swaps $S^+$ and $S^-$; a constant added to every plane changes nothing; a gain $a$ scales both by $a$; $S^+ - S^-$ equals the mean of $D_n$; a Gaussian blur with wrap boundary (each plane's total conserved) gives $S^+ = S^-$ to 1e-9 relative; planes of white noise of SD $s$ give $S^\pm = s/\sqrt\pi$ within 5 standard errors; an invalid plane makes its two pairs NaN; a non-finite pixel counts only inside the mask; refusals of one plane, a bad `valid`, an empty or misshapen mask; a rendered thin tube (no noise, centre in plane 0) has its dip next to plane 0; `plane_differences.run` on the synthetic cell: planes $\pm 8$ about the SWC plane with the two missing ones NaN, the dip next to plane 0, a PNG and a record per node, the soma recorded as skipped, `--band-um` counts fewer pixels Profile-area evaluation (same suite, 2026-10-08, later): the areas of linear profiles are exact under the trapezoid ($a + bv$ on $[-1, 1]$ gives $2a$); random profiles against a hand trapezoid to 1e-9; a Gaussian dip blurred by Gaussians of width 0-1 um keeps its area to 1e-9 relative (window $\pm 12$ um); a constant $c$ adds $c$ x the window length; NaN for an invalid or non-finite profile and for the differences touching it; refusals of a decreasing or mismatched $v$; the interquartile mean of eight hand values (5.25, where the median is 4.5 and the mean 17); the far-pixel mask of a segment and the soma by hand; on the synthetic cell: one area per plane, NaN for the missing planes, $B_k$ flat within 1 grey level, normalised areas within 1 % of the raw ones; with one plane made 2 % darker after the camera, the smallest raw area moves to that plane while the normalised areas are the unchanged run's times one common factor (to 1e-9). Entropy evaluation (`tests/smoke/test_smoke_plane_entropy.py`, 2026-10-08, evening): eight distinct levels give 3 bits, one level 0, two levels in the ratio 1:3 give 0.811278 bits (closed form); the half-integer boundaries by hand (0.5 and -0.5 go up) and bins of width 2 (1.5 bits); `grey_histogram`'s centres and counts by hand; `stripe_mask` by hand, axis-aligned, turned by 90 degrees, and with a frame offset and a pixel size (boundary pixels included); random values against a count in a dict with `math.log2` to 1e-12; `stripe_mask` against a pixel loop; `plane_entropies` against per-plane calls; the plug-in mean of a rounded Gaussian (SD 3) over 800 draws reads low at $N$ = 100 and 1000, 5 to 15 times more at 100; adding a multiple of $w$, permuting, or scaling the values with $w$ leave $\hat H$ unchanged, and so does a different integer offset per plane in `plane_entropies`; a gain of 2 on a Gaussian of SD 20 (1e6 values) adds 1 bit within 0.01; 1e6 values of a rounded Gaussian (SD 3, mean 0.3) lie within 0.005 bits of the discretised distribution's exact entropy (a sum over the normal CDF), itself within 0.01 bits of $\frac12\log_2(2\pi e s^2)$; NaN dropped, (NaN, 0, 0) with nothing finite, refusals of $w \le 0$ or NaN; an invalid plane, or a non-finite sample in a set, makes that set's entropy NaN with $N$ = 0, a non-finite pixel outside the strip does not; refusals of mismatched shapes, a misshapen or empty strip and a bad `valid`; a rendered thin tube (no noise, centre in plane 0): both dips at plane 0; `plane_differences.run --evaluation entropy` on the synthetic cell: planes $\pm 8$ with the two missing ones NaN, 53 samples and 917 pixels in every other plane, `planeentropy_*` files and no `planediff_*`, both dips at plane 0, the soma skipped; a plane shifted by +3 or -5 grey levels after the camera leaves every entropy unchanged (exactly); made 2 % darker, its strip entropy moves by less than 0.05 bits (0.0075 here); a strip of half-width 0.5 um holds fewer pixels (457). Added 2026-10-08, evening: the pick rules on node 2's real line-entropy curve (global minimum: plane 114; dip rule: plane 121), ties to the first, None with nothing finite, a refused rule; the record's profiles (17 x 53, NaN rows for the missing planes); with a $\pm 5$ um line on the synthetic cell (block $\pm 3.5$ um + 1 px) the square is fetched, 89 samples in every valid plane and none NaN, in the entropy and the profile evaluation; the saved profiles (2 decimals) integrate back to the areas within 0.12 grey levels x um; `--entropy-pick dip` still takes plane 0 there. Added 2026-10-09: the thin-node selection on the hand-built seven-stretch SWC of `test_smoke_stretches.py`, with rows failing one criterion each (not in S, $2r$ 0.7 um, $\hat d$ 1.2 um, the focus rules disagreeing, steep, `z_sub_um` NaN, a missing field; strings "True"/"False" read as booleans): the qualifying ids of a stretch, the per-stretch spread and the final spread worked out by hand (3 per stretch and 12 in all: 2, 4, 14, 16, 18, 25, 34, 45, 51, 56, 59, 60; 1 per stretch: 4, 16, 29, 50, 58), an empty choice when no row qualifies; `pick_summary` on hand records (offsets 1, 0, 4 and -2, None, 0; within one plane 2 of 3 and 1 of 2; median 1.0 and 1.0; a skipped node left out); three nodes of the synthetic cell in one run: the summary CSV (one row per node, each offset equal to its pick minus $k^*$, Allen's $2r$ twice the record's radius), the summary figure, and the summary line in the log; the summary figure with five records draws five panels on a 2 x 4 grid. Added 2026-10-09 (afternoon), `--hide-line`: on a hand-built three-plane block (the middle plane missing) the entropy figure's plane panels carry 2, 0 and 2 lines (the line and the outline) by default and none with `show_line=False`, the images (1, 0, 1) and the picked plane's frame kept, the legend naming the outline only when it is drawn and the bare planes only when they are; the area figure's plane panels carry 1, 0 and 1 lines by default and none with the line hidden; `run()` hands `show_line` to either figure (a spy on the figure function) and writes the same record either way (equal JSON but for the PNG path); `main()` passes `--hide-line` on as `show_line=False` (Allen's server stubbed, `run` replaced by a spy). Gradient lines, blend and node choice by diameter (`tests/smoke/test_smoke_plane_gradient.py`, 2026-10-09, later): $w$ = 1/2 at $d_0$ and 1/4, 3/4 at $d_0 \pm s \ln 3$ (three pairs $(d_0, s)$), NaN for a NaN $\hat d$; min-max by hand (2, 4, 6 to 0, 1/2, 1; inverted 1, 1/2, 0; a flat curve 0; a plane outside `valid` NaN); the blend by hand ($G$ = 1, 3, 2 and $H$ = 5, 4, 3 give $g$ = 0, 1, 1/2, $h$ = 1, 1/2, 0, and $J = wg - (1 - w)h$ = -1/2, 1/4, 1/4 at $w$ = 1/2, the tie to the first plane; with $G$'s pick $p$ = 1 and the entropy's $q$ = 2, $w^* = h_p/(h_p + (1 - g_q))$ = 1/2, so $w$ = 0.6 picks $p$ and 0.4 picks $q$; a NaN in $H$ takes the plane out of $V$ and re-scales the rest) [updated 2026-10-10, D-041: until then $\eta = 1 - h$ and $J = wg + (1 - w)\eta$, checked the same way; the reference loop now also checks that this earlier form is $J + (1 - w)$, the same maximum]; $G$ of a linear ramp of slope $a$ with smoothing off is $a^2 \cdot 2N\Delta / B^2$ exactly; the d-line's half-length $m \hat d/2$; `plane_gradient_energies` equals `gradient_energy` called plane by plane with the window "whole_profile" and $B_k$ as override, NaN for an invalid plane, a non-finite sample or $B_k \le 0$; `blend_scores` against a loop by hand to 1e-15; a Gaussian dip (A 60, sigma 0.4 um, B 200) converges to $A^2\sqrt\pi/(2\sigma B^2)$ at second order (error ratios 3.5-4.5 on halving the step); a 2 % darker plane (profile and $B_k$) keeps its $G$ to 1e-12; positive affine maps of $G$ and $H$ leave $g$, $\eta$, $J$ unchanged; $w(d_0 + x) + w(d_0 - x) = 1$ and $w$ decreasing; $w$ = 1 picks $G$'s plane, 0 the entropy's; flat ground with white noise of SD 3 (4000 planes, smoothing off) gives the closed-form mean $(2s^2/\Delta + (M - 2)s^2/(2\Delta))/B^2$ within 5 standard errors; refusals (shapes, $s \le 0$, $w$ outside [0, 1], $\hat d$ or $m$ not positive, four or repeated fixed lines); the node choice on the hand-built seven-stretch SWC with rows failing one criterion each (not converged, steep, crossing, $z_{\rm sub}$ NaN, $\hat d$ NaN or 0, `steep` missing or the string "True"; kept: another flag, an empty flags cell, $\hat d$ on a bin's upper edge): 2 per bin with the trunks [2, 3, 13, 18, 14, 19, 24, 25, 34, 45, 56, 57, 60] (node 40 added only if in the pilot), 1 per bin [11, 16, 21, 29, 50, 58], refusals of bad bins;
