### Block 12: the skewed generalized-t kernel and the seven-parameter fit at a plane (D-052.1-2; D-053 (ii); D-054 (iv))

- Modules: `src/allen_diameter/model/blur_kernels.py` (the SGT family, added beside the D-046 families), `src/allen_diameter/analysis/fit_sgt.py` (the fit), `src/allen_diameter/config.py` (the `sgt_*` fields of `MeasureConfig`)
- Kernel (D-052.1): with $B$ the beta and $\Gamma$ the gamma function, for $\kappa = (\beta, \xi, \zeta)$ with $\beta > 0$, $\xi \ge 0$, $\zeta > 0$ and every $x \in \mathbb R$: $h_{\beta,\xi}(x) = \beta\,\xi^{1/\beta}\,[2B(1/\beta, 1/\xi)]^{-1}(1 + \xi|x|^\beta)^{-(1/\xi + 1/\beta)}$ when $\xi >$ `SGT_XI_ZERO` ($10^{-12}$), else $h_{\beta,0}(x) = \beta\,[2\Gamma(1/\beta)]^{-1}e^{-|x|^\beta}$ (H1); $f(x \mid \kappa) = 2(\zeta + 1/\zeta)^{-1}h_{\beta,\xi}(x/\zeta)$ for $x \ge 0$ and $2(\zeta + 1/\zeta)^{-1}h_{\beta,\xi}(\zeta x)$ for $x < 0$ (H2), a density of unit integral with its mode at 0, right mass $\zeta^2/(1 + \zeta^2)$ and right-to-left half-width ratio $\zeta^2$; its FWHM $W(\kappa) = (\zeta + 1/\zeta)\,[\mathrm{expm1}(e \ln 2)/\xi]^{1/\beta}$ with $e = \xi\beta/(\beta + \xi)$ for $\xi > 0$ (`expm1`: $2^e - 1$ without cancellation at small $\xi$), $(\zeta + 1/\zeta)(\ln 2)^{1/\beta}$ for $\xi = 0$; the kernel of FWHM $w$ (um), $g_{w,\kappa}(z) = (W(\kappa)/w)\,f(W(\kappa) z/w \mid \kappa)$ for every $z$ (um) (H3). Evaluated in logs: $\ln g = \ln c(\beta, \xi) + \ln(2/(\zeta + 1/\zeta)) - \ln a + \ln h$-core, $a = w/W(\kappa)$, the core $-(1/\xi + 1/\beta)\,\mathrm{log1p}(\xi|u|^\beta)$ or $-|u|^\beta$. Members: $(2, 0, 1)$ Gaussian, $(1, 0, 1)$ Laplace, $(2, 2/\nu, 1)$ Student's $t_\nu$ ($(2, 2, 1)$ Lorentzian), $(\beta, 0, 1)$ generalized normal, $\zeta \ne 1$ their two-piece skewed versions; tails $|z|^{-(1 + \beta/\xi)}$ for $\xi > 0$. The family is **not** added to `blur_kernels.FAMILIES`: those are the one-shape scipy families of the D-046 variant grid (Cells 4l, 4m), whose code and suites stay unchanged (deviation from the handoff's §8.1, which proposed adding it there; the assistant's, 2026-10-18)
- Model (H4) = (3'') of section 2.2: `tube_model.model_profile_kernel(v, d, alpha_from_mu(mu, d, phi), v0, sgt_kernel_pdf(w, beta, xi, zeta), B_bar, N)`
- Search coordinates $q = (d, \mu, v_0, w, \log_{10}\beta, \xi, \ln\zeta)$ (`fit_sgt.PARAM_NAMES`) in the box $\Theta$: `fit_d_bounds_um` x `fit_mu_bounds_per_um` x `fit_v0_bounds_um` x `sgt_w_bounds_um` x $\log_{10}$(`sgt_beta_bounds`) x `sgt_xi_bounds` x `sgt_ln_zeta_bounds` = [0.05, 6] um x [0, 20] /um x [-1, 1] um x [0.1, 2] um x [0, 1] x [0, 2] x [-0.7, 0.7] at the defaults (D-052.2). Every solve: `scipy.optimize.least_squares(residuals, q0, bounds=box, method="trf", jac="2-point", x_scale="jac", ftol = xtol = gtol = fit_tol, max_nfev = sgt_max_nfev)` with residuals $\sqrt{\omega_n}\,(I_{\rm model}(v_n \mid q) - I_n)/\bar B$, so that the solver's cost is $Q(\theta \mid \omega)$ of (3''), and $q_0$ clipped into the box shrunk by `fit_sgt.START_MARGIN` ($10^{-9}$, as the D-052 check's solves)
- Steps (`fit_sgt.fit_plane`): (1) the two Gaussian fits of Block 11's `fit_variants` (D-046) on the same profile: Block 5's Gaussian at $\sigma_{\rm fit}$ (`gauss_fixed`, the unweighted fixed-width Gaussian) and the Gaussian of fitted width (`gauss_fitted`, warm-started from it; its own box `fit_variants.FITTED_FWHM_BOUNDS_UM` (0.1, 2) um, equal to the default `sgt_w_bounds_um`); (2) the unweighted fit $\hat\theta^{(0)}$: one solve from `gauss_fitted`'s $(d, \mu, v_0, w)$ with each shape of `sgt_start_shapes`, then one from `gauss_fixed`'s $(d, \mu, v_0)$ at $w$ = `sgt_start_w_um` with shape `sgt_base_start_shape` (a start with a non-finite entry is left out); the reported solve is the converged one (scipy status > 0, finite $q$ and cost) of least cost, else the finite one of least cost, the first in start order on ties (PROVISIONAL start rule, D-052 (h): the sandbox check's, `checks/estimator_2026-10-15/identifiability_sgt.fit_sgt`); (3) the IRLS from $\hat\theta^{(0)}$'s $q$: weights $\omega^{(t)}_n = \exp(-|v_n - \hat v^{(t)}_0|/\lambda)$ (`fit_variants.centre_weights`), one solve from $q^{(t)}$ per iteration, stop with `irls_ok` True when $|\Delta d| \le$ `sgt_irls_tol_um` and $|\Delta v_0| \le$ `sgt_irls_tol_um`, with `irls_ok` False when a solve does not converge or after `sgt_irls_max_iter` solves; the last solve's $q$ is reported
- Status (D-053 (ii)), one of `fit.FIT_STATUSES`: `"failed"` when the fit's own rule is not met (unweighted: no solve converged; IRLS: `irls_ok` False, the cap or a failed solve such as one stopped at `sgt_max_nfev`); else `"at_bound"` when $d$, $\mu$, $v_0$ or $w$ lies within `fit_at_bound_rel_tol` x its range of a bound; else `"converged"`. `at_bound` names every coordinate at a bound, the shapes included (`d_lo` ... `w_hi`, `beta_lo`, `beta_hi`, `xi_lo`, `xi_hi`, `zeta_lo`, `zeta_hi`); a shape at its edge never changes the status ($\xi = 0$ is the generalized-normal edge, $\beta = 1$ the Laplace: members, not failures), so Block 6's `fit_status == "converged"` implements D-053 (ii) unchanged
- Quadrature: $N = \max(\texttt{fit\_quad\_min\_nodes}, \lceil\texttt{fit\_quad\_nodes\_per\_sigma}\cdot d_{\rm hi}/\sigma_{\min}\rceil)$, $d_{\rm hi}$ = `fit_d_bounds_um[1]`, $\sigma_{\min}$ = `sgt_w_bounds_um[0]` $/(2\sqrt{2\ln 2})$ the Gaussian SD at the narrowest width: 848 at the defaults (D-052.2), kept as decided. The rule was derived for the Gaussian; on the SGT family Gauss-Legendre converges exponentially only for the analytic members ($\zeta = 1$ with $\beta$ = 2 or 4, any $\xi$), and algebraically, with order about $k + 1$ for a jump in the $k$-th derivative of $g$ at its peak, for the others: observed at the box corner ($d$ = 6 um, $w$ = 0.1 um), orders 3.2-3.3 for the two-piece skew ($\zeta$ = 1.3, a jump in $g''$), 2.6 for $\beta$ = 1.5, 2.0 for the Laplace (a kink), 2.8 for the box-like $\beta$ = 10 (analytic but pre-asymptotic), with model errors at $N$ of 0.05, 0.18, 0.70 and 2.1 grey levels there and a minimum $I_{\rm model}$ of -7.5 grey levels for $\beta$ = 10, $\mu$ = 20 ($I_{\rm model} \le \bar B$ holds exactly at any $N$). In the fitted regime ($w \ge$ 0.3 um, $d \le$ 5.5 um) the model error at $N$ is at most 0.07 grey levels and the bias it puts on the IRLS $\hat d$ at most 7.5e-4 um (noise-free fits of a 16 $N$ truth, four shapes, 2026-10-18; Laplace at $d$ = 5.5 um the worst); fits that reach a $w$ bound fail $\mathcal S$. Open point in section 8
- Public API: `blur_kernels.SGT`, `SGT_XI_ZERO`, `check_sgt_shape(beta, xi, zeta) -> (beta, xi, zeta)` (ValueError unless finite, $\beta > 0$, $\xi \ge 0$, $\zeta > 0$), `sgt_core_hwhm(beta, xi) -> float`, `sgt_unit_fwhm(beta, xi, zeta) -> W`, `sgt_unit_pdf(x, beta, xi, zeta) -> f` (array of the shape of x), `sgt_kernel_pdf(fwhm_um, beta, xi, zeta) -> g` (a vectorised callable of $z$ um; ValueError for a FWHM not finite and > 0); `fit_sgt.box(cfg) -> (lb, ub)` (7,) float64, `to_q(d, mu, v0, w, beta, xi, zeta) -> q`, `from_q(q) -> (d, mu, v0, w, beta, xi, zeta)`, `n_nodes_for(cfg) -> int`, `sgt_model(v, q, phi, B_bar, n_nodes) -> I_model`, `objective(v, I, phi, B_bar, q, omega, n_nodes) -> Q`, `start_points(gauss_fitted, gauss_fixed, cfg) -> [(label, q)]`, `fit_unweighted(v, I, phi, B_bar, cfg, starts) -> SGTFit`, `fit_irls(v, I, phi, B_bar, cfg, start) -> SGTFit`, `fit_plane(v, I, phi, B_bar, cfg) -> PlaneSGT`, `fit_curve(fit, v, B_bar) -> I_model`
- `SGTFit` (frozen): `weighting` (`"irls"` the deliverable, `"none"` the unweighted comparison; `fit_sgt.WEIGHTINGS`), `d_um, mu_per_um, v0_um, w_um, beta, xi, zeta, alpha_hat, phi_rad`, `q` (the 7 search coordinates as the solver returned them), `status`, `at_bound`, `solve_ok` (unweighted: a solve converged; IRLS: every weighted solve converged), `irls_ok` (None unweighted), `n_irls`, `irls_path` (($d$, $v_0$) after each weighted solve), `cost` ($Q$ of the reported solve, for the IRLS at the weights it was solved with), `rss_gl2` ($\sum_n (I_{\rm model} - I_n)^2$), `nfev` (summed), `n_starts`, `best_start` (label), `n_nodes`, `message`, `model_gl` ($I_{\rm model}(v_n)$, (N,)); `PlaneSGT`: `final` (the IRLS fit, the deliverable), `unweighted`, `gauss_fitted`, `gauss_fixed` (`fit_variants.VariantFit`, labelled comparisons)
- Inputs: as Block 3 (`fit._check_profile`): `v` (n,) strictly increasing um, `I` (n,) finite grey levels, `phi` rad in [0, pi/2), `B_bar` > 0, `cfg` a `MeasureConfig`; else ValueError
- Parameters: `fit_d_bounds_um`, `fit_mu_bounds_per_um`, `fit_v0_bounds_um`, `fit_tol`, `fit_at_bound_rel_tol`, `fit_quad_min_nodes`, `fit_quad_nodes_per_sigma`, `sigma_fit_um` and `fit_max_nfev` (the Gaussian start fits), and the new fields `sgt_w_bounds_um` = (0.1, 2.0) um, `sgt_beta_bounds` = (1, 10), `sgt_xi_bounds` = (0, 2), `sgt_ln_zeta_bounds` = (-0.7, 0.7), `sgt_lambda_um` = 0.5 um, `sgt_irls_tol_um` = 1e-4 um, `sgt_irls_max_iter` = 30, `sgt_max_nfev` = 3000 (D-054 (iv)), `sgt_start_shapes` = ((2, 0, 1), (1, 0, 1), (5, 0, 1), (2, 1, 1)), `sgt_start_w_um` = 0.7 um, `sgt_base_start_shape` = (2, 0, 1); all in the estimator signature. `config.validate` requires $0 < w_{\rm lo} < w_{\rm hi}$, $0 < \beta_{\rm lo} < \beta_{\rm hi}$, $0 \le \xi_{\rm lo} < \xi_{\rm hi}$, $\ln\zeta_{\rm lo} < \ln\zeta_{\rm hi}$, $\lambda$ and the IRLS tolerance > 0, the cap and the budget >= 1, at least one start shape, every start shape inside the box and `sgt_start_w_um` inside the $w$ bounds; overrides of the nested start shapes are coerced to floats like every float tuple (one configuration, one hash)
- Library calls relied on: `scipy.optimize.least_squares`, `scipy.special.betaln`, `scipy.special.gammaln`, `numpy.log1p`, `math.expm1`; Block 11's `fit_variants.PlaneFits`, `centre_weights`; in the test `scipy.integrate.quad`, `scipy.special.betainc`, `scipy.special.gammainc`, `scipy.optimize.brentq`, `scipy.stats` (`norm`, `laplace`, `t`, `cauchy`, `gennorm`)
- Custom code: the SGT density (scipy.stats has no generalized t and no SGT; checked in the D-052 check: `t`, `gennorm`, `genhyperbolic`, `jf_skew_t`, `johnsonsu`, `pearson3`, none of which spans the platykurtic and the power-law members with one skew) and the IRLS loop (no scipy routine re-centres its weights on the fit's own axis)
- Test oracles (`tests/smoke/test_smoke_sgt_fit.py`): the kernel's mass on $[0, X]$ and $[-X, 0]$ by `scipy.integrate.quad` against the closed forms $(2\zeta/s)\,H(X/(a\zeta))$ and $(2/(s\zeta))\,H(\zeta X/a)$, $s = \zeta + 1/\zeta$, $H(y) = \tfrac12 I_{t}(1/\beta, 1/\xi)$ with $t = \xi y^\beta/(1 + \xi y^\beta)$ (`betainc`), $\tfrac12 P(1/\beta, y^\beta)$ at $\xi = 0$ (`gammainc`), on a grid of $(\beta, \xi, \zeta)$ including the box corners (1e-11: quad on a finite smooth range, epsabs 1e-14); the unit integral over $\mathbb R$ by quad with the tail mapped $x = t^{-m}$, $m = 2\xi/\beta$, so that the integrand is regular (1e-10); FWHM = $w$ by `brentq` on the kernel itself (1e-12 um) and half-width ratio $\zeta^2$ (1e-10 relative); the mode at 0 and monotone decrease on each side; the members against `scipy.stats` `norm`, `laplace`, `t(4)`, `cauchy`, `gennorm(beta)` rescaled to FWHM $w$ and against `blur_kernels.kernel_pdf` (student, lorentz, gennorm, gauss) (1e-12 absolute, the kernel's values being at most a few per um); continuity at $\xi \to 0$ and the `expm1` branch ($\xi = 10^{-10}$: $W$ within $10^{-9}$ relative of the $\xi = 0$ value, while the naive $2^e - 1$ errs by more than $10^{-8}$; the kernel at $\xi = 10^{-9}$ within $10^{-6}$ of the $\xi = 0$ kernel); the model against a direct convolution by quad at the fit's $N$, by member class (analytic 1e-7, skewed 1e-4, Laplace 2e-3 grey levels: the measured errors at $w$ = 0.5 um, $d \le$ 1.5 um with a margin); the quadrature's observed order at the box corner per class (analytic: >= 8 between $N/2$ and $N$, error <= 1e-6 gl; skew [2.6, 3.8]; $\beta$ = 1.5 [2.1, 3.0]; Laplace [1.6, 2.5]) and the bias of $\hat d$ it causes in the fitted regime (<= 2e-3 um on two cases, Laplace at $d$ 5.5 um, $w$ 0.5 um the worst); $I_{\rm model} \le \bar B$ exactly at $N$ and >= -0.02 gl at 16 $N$ over the box and its worst corners; noise-free recovery of $\theta$ on a 1.5 um tube ($d$, $v_0$, $w$ to 1e-6, $\mu$ to 1e-5, shapes to 1e-3, residual sum of squares <= 1e-12 gl^2); nesting: the unweighted SGT's residual sum of squares is at most the Gaussian-of-fitted-width's on the same noisy profile (1e-6 relative slack, the start clipped to $\xi = 10^{-9}$), the SGT at $(2, 0, 1)$ reproducing the Gaussian model (1e-10 gl); five draws of the D-052 Monte Carlo (seed 20261017, `checks/estimator_2026-10-15/ident_sgt/identifiability_sgt.json`, values embedded in the test) reproduced, unweighted and IRLS, including the draw whose weighted solve stopped at the 3000 budget (status `failed`), a $\mu$ = 20 collapse (`at_bound`) and fits at $\xi = 0$ (`converged`, `xi_lo` named); IRLS: deterministic (two runs bit-identical), stops by its rule (the last step within tolerance, earlier ones not), `irls_ok` False and status `failed` at a cap of 1 and at a budget of 5; invariances (a gain on $I$ and $\bar B$; a shift of $v$; the mirror $v \to -v$ maps $v_0 \to -v_0$, $\zeta \to 1/\zeta$); the defaults (D-052.2, D-054 (iv)) and `config.validate`'s refusals; `blur_kernels.FAMILIES` unchanged
- Data flow: consumes a plane's profile, $\varphi$ and $\bar B$ (Block 5's sampling, background and pilot); Block 11's `fit_variants` (start fits); feeds Block 13 (each iteration round's $d$ and the final fit at $k^{\rm it}$), the D-053 (i) radius check and, through the measurement, Blocks 6-7
- Confirmed: yes for the kernel, the model, the objective, the box, $\lambda$, the IRLS rule and the quadrature rule (D-052, the user, 2026-10-16), the status rule (D-053 (ii), the user, 2026-10-16) and the budget (D-054 (iv), the user, 2026-10-18); the start rule and the IRLS cap PROVISIONAL (D-052 (h)); keeping the SGT out of `FAMILIES`, the module split, `START_MARGIN` and the reuse of `fit_variants` for the Gaussian starts are the assistant's (2026-10-18)
- Status: smoke-tested 2026-10-18 (`test_smoke_sgt_fit.py` 7 pass, 2 min; sandbox, Python 3.13.16, NumPy 2.5.3, SciPy 1.18.1). Kernel: mass vs the closed forms 3.9e-12 and unit integral 9.9e-13 over 64 shapes, FWHM 4.4e-16 um, half-width ratio 2.6e-15; members vs scipy.stats 8.9e-16, vs `kernel_pdf` 5.1e-14. Model vs quad at $N$ = 848: analytic 2.7e-11, skewed 2.3e-6, Laplace 8.6e-4 grey levels. Noise-free recovery on a 1.5 um tube: $|\hat\theta - \theta|$ <= 1.5e-13. The five D-052 Monte Carlo draws reproduced bit for bit (max |diff| 0), statuses `converged` with `xi_lo` (three fits), `at_bound` `mu_hi` (the collapse) and `failed` (draw 39's weighted solve at 3000 evaluations). Quadrature orders at the box corner: analytic 20.9, skew 3.3, $\beta$ 1.5 2.6, Laplace 2.0; $\hat d$ bias in the fitted regime -7.5e-4 um (Laplace, 5.5 um) and -1.1e-6 um (skew, 3 um). Invariances: gain 1.3e-6, shift 1.7e-6, mirror 5.1e-7. About 1.5-2 s per thick profile and 7-9 s per thin one for `fit_plane` on one core (draw 39: 30 s). Estimator hash at the defaults `1bb3585b4a4f1373` (was `6f0397236447fe6f` at `ea49181`). Every other suite re-run unchanged (23, same pass and skip counts as at `ea49181`). ### Block 13: the Allen-guided plane and the SGT fit inside the per-node chain (D-052.3, D-057 (iii), D-061, D-064; handoff 2026-10-19 s.6.1)

- Modules: `src/allen_diameter/analysis/plane_iteration.py` (new: the iteration's rule, its background, its line profiles, the refit at a plane, the square and the scene), `analysis/node_pipeline.py` (`measure_pilot`, `measure_node`), `analysis/cell.py` (`cell_scene`), `analysis/survey.py` (`node_planes` on the shared scene rule), `config.py` (the iteration's fields), `scripts/plane_differences.py` (imports the moved functions; `node_stack` on the shared square rule), and the readers of `NodeResult` (Blocks 6, 8, 10, 11). Sub-blocks 13.1-13.5, each committed on its own
- 13.1, the move (no behaviour change): `ITER_LINE_MULT` (3.0, D-044), `ITER_STATUSES`, `check_iteration`, `iterate_planes`, `far_from_dendrites`, `plane_background`, `block_background`, `line_profiles`, `refit_at_plane`, `gradient_iteration` and `_argmax_plane` move from `scripts/plane_differences.py` into `analysis/plane_iteration.py` with their bodies unchanged; the script imports them under the same names, so Cells 4e-4m and their suites call the same objects (one implementation). Their definitions are Block 11's (D-036, D-040, D-042, D-044, D-047). `refit_at_plane` keeps Block 5's Gaussian fit (`fit.fit_profile`: the diagnostics' fit, the inputs of D-042); the deliverable's per-round fit enters through `gradient_iteration`'s `fit_at` (13.3). `node_stack` stays in the script until 13.2
- 13.2, the square and the scene (D-064; implemented 2026-10-20): a scene $\Sigma$ = every traced segment $s$ with parent end $a_s \in \mathbb R^3$ and child end $b_s \in \mathbb R^3$ (um, the image frame of the Branch: `cell.to_image_um` with the transform), Allen's radii $r_{0,s}$, $r_{1,s}$ at the two ends (um; $r_{0,s} = r_{1,s}$ when the parent is not a dendrite node, so the soma's radius is not a neurite's), the ids of its child and parent, and the soma discs $(x, y, r)$ (um; SWC type 1). `cell.cell_scene(swc, types, res0_um, transform) -> Scene`: every segment whose child is a dendrite node of `types`, and the soma nodes. `Scene.from_branch(branch)`: the branch's consecutive nodes with `branch.radius_um`, no soma -- what `scene=None` means (a phantom: its own tube). `plane_iteration.scene_segments(scene, frame, shape, branch) -> (n, 9)` float64: the rows $(a_s, b_s, r_{0,s}, r_{1,s}, \mathbb 1[\text{both ends in the Branch}])$ of the segments whose xy bounding box meets the block $[(\ell - \tfrac12)p, (\ell + W - \tfrac12)p] \times [(t - \tfrac12)p, (t + H - \tfrac12)p]$ ($\ell$, $t$ the block's left and top in full-resolution pixels, $W \times H$ its shape, $p$ = `res_um_px`) -- the rule of `survey.node_planes` (Block 11), which calls it. The square: `plane_iteration.cut_square(block, ks, valid, frame, o, half_um, provider) -> (stack, valid, frame, fetched)`, `node_stack`'s rule: columns $c_0 = \lceil (o_x - h)/p - \ell \rceil$, $c_1 = \lfloor (o_x + h)/p - \ell \rfloor + 1$, rows likewise in $y$ ($h$ = `half_um`, $o$ the SWC node's xy, um); ValueError below 2 x 2 pixels; cut from the block when $0 \le c_0$, $0 \le r_0$, $c_1 \le W$, $r_1 \le H$, else requested from the provider over the block's planes (ValueError when it returns other planes), its `valid` ANDed with the block's. `plane_iteration.iteration_state(...)` assembles the dict that `gradient_iteration` and its callees read (`stack, ks, valid, frame, o, theta, block, block_frame, segments, soma, square_half_um, square_fetched, allen_radius_um, result, branch, index, k_swc`); `node_stack` (the script) and `measure_node` (13.3) both build it
- 13.3, the measurement, in one commit (13.3a and 13.3b; planned as two, committed together because the suite's checks of both share one file); 13.4's suite changes travel with the code they test, so that every commit passes the battery (2026-10-20)
- 13.3a, the fit at a plane (`plane_iteration`; implemented 2026-10-20). `plane_inputs(st, cfg, k) -> dict(status, v_um, profile, phi_rad, B_bar, bbar_ok)`: the inputs `refit_at_plane` used (D-042), factored out and called by it (no behaviour change): the offsets $v$ = `profiles.profile_offsets(cfg.measure)` (um), the profile $I_i(v \mid k)$ through the pilot's fitted centre $c_i$ = (`cx_um`, `cy_um`) across its heading $\theta_i$ in plane $k$ of the block, averaged over $2A + 1$ lines along the branch (`profiles.n_along`, `profile_step_um`; grey levels), the pilot's tilt $\varphi_i$ (rad), and $\bar B_i(k)$ = `node_pipeline.node_background` of that plane about the SWC node (D-018.1, grey levels) with its `ok`. `status` is "ok", or why no fit can be made: "no_plane" ($k$ not a valid plane of the block; `v_um` and `profile` None, `B_bar` NaN), "profile_nan" (a sample outside the block), "bbar_nonpositive" ($\bar B_i(k) \le 0$). `sgt_at_plane(st, cfg, k) -> dict`: the deliverable's fit at plane $k$, $\hat\theta_i(k)$ = `fit_sgt.fit_plane(v, I_i(. | k), phi_i, B_bar_i(k), cfg.measure).final` (D-052.2), as the round record `iterate_planes` reads: `d_hat_um`, `fit_status` (the final fit's, D-053 (ii): converged / at_bound / failed; without inputs, the inputs' status), `v0_hat_um` (relative to $c_i$ along $\hat y(\theta_i)$, as Block 5's), `mu_hat_per_um`, `B_bar`, `bbar_ok`, `w_hat_um`, `beta_hat`, `xi_hat`, `zeta_hat`, `alpha_hat` ($\hat\mu\hat d/\cos\varphi_i$), `irls_ok`, `at_bound`, `plane_fit` (`fit_sgt.PlaneSGT`; None without inputs), `v_um`, `profile`; NaN numbers without inputs. A round's $d$ is that record's `d_hat_um` whatever its status (D-052: the settled fit's own iteration, as Cell 4m runs it per variant, D-047); a fit without numbers ends the iteration `fit_failed` (D-042)
- 13.3b, the chain (`node_pipeline`, `config`, the readers; implemented 2026-10-20). `node_request(branch, i, cfg) -> (left, top, width, height, k_lo, k_hi)`: node $i$'s one request (D-061.1): Block 5's xy block (`block_half_um` about the window nodes) over the planes from the lowest to the highest of the window nodes' pilot ranges ($\pm$`planes_half`, widened for tilt) and of $K_i = \{k_{{\rm SWC},i} - P, \ldots, k_{{\rm SWC},i} + P\}$, $k_{{\rm SWC},i}$ = round($z_i/\Delta z$), $P$ = `iter_planes_half`. `measure_pilot(branch, i, provider, cfg, reg=None) -> NodeResult`: Block 5 on Block 5's planes of the block of that request (`_pilot_planes`: the lowest to the highest of the window nodes' pilot ranges from their raw directions, the planes Block 5's own request spanned; a pass whose redrawn direction is tilted widens its range and is clipped to them, as it was), so that its result equals Block 5's at `84ea91f` bit for bit on a provider serving the same pixels for any request; the Block 13 fields at their defaults -- what `survey.node_planes`, the Phase II diagnostics and Block 10's `scan_node` call (D-061.2); since the request is `measure_node`'s, the diagnostics are served from the image cache. [2026-10-20: without the clip, a pass-1 direction tilted by the pilot's own sub-plane depths read up to K's planes, and on the fixture's 3.0 um tube $k^*$ moved off -4 on four of six nodes, to -5 on one and -6 on three (the run behind this rule).] `measure_node(branch, i, provider, cfg, reg=None, scene=None) -> NodeResult`: (1) the pilot on Block 5's planes of the same block, bit-identical to `measure_pilot`'s; (2) the iteration on the block's planes $K_i$: `iteration_state` with the square $\pm$`block_half_um` about the SWC node (inside the block, so never a call), the segments `scene_segments(scene, block frame, block shape, branch)` and `scene.soma` (`scene=None`: `Scene.from_branch(branch)`, a phantom's own tube, D-064), and $k_{{\rm SWC},i}$; `gradient_iteration` from $d^{(0)}_i = 2\,r_{{\rm br},i}$ (`branch.radius_um[i]`: Allen's on a real node, the phantom's on a phantom) with `line_mult` = `iter_line_mult`, `max_rounds` = `iter_max_rounds`, `bg_margin_um` = `iter_bg_margin_um`, `fit_at` = `sgt_at_plane`, and `restack(h)` = `iteration_state` at the half-width $h + 2p$ (cut from the block when it lies inside it, else one provider call: D-061.1's one call per restack); (3) $k^{\rm it}_i$ = the last plane picked (`k_final`), the deliverable $\hat\theta_i(k^{\rm it}_i)$ = `sgt_at_plane`'s record of that round. Columns: the centre, heading, tilt, $k^*$, sub-plane depth, `steep`, `vertical` and the focus curves stay the pilot's, and `fit` stays the pilot's Block 3 `FitResult` at $k^*_i$; `d_hat_um`, `mu_hat_per_um`, `v0_hat_um`, `alpha_hat` and `B_bar` = $\bar B_i(k^{\rm it}_i)$ are the deliverable's; `fit_status` is the SGT fit's when one was made at $k^{\rm it}_i$, else "none". Flags (D-061.3), Block 5's rules and order at $k^{\rm it}_i$: `vertical` (the pilot's); `profile_nan` when the profile at $k^{\rm it}_i$ holds a NaN, else, with a fit, `faint` and `crossing` from that profile and $\bar B_i(k^{\rm it}_i)$ (`_profile_flags`); `stack_edge` when $k^{\rm it}_i$ lies within `edge_margin_planes` of the first or last plane of $K_i$ with a finite $G$ in the round that picked it (`focus.best_plane`'s rule on the iteration's curve); `bbar_few` when $\bar B_i(k^{\rm it}_i)$'s unmasked fraction is below `bbar_min_unmasked_frac` (and, as in Block 5, when there is no plane); `steep` (the pilot's tilt); `dark` when the SGT fit's $\hat\alpha$ > `alpha_dark_flag` (a label from Block 14, D-030); the SGT fit's status when not converged. Endings: no plane picked (round 1 ends `no_plane`, `line_short` or `square_small`): `k_iter` = -1, NaN estimates, `fit_status` "none"; a plane without inputs (`fit_failed`): `k_iter` that plane, NaN estimates, `fit_status` "none"; a plane with a fit, whatever the ending: its fit. [settled 2026-10-21 by D-068: they do not fail $\mathcal S$, and every ending but `converged` adds the label `iter_<ending>` (13.6).] `NodeResult` gains, with defaults so that every existing construction stays valid: `k_iter` (-1), `iter_status` (""), `n_rounds` (0), `n_restacks` (0), `w_hat_um`, `beta_hat`, `xi_hat`, `zeta_hat` (NaN), `irls_ok` (None), `at_bound` (()) -- the SGT fit's --, `d_pilot_um`, `mu_pilot_per_um`, `v0_pilot_um`, `alpha_pilot`, `B_bar_pilot` (NaN), `pilot_status` (""), `pilot_flags` (()) -- the pilot's at $k^*_i$ --, `plane_fit` (None; the `PlaneSGT` at $k^{\rm it}_i$) and `iteration` (None; `gradient_iteration`'s record without its squares). `pilot_view(result) -> NodeResult`: the pilot's result (its fitted columns, status, flags and $\bar B$ in place of the deliverable's); the identity when `pilot_status` is "" (a pilot, or a result built before Block 13)
- 13.6, D-068 and D-069 (implemented 2026-10-21). (D-068) `deliverable_flags(pilot, rec, ks, m, ending=None)`: when `ending` is given and is not `converged`, the label `iter_<ending>` is appended after Block 5's flags; `measure_node` passes the iteration's ending; `phantoms.SELECTION_FLAGS` and `reject_reasons` unchanged, so the label is not a criterion and a non-converged iteration with a fit at its last plane stays in $\mathcal S$ when the rest allows. (D-069) `iterate_planes(..., min_first_half_um=None)`: round 1's half-length is $\max(m\,d^{(0)}/2,$ `min_first_half_um`$)$ when given (ValueError unless finite and $\ge 0$), later rounds $m\,d^{(i-1)}/2$ as before; the round's `half_um` records the line used. `gradient_iteration(..., min_first_samples=None)`: `min_first_half_um` = $\tfrac{n - 1}{2}$ `profile_step_um` for $n$ = `min_first_samples` (ValueError unless an odd integer $\ge 3$); None, the default, keeps the Phase II diagnostics as they were. `measure_node` passes `min_first_samples` = `MeasureConfig.iter_min_first_samples` (3; `validate` refuses a value that is not an odd integer $\ge 3$), in the estimator signature, so the estimator hash changes. Oracles: by hand, round 1's line at the floor for a start too thin for 3 samples, at $m\,d^{(0)}/2$ otherwise, and later rounds never floored; on the fixture a branch of radius 0.01 um ends `line_short` without the floor and picks a plane with it (round 1: 3 samples, half-length $\Delta v$); the label on a node whose iteration ends `max_rounds` (`iter_max_rounds` = 1), its fit kept, the node in $\mathcal S$; no label on a converged node; the default and `validate`'s refusals; the estimator hash differs from `1654924e11020dd9`; the 25 suites pass
- Parameters (13.3b, 13.6): `MeasureConfig.iter_min_first_samples` = 3 (D-069; 13.6), `iter_line_mult` = 3.0 (D-044), `iter_max_rounds` = 5 (D-042; PROVISIONAL, D-052 (h)), `iter_planes_half` = 6 (D-052.3), `iter_bg_margin_um` = 4.0 (D-036; PROVISIONAL, D-052 (h)); `validate` refuses a multiplier that is not finite and positive and a round limit that is not an integer >= 1 (`check_iteration`), `iter_planes_half` < 1 and a margin that is not finite and >= 0; all in the estimator signature, so the estimator hash changes and every table built before is refused (procedure s.3.11)
