Steeper phantoms render more planes (up to 23 at 90 deg), so the mean over D-062's design ($\varphi$ uniform in [0, 90] deg) is above this one **[reasoning]**; $10^6$ realizations at 48 s are $1.3 \times 10^4$ CPU-hours, a lower bound **[arithmetic]**. 13.1-13.5 pushed 2026-10-21 at the user's agreement (`84ea91f..888452a`; a fresh clone has the tested tree). 13.6 implemented and verified 2026-10-21: `tests/smoke/test_smoke_block13.py` 6 pass, 1 skip; 11 of 11 mutants of the new code killed; `check_13_1_move.py` PASS (16 runs: the diagnostics' iteration takes no floor); the 25 suites pass (157 pass, 0 fail, 22 skip); the hashes move to estimator `0508e7ff12f48728`, simulation `bf65d525963d5f0f`

### Block 14: the table under D-058 to D-063 -- the design, the coordinates, the local linear estimator, the inversion, the jobs (S4 of the 2026-10-20 plan; D-030, D-057 (i), D-058 to D-063, D-067.3, D-070.2)

- Modules: `analysis/phantoms.py` (14.1, 14.2), `config.py` (`PhantomConfig`, `CorrectionConfig`), `analysis/table.py` (14.3, 14.4), `analysis/invert.py` and `analysis/cell.py` (14.5), `loading/table_io.py`, `scripts/build_table.py`, `scripts/pbs/build_table.pbs`, `scripts/end_to_end.py`, `configs/gate2_reduced.json` (14.6); `scripts/calibrate_kernel.py` and the checks that build a phantom follow 14.2's draw. Sub-blocks 14.1-14.6, each committed on its own [corrected 2026-10-21: 14.3-14.6 in one commit -- the rows as a dict of columns serve the table and the merge alike]; section 2.4 is rewritten as they land. Status of the whole entry: drafted 2026-10-21 (D-070.2)
- Notation (D-062, D-063): $g = 1, \dots, G$ a draw (one cylinder), $r = 1, \dots, n_{\rm real}$ its realizations; the draw's features $d_g$ (um), $\varphi_g$ (rad), $\theta_g$ (rad), $\mu_g$ (1/um); a realization's nuisances: the lateral offset $(c_{x,g,r}, c_{y,g,r})$ within one pixel, the axis depth $c_{z,g,r}$ within $\pm\Delta z/2$, the branch jitter, the camera noise, and the branch radius $r_{{\rm br},g,r}$ (um); $\hat d_{g,r}$ (um), $\hat\mu_{g,r}$ (1/um) the deliverable's fit (Block 13); $y_{g,r} = \hat d_{g,r}/d_g$ the ratio; $\mathcal S$ the selection event; the table coordinates of a row in $\mathcal S$, $x_{g,r} = (\ln(d_g/{\rm um}),\ \varphi_g,\ \ln(\hat\mu_{g,r}\hat d_{g,r}))$ (`table_phi_axis = "true"`; $\hat\varphi_{g,r}$ in place of $\varphi_g$ under `"measured"`, the labelled comparison), and of every row in the failure count, $x^{\rm fail}_{g,r} = (\ln d_g,\ \varphi_g,\ \ln(\mu_g d_g))$ (D-062.5 with D-063.1); $h = c\,(h_1, h_2, h_3)$ the bandwidths, $(h_1, h_2, h_3)$ = `tau_kernel_bandwidth` (0.15, 5 deg, 0.3; D-054 (ii)) and $c$ the multiplier of D-063.2; the Gaussian weight $\varpi_n(x) = \exp\!\big(-\tfrac12\sum_{k=1}^{3}(x_{n,k} - x_k)^2/h_k^2\big)$ of row $n = (g, r)$ at a point $x$
- 14.1, the selection and the failure count (D-030, D-057.1). `phantoms.SELECTION_FLAGS` = (`faint`, `crossing`, `stack_edge`, `bbar_few`, `profile_nan`): `dark` ($\hat\alpha >$ `alpha_dark_flag`) stays a flag of the node and is no criterion of $\mathcal S$, for phantoms and real nodes, so a dark real node is inverted, not filled, and keeps the label. `CorrectionConfig.failure_rate_ignore = ()` (`# source: D-057`): every row enters the failure count; the field stays, and a screen named there still removes the rows it rejected. Until 14.4 the two-axis spline of section 2.4 reads these rules [implemented 2026-10-21]. Oracles: a node in $\mathcal S$ but for `dark` is in $\mathcal S$ (`reject_reasons` empty) and `correct_nodes` inverts it (its row's flags keep `dark`); at the default every row is counted in the failure rate; `("stack_edge",)` still removes the rows `stack_edge` rejected; Block 10's reasons no longer list `dark` [amended 2026-10-28, D-096: `faint` (a dip under `MeasureConfig.faint_min_dip_gl`, PROVISIONAL 6 gl) leaves `SELECTION_FLAGS` too and stays a label, for phantoms and real nodes: a faint real node is inverted, not filled, and keeps the label, and the table keeps the phantom replicates flagged `faint` (a node whose fit fails or ends at a bound still fails $\mathcal S$ by its status, D-053.2, and is filled). The flags enter the estimator signature (Block 1), so the estimator and simulation hashes change and a table or a row written under the old $\mathcal S$ is refused (`check_estimator`, the merge's `sim_hash`). Oracles: a node in $\mathcal S$ but for `faint` is in $\mathcal S$ and `correct_nodes` inverts it (its flags keep `faint`); `SELECTION_FLAGS` holds neither `faint` nor `dark`; the estimator signature carries `selection.flags`, and a table built for another selection is refused]
- 14.2, the design (D-059, D-062.1-2, D-058). `PhantomConfig.n_draws` = $G$ (100000, D-062.2: a record of the production's size; the run's draw range sets what is rendered), `n_realizations` = $n_{\rm real}$ (10, D-062.2), `radius_noise_sd_um` = $\sigma_{\rm br}$ (0.2 um, D-062.1); `n_replicates` removed. Generators (D-062's keys, indices from 1): draw $g$'s features from `numpy.random.default_rng([seed, g])`, in this order $d_g$, $\varphi_g$, $\theta_g$, $\mu_g$ (the laws of D-024 (iii); the grid design takes cell $(g - 1)$ mod ($n_d n_\varphi$)); realization $r$'s nuisances from `default_rng([seed, g, r])`, in this order $c_x$, $c_y$, $c_z$, the branch jitter, then the camera noise; its radius from `default_rng([seed, g, r, 1])`: $\epsilon \sim \mathcal N(0, \sigma_{\rm br}^2)$ drawn until $d_g/2 + \epsilon > 0$, $r_{{\rm br},g,r} = d_g/2 + \epsilon$, the number of rejected draws recorded (`radius_redraws`). Indices start at 1 because NumPy's `SeedSequence` pads a key with zeros: `default_rng([s, g, 0])` is `default_rng([s, g])` and `default_rng([s, 0])` is `default_rng(s)` (checked, NumPy 2.5.3), so a realization 0 would replay its draw's features; $g$ or $r$ < 1 raises ValueError. The phantom's tube has radius $d_g/2$; its branch carries $r_{{\rm br},g,r}$ at every node, so the one radius reaches the four places of D-062.1 (the iteration's start, the pilot's focus window, the $\bar B$ mask, the iteration's background mask) through `measure_node` unchanged (D-057 (iii)). API: `phantoms.draw_replicate(cfg, seed, g, r) -> (Draw, generator)` (`Draw`: `draw_index`, `realization_index`, `seed`, `d_um`, `phi_rad`, `theta_rad`, `mu_per_um`, `cx_um`, `cy_um`, `cz_um`, `radius_um`, `radius_redraws`; the generator positioned for the jitter and the camera); `phantom_branch(draw, cfg, rng)` (radius `draw.radius_um`); `run_replicate(cfg, seed, g, r, backend=None, d_override_um=None) -> row` (the `Draw`'s fields and today's measured columns; with `d_override_um` the radius is drawn about the overridden $d$). Oracles: draws reproducible per (seed, $g$) and realizations per (seed, $g$, $r$), whatever the order they are run in; the realizations of a draw share $(d_g, \varphi_g, \theta_g, \mu_g)$ and differ in their nuisances; no two of the feature, nuisance and radius streams coincide for $g, r \in \{1, 2, 3\}$; the kept $\epsilon$ at $d$ = 0.2 um follows the normal truncated below at $-d/2$ (Kolmogorov-Smirnov against `scipy.stats.truncnorm`, 4000 draws, $p$ > 0.01) and the mean number of rejections per kept draw is $\Phi(-d/(2\sigma_{\rm br}))/(1 - \Phi(-d/(2\sigma_{\rm br})))$ within 4 SE; every node of the branch carries `radius_um`; $\sigma_{\rm br}$ = 0 gives $d/2$ with no redraw; index 0 refused; D-062's keys and orders, recomputed from NumPy alone, give the same features, nuisances (and the returned generator's position) and radius; the grid design visits cell $(g - 1)$ mod ($n_d n_\varphi$) [added 2026-10-21 with the 14.2 mutation check: 15 of 15 mutants killed]. The run side of the jobs moved here from 14.6 so that every commit runs: `build_table.py run --draw-start S --draw-stop E` and `build_table.pbs`'s chunking by draw (`DIAM_N_DRAWS`, `DIAM_N_TASKS`, `DIAM_DRAW_OFFSET`, as 14.6 states them); `configs/gate2_reduced.json` takes the new phantom fields (48 draws x 5 realizations, $\sigma_{\rm br}$ 0.2 um; PROVISIONAL) and an empty `failure_rate_ignore`; `config_from_dict` refuses the retired `phantom.n_replicates` by name; `calibrate_kernel.calibration_phantom` builds the new `Draw` with the true radius $d/2$ (a calibration phantom's Allen diameter is $d$) [implemented 2026-10-21]
- 14.3, the coordinates (D-063.1, D-054 (ii)). `table.table_inputs(d_um, phi_rad, mu_hat_per_um, d_hat_um) -> (n, 3)` = $(\ln d, \varphi, \ln(\hat\mu\hat d))$ (broadcasting; ValueError unless $d > 0$, $\varphi$ finite and $\hat\mu\hat d$ finite and $> 0$); the failure count's coordinates are `table_inputs(d_g, phi_g, mu_g, d_g)`. `CorrectionConfig.tau_kernel_bandwidth` = (0.15, 5.0, 0.3) ($\ln d$, deg, $\ln(\hat\mu\hat d)$; `# source: D-054 (ii), D-063.1`, PROVISIONAL); `validate()` requires three positive finite values [implemented 2026-10-21: `table_inputs` also refuses an infinite $d$; a row in $\mathcal S$ without a finite $\hat\mu\hat d > 0$ is counted as a failure (`table.table_rows`, its `TableRows`: the rows in $\mathcal S$ `X`, `y`, `draw` and the counted rows `X_fail`, `failed`); the rows may be a list of dicts or a dict of columns (the merge of 14.6)]
- 14.4, the estimator (D-063.2-4, D-067.3). `response_estimator = "local_linear"` (the default; `"tps_spline"` stays for small tables and refuses $N_{\mathcal S}$ above `tps_max_rows`, its CV folds by draw (D-062.3) and `spline_smoothing_grid` extended by $10^3$, $10^4$ with a flag when the choice is at either end (D-062.4)). The grid (D-071.3): three regular axes -- $\ln d$ on $[\ln d_{\min}, \ln d_{\max}]$ (`d_range_um`), $\varphi$ on `phi_range_deg`, $\ln(\mu d)$ on the smallest interval holding the design's band $[\ln(\mu_{\min}d_{\min}), \ln(\mu_{\max}d_{\max})]$ and the $\ln(\hat\mu\hat d)$ of every row in $\mathcal S$ -- with spacing at most $c\,h_k/2$ on axis $k$ at the chosen multiplier $c$, $n_k = \lceil L_k/(c\,h_k/2)\rceil + 1$ points for a range of length $L_k$ ((41, 37, 37) at $c = 1$ when the rows stay inside the band). At each grid point $x$: $A(x) = \sum_{n \in \mathcal S}\varpi_n(x)\,z_n z_n^\top$ and $u(x) = \sum_{n \in \mathcal S}\varpi_n(x)\,z_n y_n$ with $z_n = (1,\ (x_n - x)/h)$ (the offsets scaled per axis), $\hat\beta(x) = A(x)^{-1}u(x)$ and $\hat b(x \mid \mathcal C, \mathcal S) = \hat\beta_0(x)$, so $\hat b(x) = \sum_n \ell_n(x)\,y_n$ with $\ell_n(x) = e_1^\top A(x)^{-1} z_n\,\varpi_n(x)$; NaN where the reciprocal condition number of $A(x)$ is below $10^{-12}$, and where $N^{\rm loc}_{\rm eff}(x)$ (below) is under `min_neff` (10; both PROVISIONAL) -- the support floor: there every array of the table is NaN, and the interpolants read the nearest supported grid value instead, so that a local fit extrapolated far from the rows cannot leak into the support through the cubic interpolant (a query there is caught by `off_support`). The weights are not truncated: every kernel sum $\sum_n f_n\,\varpi_n(x)$ over the grid is a product of per-row one-dimensional factor matrices (the weight is a product over the axes), computed exactly in row chunks (`numpy` matrix products). Residuals $e_n = y_n - \hat b(x_n)$, $\hat b$ at the rows by the interpolant. The reported SE is the cluster-robust sandwich by draw (D-071.2: the one SE reported beside the labelled independent-rows value; its calibration is the 60-table oracle below), $\widehat{\rm Var}(\hat b(x)) = \sum_g\big(\sum_{r:\,(g,r) \in \mathcal S}\ell_{g,r}(x)\,e_{g,r}\big)^2 = e_1^\top A^{-1}\big[\sum_g v_g v_g^\top\big]A^{-1}e_1$, $v_g = \sum_r \varpi_{g,r}z_{g,r}e_{g,r}$ (exact, by the same products: under `table_phi_axis = "true"` the realizations of a draw share their first two coordinates, so $v_g$ factors into the draw's two one-dimensional factors and two sums over its realizations on the third axis; under `"measured"`, by the pairs of realizations of a draw); beside it, labelled, the independent-rows value $\hat\tau(x)/\sqrt{N^{\rm loc}_{\rm eff}(x)}$ with $\hat\tau^2 = \frac{\sum\varpi e^2}{\sum\varpi}\frac{N^{\rm loc}_{\rm eff}}{N^{\rm loc}_{\rm eff} - 1}$, $N^{\rm loc}_{\rm eff} = (\sum\varpi)^2/\sum\varpi^2$ (over $\mathcal S$). Over all rows at $x^{\rm fail}$: the mass $M(x) = \sum_n\varpi_n(x)$, the failure rate $\hat p_{\rm fail}(x \mid \mathcal C) = \sum_n\varpi_n(x)[n \notin \mathcal S]/M(x)$ (less the rows of `failure_rate_ignore`), and $q_{\rm LOO}$ = the 5th percentile (PROVISIONAL, D-063.4) of the rows' leave-one-out masses $M(x^{\rm fail}_n) - 1$ ($M$ at the rows by the interpolant). Queries read tensor cubic interpolants of the grid arrays (`scipy.interpolate.RegularGridInterpolator`, method `"cubic"`, $C^2$, its iterative solver at `rtol` 1e-13: at the default tolerance a grid of 41 x 37 x 37 values is reproduced only to about $2.5 \times 10^{-5}$, checked here; NaN outside the grid). The per-draw table (D-060.2): per draw $g$, $(d_g, \varphi_g, \theta_g, \mu_g)$, $n_{\mathcal S,g}$, $\bar y_g$ (the mean ratio of its realizations in $\mathcal S$), their SD, the failure share $1 - n_{\mathcal S,g}/n_g$ ($n_g$ its rows). The multiplier $c$ (D-071.1): $K$-fold cross-validation with folds by draw (draw $g$ in fold $(g - 1)$ mod $K$, $K$ = `spline_cv_folds`; D-062.3) over `bandwidth_cv_grid` = (0.5, 0.7071, 1, 1.4142, 2) (PROVISIONAL values): for each $c$ and fold $f$, the table of the other folds' rows in $\mathcal S$ ($\hat b$ only, on its own grid at $c$, with the support floor) is read by its interpolant at fold $f$'s rows in $\mathcal S$ ($\ln(\hat\mu\hat d)$ clamped to the grid); the score is the mean squared error of $y$ over all rows in $\mathcal S$, the smallest wins, a choice at either end of the grid is flagged (`cv_at_grid_edge`), the scores are stored; a grid of one value fixes $c$. `BiasTable` holds the axes, the arrays ($\hat b$, the sandwich SE, the independent-rows SE, $\hat\tau$, $N^{\rm loc}_{\rm eff}$, $M$, $\hat p_{\rm fail}$), $q_{\rm LOO}$, the bandwidths, $c$, the counts, the signature and the estimator hash; `table_io` writes the arrays (`.npz`), the record (`.json`) and the per-draw table (`draws_<hash>.csv`). Oracles: on a small design the products equal brute-force sums (1e-12 relative); the weights $\ell_n(x)$ sum to one and annihilate a linear $b$ (1e-10); the interpolant reproduces the grid; on the fixture $b = 1 + 0.06/d + 0.15\sin^2\varphi + 0.05\ln(\mu d)$ under the $G \times n_{\rm real}$ design (a draw-level offset of correlation 0.2) $\hat b$ lies within 4 SE of $b$ at every grid point inside the band and the sandwich SE is within 25 % of the across-table SD (60 tables); the table's file round trip is exact; determinism [implemented 2026-10-21, with these corrections of the draft (the assistant's, PROVISIONAL): (a) each axis spans the design's range joined with the 0.1-99.9 percentile range of the rows' coordinate (`AXIS_ROW_PERCENTILES`), not every row's, so that one stray $\hat\mu$ cannot stretch the grid -- every row still enters every kernel sum, and a query beyond the axis is clamped (`mu_d_out_of_domain`); the rule is the same on the three axes and moves the tilt axis only under `"measured"`; (b) the support floor makes the arrays of the rows in $\mathcal S$ NaN ($\hat b$, both SEs, $\hat\tau$, $N^{\rm loc}_{\rm eff}$) and keeps the mass and the failure rate, which `off_support` and `high_failure` read [the draft said every array: a mass filled from the nearest supported point would hide a query off the support]; (c) the cross-validation's sums are the whole set's less fold $f$'s (exact); (d) `"tps_spline"` fits the coordinates range-normalised by the grid's axes (D-054.1), its smoothing by cross-validation with folds by draw over the extended grid, evaluated on the grid at $c$ = 1, and reports the independent-rows SE (`meta["se_method"]`); (e) queries take $(d, \varphi, \mu d)$ with $\mu d = \hat\mu\hat d$; `BiasTable.meta` is the record; `table_io` writes the format key `grid-3d-2026-10-21` and refuses a table file without it; `table.per_draw_table(rows, cfg)` gives the per-draw table (its file is 14.6's). Oracles as run (`tests/smoke/test_smoke_table.py`): the arrays equal a brute-force evaluation of their definitions within $10^{-12}$ relative (measured $\le 8 \times 10^{-14}$) under both tilt axes; the weights sum to one and annihilate a linear $b$ ($\le 2 \times 10^{-13}$); the sandwich by factors equals the sandwich by pairs ($1.4 \times 10^{-15}$); noise-free linear and constant $b$ are reproduced within $10^{-10}$. [corrected 2026-10-21: the oracle "$\hat b$ within 4 SE of $b$" is replaced by two -- the noise $\hat b - \hat b_0$, $\hat b_0$ the fit of the same design without noise, over the sandwich SE has SD 0.8-1.25 (0.87-1.10 over five seeds), at most 12 % beyond 2 and none beyond 5; and the bias $|\hat b_0 - b| \le 0.003$: at $G$ = 1000 x 5 the local linear fit's $O(h^2)$ bias reaches 0.0023, 1.0-1.3 SE, near the thin end, and "within 4 SE of $b$" failed on the suite's seed and on one of five others **[run, sandbox: `checks/table_2026-10-21/check_noise_z.py`]**.] Over 60 tables of 600 draws x 5 the sandwich SE matches the across-table SD at five points, pooled within 25 % and each within 40 % (measured 0.80-1.20 per point), while the independent-rows SE falls short (0.56-0.93) [corrected: "within 25 %" at each point would fail by chance, the SD of 60 tables carrying about 9 % noise]. Time **[run, sandbox, 2 cores, matrix products about 100 GFLOP/s: `checks/table_2026-10-21/check_cv_timing.py`]**: 80,153 rows in $\mathcal S$ (20,000 draws x 5, the default ranges): the cross-validation over five multipliers 131.5 s (it chose $c$ = 1; scores 9.03-9.17 x $10^{-4}$ for $\tau^2$ = 9 x $10^{-4}$), the table at $c$ 13.7 s; both linear in the rows in $\mathcal S$, so about 27 min and 3 min per $10^6$ of them]
- 14.5, the inversion (D-063.1, D-063.4, D-030). `invert.invert_node(d_hat, phi, mu_hat, table, cfg)`: the root $\tilde d_i$ of $d\,\hat b(\ln d,\ \varphi_i,\ \ln(\hat\mu_i\hat d_i)) = \hat d_i$ on the table's $d$ range (the inversion grid and `brentq` of Block 7); flags `no_estimate` (inputs not finite, $\hat d_i \le 0$ or $\hat\mu_i\hat d_i \le 0$), `phi_out_of_domain`, `mu_d_out_of_domain` (D-071.4, a label only: $\ln(\hat\mu_i\hat d_i)$ outside the table's third axis is clamped to its nearest end, the node is inverted there and keeps the flag, and is not filled for it; the clamp is the assistant's), `out_of_domain`, `non_monotone`, `large_correction`, `high_failure` ($\hat p_{\rm fail}$ at $(\ln\tilde d_i, \varphi_i, \ln(\hat\mu_i\hat d_i))$ above `max_failure_rate`), `off_support` ($M$ there below $q_{\rm LOO}$, D-063.4); any other flag sends the node to the fill, the root kept as `d_root_um`. `correct_nodes` passes `res.mu_hat_per_um`; the per-node CSV gains `mu_d_hat` ($\hat\mu\hat d$). Oracles: `off_support` fires on the node of `check_table_se_coverage.py` C5 (0.5 um at 4 /um) and not in the band's middle; a dark node is corrected and keeps `dark`; the root reproduces $\hat d_i$ (1e-9) [implemented 2026-10-21: `off_support` also fires when a grid point around the root has no support (the trilinear interpolant of the support mask below 1; the assistant's addition, so that a root among filled values is always flagged); `phi_out_of_domain` clamps $\varphi$ for the diagnostic root (the node is filled); `shortcut(d_hat, phi, mu_hat, table)`; `correct_nodes` passes `res.mu_hat_per_um`; `mu_d_hat` follows `fit_status` in the per-node CSV; `end_to_end.evaluate` inverts with the phantom's $(\hat d, \hat\varphi, \hat\mu)$. The C5 check on a fitted table (3000 draws x 2, the default ranges, $c$ = 1): mass 6.2 at C5 against $q_{\rm LOO}$ = 15.2 and 25.0 in the band's middle **[run, sandbox]**; the other oracles read tables built from known grid values (`test_smoke_invert.grid_table`)]
- 14.6, the jobs (D-059, D-062.2; the run side and the PBS chunking implemented with 14.2). `build_table.py run --draw-start S --draw-stop E` renders draws $g = S, \dots, E - 1$ ($S \ge 1$), each with its realizations $r = 1, \dots, n_{\rm real}$, into `rows_<hash>_<S>_<E>.csv`; `merge` reads them, refuses rows of another `sim_hash` and duplicate $(g, r)$, reports draws with fewer than $n_{\rm real}$ rows, and writes the table and the per-draw table. `build_table.pbs`: `DIAM_N_DRAWS` (default `n_draws`), `DIAM_N_TASKS` (= the `-J` size), `DIAM_DRAW_OFFSET` (default 0; task $i$ runs draws $O + i\,c + 1, \dots, O + \min((i + 1)c, N)$, $c = \lceil N/T\rceil$), so a production larger than one array goes in several submissions; walltime and draws per task from the davinci pilot (S6). `end_to_end.py phantoms`: one realization ($r$ = 1) per test phantom, its draw from the test seed; `evaluate` inverts with $(\hat d, \hat\varphi, \hat\mu)$; the gate-2 commands drop `--alpha-range` (D-030). `configs/gate2_reduced.json` in the new schema (three bandwidths, `failure_rate_ignore` empty, `n_draws`, `n_realizations`, `radius_noise_sd_um`, `local_linear`) [implemented 2026-10-21: the merge reads only the columns the table reads (`table_io.read_columns`, typed arrays, so $10^6$ rows hold a few arrays and not $10^6$ dicts; `check_rows_config`, `table_rows` and `per_draw_table` take either form), prints the rows, draws and files beside the recorded `phantom.n_draws`, refuses (exit `merge refused: ...`) a (draw, realization) seen twice, notes the draws with fewer than `n_realizations` rows, writes `draws_<hash>.csv` (`table.per_draw_table`) beside the table and warns when the cross-validated parameter is at an end of its grid; at $10^6$ rows it runs in a job, not on the login node (Block 14.4's timing). `end_to_end.py`'s gate-2 commands use `--draw-start/--draw-stop` and `configs/gate2_reduced.json`, without `--alpha-range` (kept as a labelled comparison); the gate-2 configuration's correction section in the new schema (`local_linear`, three bandwidths, the CV grid, `min_neff`, `loo_mass_percentile`, `tps_max_rows`). Oracles (`test_smoke_table.py` `test_contract`): the CLI's two rendered chunks merged with 200 synthetic draws give `n_all` 204, the note on the 200 draws short of $n_{\rm real}$, the per-draw file of 202 draws; a rows file copied under a second name is refused]
- Parameters: `PhantomConfig.n_draws`, `n_realizations`, `radius_noise_sd_um` (in the simulation signature); `CorrectionConfig.response_estimator`, `tau_kernel_bandwidth`, `failure_rate_ignore`, `tps_max_rows`, `spline_smoothing_grid`, `spline_cv_folds` (folds over the draws), `bandwidth_cv_grid` (D-071.1), `min_neff` (the support floor), `loo_mass_percentile` (5.0, D-063.4); `max_failure_rate` (0.2, PROVISIONAL until the davinci pilot, D-054 (iii))
- Library calls relied on: `numpy` (matrix products, `linalg`), `scipy.interpolate` (the cubic interpolant), `scipy.optimize.brentq`, `scipy.stats.truncnorm` (oracle only)
- Custom code: the tensor-product kernel sums and the sandwich by pairs (no library computes a local linear smoother with a by-cluster sandwich on a grid); the radius redraw
- Confirmed: D-030, D-057 (i), D-058, D-059, D-060 (its target and per-draw table), D-062, D-063 (the user); D-071 (the user, 12:30): $c$ by cross-validation, the sandwich alone, the spacing at half the chosen bandwidths, `mu_d_out_of_domain` a label; the 1-based indices, the field names, the untruncated sums, the third axis's extent, the support floor, the clamp, the reciprocal-condition threshold and the CV grid's values are the assistant's (2026-10-21)
- Status: drafted 2026-10-21; its [to confirm] items settled by D-071 (2026-10-21, 12:30); 14.1-14.6 implemented 2026-10-21 (local commits on `sci/diameter-pipeline`); gate 2 (S5) next

### Block 15: the far-field calibration (S2b of the 2026-10-20 plan; D-026, D-026.1, D-027, D-067.4, D-070, D-071.5, D-074 to D-081)

- Modules: `src/allen_diameter/analysis/far_field.py` (the far statistic, the far scan's statistics, the plane selection, the far fit, its SE), `src/allen_diameter/analysis/calibration_scene.py` (shared with Block 10: the patch, the far pixels, the planar background, isolation, straightness), `src/allen_diameter/analysis/calibration.py` (`scan_node` scans the far set through `far_field.far_plane_scan`), `src/allen_diameter/config.py` (`CalibrationConfig`'s far fields; in no signature), `src/allen_diameter/loading/calibration_io.py` (the far scans in the scans file, `save_far_fit`), `src/allen_diameter/plotting/calibration_figures.py` (D-075, D-080.2, D-081.5), `scripts/calibrate_kernel.py` (`real` and `synthetic --set far` scan the far set, `far-fit`, `figures`), notebook Cell 7 and the runbook. Rewritten 2026-10-23 from the draft of 2026-10-21 with D-074 to D-081; the items the user settled are cited by decision, the assistant's readings marked PROVISIONAL
- Notation (D-026, D-074): $j$ a node of the far set (D-067.4: Allen's diameter $0.6 < D_{{\rm A},j} \le 0.8$ um; in $\mathcal S$, flat, faint, isolated and straight by Block 10's criteria); $z_{{\rm p},j}$ the pilot's sub-plane depth (um), $z_k = k\,\Delta z$ plane $k$'s depth, $s_j$ the fitted axis shift (um), $\hat\delta_{j,k} = z_{{\rm p},j} + s_j - z_k$ (object minus plane, as Block 10), $\delta_0 = P\,\Delta z$ = 0.84 um the near field's reach, $x_{j,k} = |\hat\delta_{j,k}| - \delta_0$; $\hat\omega_{j,k}$ (um$^2$) the squared Gaussian-core width of plane $k$'s dip; $\varsigma$ the fitted core SD (um; $\hat\omega = \hat\varsigma^2$), FWHM$(\varsigma) = 2\sqrt{2\ln 2}\,\varsigma$; $\sigma_{\rm p}(k) = \sigma_{\rm r}(|z_{{\rm p},j} - z_k|)$ the prior width from the renderer's current law; $\hat\sigma_0$ the anchor, Block 10's $\sigma_{\rm r}(\delta_0)$; $\gamma$ the far slope (dimensionless) of (D-026.1), $\sigma_{\rm r}(\delta) = \sigma_0 + \gamma\,(|\delta| - \delta_0)$ for $|\delta| > \delta_0$; $\gamma_2$ the quadratic comparison coefficient (D-081.6); $\kappa$ the core fit's stopping tolerance (D-077.2)
- 15.1, the far scan (`far_field.far_plane_scan`, called by `calibration.scan_node` for a far node; D-074.1, D-074.2, D-078.1, D-081.2): the patch of Block 10.P over the planes $k^*_j - P_{\rm far}, \dots, k^*_j + P_{\rm far}$, $P_{\rm far}$ = `far_offsets_planes` = 16 (4.48 um on both sides of focus [user]); in each plane the far pixels, the planar background $\hat B_k$ and its reading on the line (Block 10.P); Block 10's fixed line through the pilot's centre with heading $\theta_j$, of half-length `far_profile_half_um` = 10 um [user] sampled every `profile_step_um` (the fit's extraction, along-branch averaging on); the flattened profile $I'_k(v) = I_k(v) - \hat B_k({\rm line}(v)) + \hat B_k({\rm line}(0))$ and the dip $w_{j,k}(v) = \hat B_k({\rm line}(0)) - I'_k(v)$ (gl). The planes with $|k - k^*_j| \le 3$ are scanned and reported too (D-074.1), out of the fit. The scan keeps per plane the profile $w_{j,k}(v_n)$ and the background along the line (D-075)
- 15.2, the far statistic (`far_field.core_fit_iterative`; D-077, D-078.2, D-081.4): the peak $n^\circ$ = the smallest sample of $I'_k$ smoothed by $\max(\sigma_{\rm p}/(2\,{\rm step}),\ $`focus_smooth_px`$)$ samples among those within `fit_v0_bounds_um`; depth = $\hat B_k({\rm line}(0)) - $ the smoothed value there ($\le 0$: `no_dip`). Passes $i = 1, 2, \dots, N$: the Gaussian core $a\,e^{-(v - v_{\rm c})^2/(2\varsigma^2)}$ is fitted by least squares (scipy `least_squares`, trf, tolerances 1e-12) to $w_{j,k}$ on the samples with $|v_n - v_{\rm c}^{(i-1)}| \le$ `core_fit_fwhm_factor` x FWHM$(\varsigma^{(i-1)})$, with $\varsigma^{(0)} = \sigma_{\rm p}(k)$ and $v_{\rm c}^{(0)} = v_{n^\circ}$, $\varsigma$ bounded to `far_core_width_bounds` x $\sigma_{\rm p}$ = $[0.25, 4]\,\sigma_{\rm p}$ (a fit at a bound ends the plane: `at_bound`), $v_{\rm c}$ within half the window of its start, $a \ge 0$; fewer than `min_window_samples` in the window: `few_samples`; a solver failure: `fit_failed`. After pass $i \ge 2$ the loop stops when $|\hat\varsigma^{(i)} - \hat\varsigma^{(i-1)}| \le \kappa\,{\rm SE}(\hat\varsigma^{(i)})$ (equivalently on the FWHM, D-077.2), $\kappa$ = `far_core_stop_tol` = 1 [user], with ${\rm SE}(\hat\varsigma) = {\rm RMS}\,[(J^\top J)^{-1}]^{1/2}_{\varsigma\varsigma}$, $J$ the core's Jacobian in $(a, v_{\rm c}, \varsigma)$ at the fit and ${\rm RMS} = [\sum_{\rm window}(\text{fit} - w)^2/(n_{\rm win} - 3)]^{1/2}$ (gl); no stop by pass $N$ = `far_core_max_passes` = 10 [user]: `not_converged`, the plane left out of the fit and counted (D-081.4). Per plane: $\hat\omega = \hat\varsigma^2$, ${\rm SE}_{\rm own}(\hat\omega) = 2\hat\varsigma\,{\rm SE}(\hat\varsigma)$, $\hat a$, $\hat v_{\rm c}$, RMS, $n_{\rm win}$, the passes, the last window, the status; the line-end mean of $w$ over the samples with $|v_n - \hat v_{\rm c}| \ge$ `far_line_end_fwhm_factor` (2) x FWHM$(\hat\varsigma)$ when both sides hold at least `far_line_end_min_samples` (4) of them, NaN otherwise -- the check of $\hat B_k$ where the zone exists (D-078.1). The two-pass statistic of the S2b checks is this loop with $N$ = 2 and no stop (verified: `check_iterative_core.py`, 2026-10-23)
- 15.3, the plane selection (`far_field.select_far_planes`; D-074.1's SNR rule on the predicted dip, confirmed by D-078.3): per node $A_j$ = the median over the planes with status `ok` and $|k - k^*_j| \in$ [`far_area_offsets`] = [4, 6] of $\hat a\,\hat\varsigma\sqrt{2\pi}$ (gl um; the dip's area, the same in every plane in the model), ${\rm RMS}_j$ = the median RMS over the node's `ok` planes; $K_j = \{k : |k - k^*_j| \ge$ `far_min_k_offset` = 5 $(x \ge 0.4$ um $\ge r_j$, D-026 (g)), status `ok`, $A_j / (\sqrt{2\pi}\,\sigma_{\rm p}(k)) \ge$ `far_min_snr` (3) x ${\rm RMS}_j\}$; a node with fewer than `far_min_planes_per_node` (6, PROVISIONAL) planes in $K_j$, or without $A_j$, is dropped and named. The rule on the predicted dip, not the observed amplitude, keeps the selection independent of each plane's own noise (at 2 gl the observed rule biased $\hat\gamma$ by -1.1 to -1.7 %, D-074's Why)
- 15.4, the far fit (`far_field.fit_far_slope`; D-078.3, D-081.6, D-081.7): sequential -- the anchor $\hat\sigma_0$ and its SE come from Block 10's near fit (10.A) and are held fixed. Over $(j, k \in K_j)$, with $\theta = (c \in \mathbb R^J, s \in [-b, b]^J, \gamma \in [0, \gamma_{\max}])$, $b$ = `z_ax_bound_um` (0.28), $\gamma_{\max}$ = `far_gamma_max` (5, PROVISIONAL), the residual $r_{j,k}(\theta) = c_j + (\hat\sigma_0 + \gamma\,x_{j,k}(s_j))^2 - \hat\omega_{j,k}$ and the weights frozen per round (IRLS, D-078.3; F2 with the weights inside the minimisation is a different, biased estimator): $\theta^{(0)} = \arg\min \sum r_{j,k}(\theta)^2$ from $c = 0$, $s = 0$, $\gamma = 1$; $\theta^{(t+1)} = \arg\min \sum r_{j,k}(\theta)^2 / {\rm SE}_{{\rm m},j,k}(\theta^{(t)})^2$ for $t = 0, \dots, R - 1$, $R$ = `far_irls_rounds` = 3 (scipy `least_squares`, trf, analytic Jacobian, tolerances 1e-10); $\hat\theta = \theta^{(R)}$. ${\rm SE}_{{\rm m},j,k}(\theta)$: the SE of $\hat\omega$ a Gaussian-core fit would have at that plane if the dip were the Gaussian the model predicts -- SD $w_{\rm pred} = [\max(F_{j,k}(\theta), 0.01)]^{1/2}$ with $F_{j,k}(\theta) = c_j + (\hat\sigma_0 + \gamma x_{j,k}(s_j))^2$, amplitude $A_j / (\sqrt{2\pi}\,w_{\rm pred})$, white noise ${\rm RMS}_{j,k}$, sampled every `profile_step_um` over $\pm$FWHM$(w_{\rm pred})$ cut at `far_profile_half_um`: ${\rm SE}_{\rm m} = 2 w_{\rm pred}\,{\rm RMS}_{j,k}\,[(J^\top J)^{-1}]^{1/2}_{\varsigma\varsigma}$ (the inverse Fisher information of $(a, v_{\rm c}, \varsigma)$). $\hat\gamma$ at 0 or $\gamma_{\max}$ fails the fit (`at_bound`). Reported with $\hat\gamma$: $\hat c$, $\hat s$, the residuals and their RMS by $|k - k^*|$, the measured range $\delta_0 + \max x_{j,k}$, the planes used per node; the per-sign fit ($\gamma_+$ on $\hat\delta > 0$, $\gamma_-$ on $\hat\delta < 0$, the same $c$, $s$; the test of $K_\delta = K_{-\delta}$, procedure s.3.10); the quadratic comparison $\sigma_{\rm r} = \hat\sigma_0 + \gamma x + \gamma_2 x^2$ with $\gamma_2 \in \mathbb R$ free, fitted the same way and reported as a labelled comparison only (D-026, D-081.6)
- 15.5, the SE (D-078.3, D-081.7): ${\rm SE}(\hat\gamma)^2 = {\rm SE}_{\rm boot}(\hat\gamma)^2 + (\partial\hat\gamma/\partial\sigma_0)^2\,{\rm SE}(\hat\sigma_0)^2$: ${\rm SE}_{\rm boot}$ = the SD of $\hat\gamma^*$ over `bootstrap_resamples` (1000) resamples of whole nodes with replacement (every plane of a drawn node, the full IRLS refitted; seed from the script; a resample whose fit fails is dropped and counted); $\partial\hat\gamma/\partial\sigma_0$ by the central difference $[\hat\gamma(\hat\sigma_0 + h) - \hat\gamma(\hat\sigma_0 - h)]/(2h)$, $h$ = `anchor_sensitivity_step_um` (0.01); ${\rm SE}(\hat\sigma_0)$ from Block 10.A. The interval $\hat\gamma \pm t_{J-1,\,0.975}\,{\rm SE}(\hat\gamma)$ (scipy `stats.t.ppf`; the node bootstrap read 0.83 of the true SD at $J$ = 12, D-074's Why). No cross term: the near set ($D_{\rm A} \le$ 0.6 um) and the far set are different nodes (D-078.3)
- 15.6, the output (D-080.1, D-081.7): `calibration_io.save_far_fit` writes $\hat\gamma$, its SE and its parts, the interval, $\hat c$, $\hat s$, the planes used, the range, the residual RMS by offset, the per-sign and quadratic comparisons, the anchor and its SE, the dropped nodes and the counts of `not_converged` and SNR-dropped planes, and the renderer table for production: `kernel_from_growth(growth, renderer, sigma_r0, max_delta_um=`table_max_delta_um`)` with `kernel_continuation = "linear"` and `kernel_continuation_slope` = $\hat\gamma$, so the continuation starts at the anchor (D-080.1); `RendererConfig.kernel_continuation_slope` = $\hat\gamma$ in the production configuration (Cell 8; in the simulation hash); beyond the measured range the law is extrapolated and the record says so
- 15.7, figures (`plotting/calibration_figures.py`; D-075, D-080.2, D-081.5): see Block 10.F; the far stage's width-by-plane figure spans $k - k^*_j \in [-16, 16]$ with the far law drawn for $|\delta| \ge \delta_0$ and the near table inside; the fitted-Gaussian figures of D-075.2 draw each plane's dip with the last pass's core and window
- Public API: `far_field.core_fit_iterative(v, w, depth, v_peak, sigma_p, cal, mcfg) -> dict` (`status`, `omega_um2`, `se_omega_um2`, `amp_gl`, `v_c_um`, `sigma_um`, `rms_gl`, `n_win`, `passes`, `window_half_um`); `far_field.far_plane_statistics(v, I_flat, B0, sigma_p, mcfg, cal) -> dict` (adds `depth_gl`, `line_end_gl`); `far_field.far_plane_scan(patch, node, branch, scene, cfg, keep_images=False) -> PlaneScan` (Block 10's dataclass, `set = "far"`); `far_field.select_far_planes(scans, cfg) -> FarSelection` (frozen: `node_ids`, `area_gl_um`, `rms_gl`, per observation `node`, `k`, `z_p_um`, `z_um`, `omega_um2`, `rms_gl`; `dropped` {node_id: reason}; `n_not_converged`, `n_snr_dropped`); `far_field.se_model(amp_gl, sigma_um, rms_gl, step_um, half_um) -> ndarray`; `far_field.fit_far_slope(sel, sigma0_um, cfg, per_sign=False, quadratic=False, sigma0_fixed=True) -> FarFit` (frozen: `gamma`, `gamma_minus` (per-sign), `gamma2` (quadratic), `c_um2`, `s_um`, `node_ids`, `obs_node`, `obs_k`, `residual_um2`, `weights`, `range_um`, `success`, `at_bound`, `message`, `nfev`; `rms_by_offset()`); `far_field.bootstrap_gamma(sel, sigma0_um, cfg, rng) -> (se, n_ok, draws)`; `far_field.anchor_sensitivity(sel, sigma0_um, cfg) -> float`; `far_field.far_fit_summary(...) -> dict` (the JSON content); `calibration_io.save_far_fit(path, summary, kernel)`; `scripts/calibrate_kernel.py far-fit --scans --calibration --out [--seed --config-json]`
- Parameters (`CalibrationConfig`; the user's values cited, the rest PROVISIONAL): `far_offsets_planes` 16 [user], `far_profile_half_um` 10 [user], `far_min_snr` 3 [user], `far_min_k_offset` 5, `far_area_offsets` (4, 6), `far_min_planes_per_node` 6, `far_core_max_passes` 10 [user], `far_core_stop_tol` 1.0 [user], `far_core_width_bounds` (0.25, 4.0), `far_line_end_fwhm_factor` 2, `far_line_end_min_samples` 4, `far_irls_rounds` 3, `far_gamma_max` 5, `bootstrap_resamples` 1000 [user], `anchor_sensitivity_step_um` 0.01 [user], `table_max_delta_um` 0.84 [user]; with Block 10's `z_ax_bound_um`, `core_fit_fwhm_factor`, `min_window_samples`, `patch_half_um`, `far_pixel_sigmas`, `isolation_depth_gl`; `MeasureConfig`'s `profile_step_um`, `along_branch_avg_um`, `focus_smooth_px`, `fit_v0_bounds_um`
- Library calls relied on: `scipy.optimize.least_squares`, `scipy.ndimage.gaussian_filter1d`, `scipy.stats.t`, `numpy.linalg.inv`, `numpy.linalg.lstsq` (the planar background), Block 10's `kernel_from_growth` and patch functions, Block 13's `Scene`, `scene_segments`
- Custom code: the iterative core fit (a windowed Gaussian fit re-windowed on its own width; no library fits with a self-set window), the predicted-dip selection, the far fit with its IRLS and model SE (no library fits Eq. 4 with a parametric far law and per-node nuisance parameters), the node bootstrap
- Test oracles (`tests/smoke/test_smoke_far_field.py`): (a) known answer: on a sampled Gaussian dip the iterative fit returns $\hat\omega = s^2$ to 1e-9 and stops at pass 2; with the stop disabled and $N$ = 2 it equals the two-pass statistic of the S2b checks on noisy draws (same window rule); `se_model` on a Gaussian of SD $s$ equals the Gauss-Newton SE of a fit to that Gaussian plus white noise, averaged over draws, within 15 %; (b) reference: exact (F1) data ($\omega = c_j + (\sigma_0 + \gamma x)^2$ on planes $|k| \ge 5$, both sides, axes off-plane) return $\gamma$, $c$ and $s$ to 1e-8 by the unweighted and the IRLS fits; the per-sign fit returns $\gamma_+ = \gamma_-$; the quadratic fit returns $\gamma_2 = 0$ to 1e-6; (c) Phase I: far-set phantoms ($d$ = 0.7 um, flat, $\mu d$ 0.35, the camera chain) rendered with the configuration's table and $\gamma$ = 0.79 and 1.21 over $\pm$8 planes on a $\pm$12 um patch (a reduced configuration, for time) are scanned by `scan_node` through the planar background and return $\hat\gamma$ within 10 % without camera noise; (d) invariants: a constant added to every $\hat\omega$ moves only the $c_j$; shifting every $z_{{\rm p},j}$ by $u$ moves the $s_j$ by $-u$; the SNR rule drops the farthest planes as RMS grows; the anchor sensitivity has the sign of $-1$ and $|\partial\hat\gamma/\partial\sigma_0| < 1$ per um on (F1) data with noise; (e) contract: `FarSelection` and `FarFit` fields and shapes, the far scans' JSON round trip, `save_far_fit`'s table ends at 0.84 um with the fitted slope and `validate()` passes; the command line `synthetic --set far` -> `far-fit`; (f) determinism; (g) edge cases: a flat profile (`no_dip`), a width at the bound (`at_bound`), too few far pixels (`bg_few`), one far node (ValueError), $\hat\gamma$ at $\gamma_{\max}$ (`success` False), a node without $A_j$ dropped and named
- Data flow: consumes Blocks 5 (the pilot), 10 (the anchor, its SE, the patch and background functions, the selection), 13 (the scene); feeds Block 4's kernel (the table and the continuation slope) and so Block 14's table; the figures of 10.F
- Confirmed: D-026, D-026.1, D-027, D-067.4, D-070, D-071.5, D-074.1-2, D-077.1-2, D-078, D-079, D-080, D-081 (the user); `far_min_k_offset`, `far_area_offsets`, `far_min_planes_per_node`, `far_core_width_bounds`, `far_line_end_*`, `far_irls_rounds`, `far_gamma_max`, the smoothing of the peak search, the soma's isolation margin and the trimming of the background are the assistant's, PROVISIONAL
- Status: drafted 2026-10-23; implemented 2026-10-23 (`analysis/far_field.py`, `analysis/calibration_scene.py`, the far branch of `calibration.scan_node`, `calibration_io.save_far_fit` and the patches file, `scripts/calibrate_kernel.py` `synthetic --set far` / `far-fit` / `figures`, notebook Cell 7 and runbook v22; the estimator and simulation hashes unchanged, `0508e7ff12f48728` and `0ea5a6d2ec069c68`). Phase I on rendered far phantoms ($d$ = 0.7 um, flat, the configuration's table continued with the planted slope, a reduced configuration: patch $\pm$12 um, $P_{\rm far}$ = 8): noise-free, $\hat\gamma$ within 1 % of 0.79 and of 1.21 (3 nodes each); with the camera chain, $\hat\gamma$ = 0.83 $\pm$ 0.017 against 0.79 (4 nodes; the SE is the bootstrap's with the anchor term). The full chain `synthetic --set near` and `--set far` -> `fit` -> `far-fit` -> `figures` ran in the sandbox and the figures were read (the width-by-plane medians follow the planted laws; the example node's patch images show the far mask and the window). Suites: `test_smoke_far_field.py` 6 pass, 1 skip (11 min: the iterative statistic against the sampled Gaussian and the two-pass statistic, `se_model` against the Gauss-Newton SE, the F1 reference by the unweighted and the IRLS fits, the per-sign and quadratic forms, the SNR rule, the rendered phantoms), `test_smoke_calibration_figures.py` 6 pass, 1 skip. Phase II (Allen's data, Colab Cell 7) not run; the anchor's SE and the far interval on real nodes unknown until then. Smoke-tested **[run, sandbox]**

### Block 16: S3 across cells -- the cell programme (S3 of the 2026-10-20 plan; D-065, D-067.1, D-085)

S3 runs the real-data surveys over many Allen cells instead of specimen 529878215 alone. Block 16 is the programme's
frame: 16.1 the cell pool, the acquisition check and the stratified selection (smoke-tested 2026-10-26); 16.2 the global
alignment of every selected cell and the resumable per-cell runner (smoke-tested 2026-10-26); 16.3 the check of what
Allen's projections are, run once in Colab (drafted 2026-10-27, D-090.1). Blocks 17-20 (S3a-S3d) consume it.

- Modules: `src/allen_diameter/loading/cell_pool.py`, `src/allen_diameter/analysis/cell_selection.py`, `scripts/cell_pool.py`, `src/allen_diameter/analysis/cell_alignment.py`, `src/allen_diameter/loading/survey_io.py`, `scripts/survey_cells.py`, `src/allen_diameter/plotting/survey_figures.py`, `src/allen_diameter/analysis/projection_check.py`, `scripts/projection_check.py`, `src/allen_image_io.py`, `src/allen_diameter/config.py` (every module of 16.1-16.3, for the roadmap tool, which reads this line; each sub-block's own line below) [added 2026-10-27]
- Modules (16.1): `src/allen_diameter/loading/cell_pool.py`, `src/allen_diameter/analysis/cell_selection.py`, `src/allen_diameter/config.py` (`SurveyConfig`, in no signature), `scripts/cell_pool.py` (`pool`, `check`, `select`), notebook Cell 11 and the runbook
- 16.1 (a), the pool (D-085.1; the reading PROVISIONAL): the human cells with a reconstruction in Allen's database. `cell_pool.fetch_specimen_details` pages the RMA model `ApiCellTypesSpecimenDetail` through `allen_image_io.api_query` (`pool_page_rows` rows per page, until a page comes back short; at most 50 pages). The SDK reads this model in one query (`num_rows` 'all', `CellTypesApi.list_cells_api`); `api_query` takes a number, so `pool_page_rows` = 10000 is meant to hold the model in one page, and a second page raises a RuntimeWarning: the query names no order, and the pages of an unordered query may skip or repeat rows (a repeated id is caught below, a skipped one is not) [added 2026-10-26 with the smoke test]. `cell_pool.pool_records` keeps a row when its species equals `survey.species` ("Homo Sapiens", compared case-insensitively) and its reconstruction type is not null -- the filter of the Allen SDK's `CellTypesApi.filter_cells_api` (`nr__reconstruction_type is not None`; `donor__species` lower-cased) **[src: allensdk 2.16.2, `api/queries/cell_types_api.py`, read 2026-10-26]**, which the passive-fit pipeline reaches through `CellTypesCache.get_cells(species=[HUMAN], require_reconstruction=True)` **[src: `Passive Features/Allen Institute Data/save_alleninstitute_data (6).py`, `list_human_cells_with_morphology`]**. The rows' raw field names are the ones the SDK reads from this model (`filter_cells_api`, `simplify_cells_api` **[src: allensdk 2.16.2, read 2026-10-26]**) [corrected 2026-10-26: drafted as NOT VERIFIED]; they are not checked against the live API, which the sandbox cannot reach. Each canonical field is read from the first present of its candidate keys -- the raw API name, then the SDK's simplified name (`specimen_id`: `specimen__id`, `id`, `specimen_id`; `species`: `donor__species`, `species`; `layer`: `structure__layer`, `structure_layer_name`, `layer`; `dendrite_type`: `tag__dendrite_type`, `dendrite_type`; `reconstruction_type`: `nr__reconstruction_type`, `reconstruction_type`; `area`: `structure_parent__acronym`, `structure_area_abbrev`, `area`; `donor_id`: `donor__id`, `donor_id`; `hemisphere`: `specimen__hemisphere`, `structure_hemisphere`, `hemisphere`; `normalized_depth`: `csl__normalized_depth`, `normalized_depth`) -- and a required field (`specimen_id`, `species`, `layer`, `dendrite_type`, `reconstruction_type`) missing from a row raises ValueError naming the keys the row holds. `--from-csv` reads the same canonical columns from a CSV instead (for example one exported in Colab from `list_human_cells_with_morphology`). The record: `specimen_id` (int), `species`, `layer` (Allen's label, a string), `dendrite_type`, `reconstruction_type`, `area`, `donor_id`, `hemisphere`, `normalized_depth` (float, NaN when absent); one record per specimen (a repeated id keeps its first row); None, the empty string and the strings "none", "null", "nan" (any case) read as absent
- 16.1 (b), the class (D-085.1): `cell_selection.neuron_class(dendrite_type, class_map)` = the class `class_map` assigns to the dendrite type (exact, case-insensitive match), `survey.class_map` = (spiny, exc), (aspiny, inh), (sparsely spiny, sparsely_spiny) [the user's exc/inh, the mapping PROVISIONAL; sparsely spiny a third class, D-087, its name the assistant's, confirmed by the user, D-089] [amended 2026-10-26, later: the draft left sparsely spiny cells without a class]; an unmapped or missing type has no class: the cell is listed and counted, never selected
- 16.1 (c), the acquisition check (D-085.3): `cell_pool.acquisition_record(specimen_id)` per cell -- `allen_image_io.reconstruction_info` (NeuronReconstruction: `scale_factor_x`, `scale_factor_y`, `scale_factor_z`; the plane spacing $\Delta z$ is `scale_factor_z`, as the configuration's `acquisition.dz_um` = 0.28 um was read for 529878215) and `list_images` with `plane_table` (the usable Primary planes: their count, the distinct values of `resolution`, um/px at downsample 0, their median width and height in px); a query that fails is the record's `error`. Record: `specimen_id`, `dz_um`, `scale_x`, `scale_y`, `pixel_um` (the planes' resolution when they share one value; NaN otherwise), `pixel_values` (the distinct values, ";"-joined), `n_planes`, `n_planes_failed`, `width_px`, `height_px`, `error`. `cell_selection.matches_acquisition(record, cfg)`: $|\Delta z_c - \Delta z| \le \epsilon\,\Delta z$ and $|p_c - p| \le \epsilon\,p$ with $\Delta z$ = `acquisition.dz_um`, $p$ = `acquisition.res0_um` (0.1144 um/px), $\epsilon$ = `survey.acquisition_rel_tol` (1e-3, PROVISIONAL); a NaN, an error or no plane never matches. `cell_selection.acquisition_summary(records, cfg)`: the cells checked and failed, the distinct $\Delta z_c$ and $p_c$ (rounded to 1e-4 um) with their counts (a cell whose planes hold several pixel sizes is counted under "multiple: <values>"), the matching count, the ids that do not match and why (`error`, `no_planes`, `dz`, `pixel`, `dz;pixel`). The check runs over the whole pool before anything else; what is done with cells that differ is decided on its report (D-085.3, the user)
- 16.1 (d), the selection (D-085.1): over the pool's cells with a class -- and, with `require_match`, an acquisition record that matches -- the groups $g$ = (class, layer); the cells of group $g$ in increasing `specimen_id`, permuted by `numpy.random.default_rng([seed, crc32(key_g)])` with key_g = "class|layer" (ASCII) and seed = `survey.selection_seed` (20261026, PROVISIONAL), so that a group's draw does not depend on the other groups; the first `survey.cells_per_group` (5) of the permutation, or all of the group's cells when it has fewer [user]. Output: the selected records with `neuron_class`, `group`, `rank` (the position in the permutation, from 0); per group the counts available and selected; the cells left out with the reason (`no_class`, `no_layer`, `acquisition_missing`, `acquisition_mismatch:<why>` with the reason of (c))
- Public API: `cell_pool.fetch_specimen_details(api=None, page_rows=10000, max_pages=50) -> list of dict`; `cell_pool.normalize_row(row) -> dict`; `cell_pool.pool_records(rows, species="Homo Sapiens", require_reconstruction=True) -> list of dict`; `cell_pool.acquisition_record(specimen_id) -> dict`; `cell_pool.write_table(path, rows, columns)` (exactly `columns`, a row with another key raises ValueError; written to `path.tmp` and moved into place; None empty, NaN "nan"), `cell_pool.read_table(path, types) -> list of dict` (each value parsed by its column's type, `int`, `float` or `str`; an empty cell None, or NaN in a float column); the columns and types `POOL_COLUMNS`/`POOL_TYPES`, `ACQUISITION_COLUMNS`/`ACQUISITION_TYPES`, `SELECTION_COLUMNS`/`SELECTION_TYPES`; `cell_selection.neuron_class(dendrite_type, class_map) -> str or None`; `cell_selection.matches_acquisition(record, cfg) -> (bool, reason)`; `cell_selection.acquisition_summary(records, cfg) -> dict` (`n_cells`, `n_failed`, `dz_um_counts`, `pixel_um_counts`, `n_match`, `mismatch`, `configuration`); `cell_selection.group_rng(seed, key) -> numpy Generator`; `cell_selection.select_cells(pool, cfg, acquisition=None, require_match=False) -> Selection` (frozen: `selected` tuple of dict, `groups` {key: (n_available, n_selected)}, `excluded` {specimen_id: reason}, `seed`; `require_match` without acquisition records raises ValueError)
- Script `scripts/cell_pool.py`: `pool --out POOL.csv [--from-csv FILE]` (prints the cells by class and layer and the unmapped dendrite types); `check --pool POOL.csv --out ACQ.csv [--summary ACQ.json] [--max-cells N] [--fresh]` (one line per cell; resumable: the cells already in ACQ.csv are skipped unless `--fresh`; the table rewritten atomically every 10 cells and at the end; prints the summary and writes it, by default to ACQ.json); `select --pool POOL.csv --out SEL.csv [--acquisition ACQ.csv] [--require-match] [--seed S] [--per-group N] [--summary SEL.json]` (writes SEL.csv and SEL.json: the seed, the rule, the groups' counts, the cells left out and their reasons); `--config-json` on each (the survey section is not written by `to_json`, which writes the signatures only: a `--config-json` that changes it is written by hand, e.g. `{"survey": {"pool_page_rows": 50000}}`, the other sections taking their defaults)
- Parameters (`SurveyConfig`, in no signature, like `CalibrationConfig`): `cells_per_group` 5 [user, D-085.1], `class_map` ((spiny, exc), (aspiny, inh), (sparsely spiny, sparsely_spiny)) [exc/inh PROVISIONAL; the third class D-087, its name confirmed by the user, D-089], `selection_seed` 20261026 PROVISIONAL, `acquisition_rel_tol` 1e-3 PROVISIONAL, `pool_page_rows` 10000 PROVISIONAL [2026-10-26: 2000 in the draft; one page, see (a)], `species` "Homo Sapiens" [the SDK's `CellTypesApi.HUMAN`]
- Library calls relied on: `allen_image_io.api_query`, `list_images`, `plane_table`, `reconstruction_info` (Block 0); numpy (`random.default_rng`), pandas (the DataFrame of `list_images`: the median width and height), `csv` (the tables), `zlib.crc32` (a stable key per group), `warnings`
- Custom code: the field normalization and the seeded stratified selection (a permutation per group, numpy's Generator); no numerical method
- Test oracles (`tests/smoke/test_smoke_cell_pool.py`): (a) known answer: canned API rows (raw names) in pages of 10 rows give the human cells with a reconstruction and nothing else, with a RuntimeWarning for the unordered pages, and in one page without a warning at the default page size; rows with the SDK's simplified names give the same records; groups of 8, 5, 3, 2, 6 and 4 cells give 5, 5, 3, 2, 5 and 4 selected, the last the sparsely spiny group (D-087), and with the draft's two-class map those 4 cells have no class; the acquisition record of canned NeuronReconstruction and SubImage rows (the real `reconstruction_info`, `list_images` and `plane_table` reached through a substituted `api_query`) reads $\Delta z$, the pixel size, the usable and failed planes, the median width; (b) reference: the selection equals an independent re-implementation (sort, `default_rng([seed, crc32(key)]).permutation`, first n), at two seeds and group sizes; the acquisition summary's counts by hand; (c) invariants: the pool's row order and an unrelated group's membership do not change a group's selection; with `require_match` the groups are drawn over the matching cells only; (d) contract: the CSV round trip of pool, acquisition and selection tables; a key outside the columns refused; the summary's keys; `SurveyConfig`'s refusals; the survey section in neither signature; (e) determinism: two runs of `select` give identical files; another seed changes a group of more than 5, a group of 3 is taken whole whatever the seed; (f) edge cases: an empty pool, a required field missing (ValueError naming the keys), an unmapped dendrite type, no layer, a NaN plane spacing, a failed query, a cell with two pixel sizes, `require_match` without acquisition records; `check` interrupted and resumed, and `--fresh` (in-process, the API substituted); the CLI's `pool` (from a CSV with the SDK's names; with a hand-written `--config-json` mapping two classes, the sparsely spiny type listed as unmapped) and `select` (with `--require-match`, `--per-group`) on canned files, and the three subcommands' help
